# GridWorld — project rules

Browser FPS in a green vector-grid style (wireframe on black, retro sci-fi / Tron / CRT terminal).
`legacy/grid-arena.html` is the original single-file version (from when the game was called Grid Arena), kept as the reference for behaviour.

## Commands
- `npm run dev` — dev server (Vite)
- `npm test` — Vitest (generators, terrain, saves)
- `npm run typecheck` / `npm run build`
- `npm run serve` — the dedicated server (`server/main.mjs`: the built game + multiplayer on `PORT` 8517; see `SERWER.md`, `deploy/install.sh`)
- `npm run sim` — headless simulations in `tools/` (`tools/sim.config.ts`, files `*.sim.ts`): `tools/growth.sim.ts` runs every village of a world through village life (`SIM_WORLD`, `SIM_DAYS`, `SIM_EVERY` env vars) and prints population, food, gold, buildings, raids and the food price; `tools/civ.sim.ts` runs the civilisation layer (`SIM_PLAYERS=0,4`); `tools/materials.sim.ts` regenerates `SUROWCE.md` (the owner's Polish guide to every material, processing, build and an index of uses, straight from the data: rerun it after changing recipes, needs or items; `SUROWCE.pdf` is the same rendered to A4 through Chromium (markdown → HTML with the `markdown` Python package, then Playwright `page.pdf`): regenerate it with the .md); `tools/campaign.sim.ts` walks a player bot through the campaign (restoring the old plants it needs, finding their plans, waiting on batches, order income; time and gold by category), times it and calibrates the Chariot's size to `TARGET` 100 h alone (`PACE` = the assumptions about gold per hour, speeds, dive times; batches are in game minutes, a game hour is a real minute); `tools/balance.sim.ts` prints the economy's numbers (a crate of each old plant's goods in inputs and fuel, order margins, restoration, improvement and Chariot costs; `tools/worth.ts` values a crate); `tools/uses.sim.ts` lists goods and materials with fewer than 2 uses. Economy stage 8 (balance) is paused by the owner: a draft of changes is kept in `tools/stage8-draft.patch` (see `PLAN_GOSPODARKI.md`).
- Map editor (0.207): `editor.html` (the second page of the build, `build.rolldownOptions.input`; the menu's Map editor button opens it); see the Open world notes. Fauna gallery (0.240): `fauna.html` (the third page; the menu's Fauna gallery button).
- Dev console: ~ (Backquote) opens it in every build (`ui/console.ts`): `cash` (+10000 gold), `god` (full health + immortality, toggle), `home` (back to Gridholm), `time [hour]` (show the clock / skip ahead), `map` (whole-planet dev map, `ui/devmap.ts`: pan, zoom, click a place, a toxic fog zone or any spot to teleport via `teleportTo` in `world/level.ts`; the fog zones are scanned over the whole planet a slice a frame while it is open (`scanFog`, `fogScan`), a click on one lands at its southern edge), `tp <x> <z>`, `fly [speed]` (dev flight: `G.fly`, `flyStep` in `world/player.ts`: no gravity or collisions, W/S along the view, Space/C up/down, Shift 3x; twice the load radius, fog pushed back, horizon rings hidden, `camera.far` 600, `mayspawn` refuses), `help`, `clear`. Add commands to `COMMANDS` there.
- In a dev build, `window.__game` exposes `{ G, W, camera, scene, renderer }` for debugging; F3 toggles the perf overlay (fps, lines, triangles, draw calls).

## Rules
- **After every finished task** (committed and pushed), end the reply with the download link of the current version: `https://github.com/MrFischoeder/Gridworld/archive/refs/heads/<branch>.zip` (the branch the work was pushed to). Unzip, `npm install`, then `start-gry.bat` or `npm run dev`.
- **Version number:** with every pushed change bump `VERSION` in `src/version.ts` (and `version` in `package.json` to match): the middle number for a new feature (0.5.0 → 0.6.0), the last one for a fix (0.6.0 → 0.6.1); also set `BUILD` (date · what changed) and add an entry at the top of `CHANGELOG` in `src/data/changelog.ts` (version, date, title, player-facing notes in English; shown by the Changelog button in the main menu, `ui/changelog.ts`) — every change, feature or fix, gets described there. The number is shown next to the title in the main menu ("GRIDWORLD v0.5.0") and in the browser tab; say the new number in the reply.
- **Stack:** Vite + TypeScript (strict), three.js from npm. No UI framework: HUD and windows are plain DOM + CSS.
- **Two languages, always (the owner's standing rule since 0.190): English and Polish.** Every player-visible text (HUD, menus, dialogue, items, hints, windows, toasts, logs, maps, index.html) is written in English in the code and goes through `tl('...')` (a plain string) or TL`... ${x} ...` (a template, key = the text with `{0}`, `{1}`...) from `src/i18n.ts`, and has its Polish in `src/i18n/pl.ts` (keyed by the English; the Polish may reorder `{n}` and drop ones it does not need, must keep named `{name}`-style placeholders and HTML tags). `tests/i18n.test.ts` fails on any key without Polish. Whole sentences, not glued fragments (Polish word order differs). Proper names (people, syllable place names, Gridholm) stay as they are. Identifiers and comments in English; the changelog stays English.
- **Deterministic generation:** the whole world (dungeons, villages, terrain, ruins, object placement) comes only from seeded RNG (`core/rng.ts`: `rng`, `hash`, noise). Generators **never** call `Math.random()`. `Math.random()` is allowed only for effects and unsaved things (sparks, drone loot, NPC wandering). Same seed + same coordinates = identical result. This is the basis of saves and future multiplayer.
- **Save only player changes** (opened chests, killed bosses, unlocked doors, position, character) — never generated geometry.
- **Visual style:** grid lines `#2fe060`, black background, fog to black (on the surface by day the sky and fog turn a bright hazy green, see the game clock note). Every solid that should hide what is behind it needs a dark fill (e.g. `0x010d04`, see `fillMat()` in `world/render.ts`) with `polygonOffset`, lines on top. Lines without a fill are see-through — a bug, unless the object is meant to be openwork (drones, bosses, pickups).
- **Functional colours:** drones and hostile creatures `#ffb347` (a calm Bramble is olive `#b8b060`), bosses `#ff6a4a`, gold/chests `#ffd060`, stairwells `#5cc8ff`, XP `#9dffe0`, locked doors `#ff5a3c`; water: fresh `#2ac8b0`, murky `#8a9a4a`, toxic `#b6ff3a` (glowing).
- **Controls: desktop only for now** (pointer lock, WASD, mouse). Mobile/touch development is paused: keep the existing touch code (joystick, buttons in `ui/touch.ts`) in place, but new features do not need touch controls, touch layouts or phone testing, and mobile is not considered in design decisions until the owner says otherwise.
- **Performance:** smooth on an average laptop (phones are out of scope while mobile is paused). Measure line/triangle counts (F3) on big changes.
- **Map format:** voxel maps are ordered op lists `{op:'room'|'solid', x,y,z,w,h,d}` (later ops override earlier). The future map editor will save this format — keep it.
- **Multiplayer** (stage 1 relay in place, see the Open world notes; later up to 8 players, Node + WebSocket, authoritative server): keep world logic (`core/`, `gen/`) free of three.js and DOM, generators deterministic, game state serialisable.
- **Everything needs power and people (the owner's rule, 14 Oct 2026; new worlds only).** Every building that does something (a warehouse, a pump, a depot, a port, a works, an old plant) has a power draw and a crew. Near a village it takes the village's grid and its people; far from one (e.g. a port warehouse on the coast) it needs its own power source and its own houses and fields so people can live there, growing into a hamlet like the outposts. Without power or hands it stops or works slower. The staged plan is `PLAN_PRAD_I_LUDZIE.md` (Z1 the settlement's own buildings, Z2 ports, Z3 cargo ships between ports, Z4 crews at the old plants, Z5 power lines, Z6 commuters, Z7 defences); design new buildings to this rule.
- **Every change is designed for multiplayer (the owner's rule, it comes before the rest).** Whatever is added, changed or only considered, work out how it behaves with several players in one world before writing it, and say so in the plan and the reply: who runs it (one game as the owner / driver / authority, or each player alone), what is saved and shared (`world/share.ts` `SHARED` and the server's `FIELDS` for world state; `relay` types in `RELAY` for live events; `PeerState` for per-player state), what the others see and when (copies follow the owner, never write the saved state themselves), what happens when two players act at once (who wins, locks, seats, `spawnAuthority`), when one joins late, leaves, goes to the menu or lags, and whether `PROTOCOL` must change (then the server must be updated, say so in `SERWER.md` and the changelog). Single-player must keep working (alone you are the authority for everything). Test it with two players (two browser contexts on the dev server's relay) when it touches shared state or live play; if something stays local for now, write it down in the notes ("Not synced: ...").

## Website
- `docs/index.html` is the game's website (Polish, CRT style, self-contained); `.github/workflows/pages.yml` deploys it to GitHub Pages with the game build under `/play/` (Pages source: GitHub Actions). Screenshots in `docs/shots/` (1280×720 JPEG, taken with Playwright from the dev build with the HUD hidden). Keep its version and numbers roughly current when big features land.
- `OPIS_GRY.md` (+ `OPIS_GRY.pdf`, rendered like `SUROWCE.pdf`) is the owner's Polish description of the game: concept, story and goal, every system, what is ready and what is not, the plan, and a Polish summary of every version in order. Hand-written: add a line to its history (section 14) and touch the other sections when a big feature lands, then re-render the PDF.
- `PLAN_GOSPODARKI.md` is the agreed staged plan (Polish) for the economy overhaul from the owner's design document: decisions (simplified iron → steel without coke, Ancient Facilities with their own power hall restored in stage II, plans only for tier 2 village works, the game's power scale kept) and stages 1–8 (0.90 raw goods and intermediates → 0.99 balance). Follow it stage by stage; append new rolls, `TECHS` and `INSTALLS` at the end so existing places keep theirs; completed stages in old saves stay completed.

## Layout
- `core/` — pure: RNG/hash, voxel grids, meshing, noise.
- `gen/` — pure generators: dungeon, stairs, village, doors placement, reachability; open world: `regions` (256 m regions, POIs), `terrain` (heightfield, flattening), `roads`, `ruins`, `trees`.
- `world/` — runtime with three.js: player, doors/stairs, enemies, loot, NPCs, `level` (loading places, transitions), `overworld` (chunk streaming, structures, field enemies).
- `ui/` — DOM: HUD, minimap, `worldmap` (surface minimap + M map), backpack, dialogue, menu, input, touch.

## Open world notes
- Regions (rx, rz) span [rx*256-128, rx*256+128); region (0,0) holds Gridholm at the origin. New place types = new `PoiType` + a placement rule in `gen/regions.ts`.
- The planet (`gen/regions.ts`): the world wraps east-west after `NR` = 470 regions (`WORLD_W` ≈ 120 km, canonical x in [`X_MIN`, `X_MIN + WORLD_W`)); north and south it ends at the poles (ice caps from `POLAR_Z` = 25 km: no places, no forest, pale ice lines; an ice wall at `POLE_Z` ≈ 30 km that the player cannot pass). Generators take any coordinates but work in canonical ones: `regionInfo`/`chunkTrees`/`chunkRocks`/`regionVehicle` fold `rx`/`cx` with `wrapR`/`wrapC` and shift the result to the requested copy, so ids and save keys are the same on both sides of the seam; noise repeats through `fbm(..., px)` with scales adjusted by `wrapScale` (near Gridholm the land is practically unchanged). At runtime `keepOnPlanet` moves the player (and vehicles, loot; foes are dropped) to the other copy when crossing the seam and rebuilds the chunks. Distances to saved places use `worldDist` / `nearX` / `wrapDx`. Big static meshes use `localize()` (small local vertices, offset in the object position) so lines do not shimmer tens of km from the origin. The sun's path tilts with `latitude(z)` (`sunTilt`): high at the equator, endless dusk at the poles. Danger is capped at 8.
- Villages (`gen/regions.ts`): Gridholm (`GRIDHOLM_ID`, region 0,0) plus villages on a grid of 10x10-region cells (~2.5 km), sparser the further from Gridholm: `villageChance(d)` 0.8 → 0.22 and a least spacing `villageGap(d)` 1.4 → 5.2 km (the weaker candidate by hash gives way; `candidate` / `villageOfCell`, both cached), `SETTLED` from 6 to 40 km (`allVillages` lists them, ~80–120 per world: none in the mountains or the dead cities), named from syllables, never on the ice. Each is `generateVillage(villageSeed(world, poi), y, x, z, name, home)`: own layout, gates, residents with their own names (`Npc.town`; dialogue swaps 'Gridholm' for the town). Walls come in tiers (`WALL_TIERS` in `gen/village.ts`, `generateVillage(..., tier)`, default 0; not saved yet — village fortification will raise them): 0 Stake Fence (2 m), 1 Timber Palisade (4 m), 2 Stone Wall (7 m, the old voxel wall with towers, battlements and buttresses, `STONE_TIER`). Below stone the wall ring is voxels that only collide (`VillageMap.ops`; the mesh uses `shown`, without them) and `fenceDeco` in `world/level.ts` draws `VillageMap.fence` runs as leaning sharpened stakes, rails and scrap patches, gate frames (sign at `WALL_TIERS[t].gate`) and stilt watch platforms in the corners (`towers`, not solid). Wall shapes (`villageSides(seed, home)`: 4 / 6 / 8 / 12, Gridholm always 4; `wallPolygon(sides)` corners on the wall's middle line in plaza coordinates, `insideWall(poly, x, z, m)`): every shape fits the square footprint; octagon and dodecagon keep the square's flat N/E/S/W sides (apothem 36.5, so gates, `GATE_SLOTS`, roads' `GATE_POINT` are unchanged) and cut its corners; the regular hexagon (R 36.5, flat N/S at apothem 31.6) gets only N and S gates (`villageGates(seed, home)`; roads pass `home`). A polygon wall is voxel runs along its edges (`ringOps`, 4-connected), towers at its corners (every other one of a dodecagon, none by a gate; stilt platforms 3.6 m inside, stone towers straddling the corner), merlons on every other cell pair, fence runs along the edges broken at gates; buildings go round the plaza (`ringLayout`: 36 jittered angles, each building facing the middle, as far out as fits 2.5 m inside the wall, clear of the middle, the gate lanes, other buildings and the towers; shops first, then up to 6 houses); trees and walk cells stay inside (`blocked`, `walk`); `ladderIn` puts a polygon tower's ladder at the inner end of the face towards the middle. The maps draw each village's outline. Every tower has a ladder (`Tower.ladder`, `ladderOf` in `gen/village.ts`: on the face towards the plaza, `top` = deck height, `deck` rect, `stilts`); `world/ladders.ts` draws them (`drawLadder` in `villageDeco`), registers them per loaded village (`setLadders` / `dropLadders`), and climbs: E at the foot or top (`nearLadder`, `startClimb`; not with hands-only items) → `updateClimb` replaces `updatePlayer` (step on, hand-over-hand pulls with sway and two hands on the camera, swing onto the deck; W/S reverse, Space lets go; weapons away via the armed rule `climbing()`); a stilt deck is a floor (`ladderFloor` in `G.floor`) and every tower top has a rail (`ladderHit` in `G.obstacle`, open at the ladder). Lookout roofs over towers stand 2.5 m up (`lookout(..., post)`). Village state (`gen/town.ts`, pure, saved as `char.towns[villageId]`: `wall`, `given`, `fixed`, `hurt`): the elder's option 'fortify' (`renderFortify` in `ui/dialog.ts`) commissions the next wall tier (`FORTIFY` needs, gold, xp; `handOver` takes materials bit by bit, raising the tier when complete, then `reloadStruct` rebuilds the village; `loadVillageStruct` passes `wallOf(state)` to `generateVillage`). Power plants: `powerKind(seed)` generator / solar / wind (`POWER`: name, fix parts, wear per day, pay), `powerSite(seed)` (plaza-local, 17 m outside a W/E/S side, off the gates), `powerCondition` (100 − days since `lastFix` × wear − hurt; never-mended villages start at a hashed age), `POWER_DOWN` 25 / `POWER_LOW` 60. `world/power.ts` draws them (`drawPower`, rotors spin, status light, village lamps named 'lamp' go dark when down), `updatePower` in the main loop, `nearPower` / `repairPower` (E, takes the `fix` parts, pays, xp). Village raids (`gen/raids.ts`, pure, no state but the outcomes you fought): `raidSource` = nearest camp within `RAID.reach` 3.5 km; a timetable per village (`raidOf` / `raidsBetween`: every 2–5 days by hash, none in the first period); `raidOutcome` = the fought result (`TownState.raids[k]`) or a hashed roll against `RAID.hold[wall tier]` (0.45 / 0.7 / 0.9, minus strength); `raidHurt` adds `RAID.loss` 35 per lost raid since the plant was last mended to `powerCondition` (`plantCondition` in `world/power.ts`); `nextRaid` / `lastRaid` for the elder. `world/villageraid.ts` (runtime): within `VRAID.reach` 450 m of a loaded village, warns `RAID.warn` before, then runs a live raid (`LiveRaid`): 3 waves from the camp's direction (~110 m out), bandits with `home` = the nearest gate or the power plant (`ambush = -1`: culled at 240 m, not patrols); at a gate they drain the defences (`VRAID.drain` by wall tier), at the plant they add to `hurt` (at most `plantMax` 45 a raid); all waves dead → won (gold, xp), defences 0 → lost; leaving the village drops the raid (it is then settled by the roll). `raidHere()` makes the village unsafe (`foeRules.playerSafe`, the armed rule), `raidLine` in the tracker, console `raid` (a test raid, k 0, not recorded). Tribute: `tribute(r, wealth, wall)` in `gen/raids.ts` (60 + 45·strength + 0.3·storehouse value, −18% per wall tier, rounded to 10); the warning is a rider's demand (`pendingTribute`, `payTribute` in `world/villageraid.ts`; buttons in the elder's and the guard's panels, `data-tribute`; the demand in `raidLine`); paid = `TownState.raids[k] = 'paid'` (no raid); unvisited villages sometimes pay (`raidOutcome` 'paid'). In a live raid gunners at their gate shoot the wall (`shelling`: tracers, defences −0.35 a burst) and now and then a villager in the open (`killVillager`, `updateFallen`, `TownState.dead` times: `recentDead` villagers are missing for 2 days); a lost live raid drops the wall a tier (`reloadStruct`). Industries (`gen/industry.ts`, pure): `industryOf(world, poi, seed)` = farm / mine / oil / refinery / lumber / fishery / workshop / salvage (weights by distance from Gridholm and hills; a farm/workshop/salvage village with an oil village within 9 km becomes a refinery town 45% of the time); `INDUSTRY[k]` = site name, `pool` of goods it makes (the market picks up to 2), goods it `wants`, `fix` parts, `build` (refinery). `industrySite(seed, k)` (plaza-local, on another W/E/S side than the power plant, 11 m + half its depth out: inside the tree clearing). `siteCondition` = 100 − lost raids (`SITE.raid` 40, healing over `SITE.heal` 4 days) − bandit damage at the site (`TownState.siteHurt/siteHurtT`, same healing), reset by `siteFixed`; `production` = condition / 100, 0 for an unbuilt refinery (`siteBuilt`, `TownState.built`); `buildPlan` / `handOverBuild` (elder commission, `REFINERY_PAY`). `quote(..., prod)` makes a damaged site's goods scarcer and dearer, none while unbuilt. `world/industry.ts` draws the sites (fields with crop rows, mine adit + headframe + rails + cart, pumpjacks with nodding beams + tanks, refinery columns / tanks / flare or its scaffolded building site, sawmill, fish racks, workshops, salvage yard; a sign), `updateIndustry` animates by production, `nearSite` / `sitePrompt` / `repairSite` (E). Live raids send a third of the bandits at the site. Workers (`Worker` in `world/industry.ts`, figures with kits 'hoe' / 'pick' / 'hammer' / 'carry' from `world/rig.ts`): 3–4 per site walk between the site's work spots and work there (a stroke every ~1.2 s), one carries crates to the storehouse door; idle when the site does not work; placed relative to the localized village group (`localize`). The village's own goods (what its industry makes) pile up in its village hall (see Village halls: `gen/hall.ts` `OWN`, `stockOf`); the old industry storehouse, its tiers, shipments and convoy are gone. While the village's own goods are ≥ 75% of their cap (`glutted` in `ui/market.ts`) they sell wholesale (`WHOLESALE` 0.8, +20 stock); buying them, the elder's share and loading a haul take them out of the hall (`takeOwn`). Trade (`gen/market.ts`, pure): goods are items of type 'good' (15 `GOODS`, `GOOD_INFO` base prices, raw/made; stack 5, bulky `BULK`); `profileOf(world, poi, seed)` = 1–2 goods a village makes (from its industry's pool) and 2 it wants (the industry's wants, fuel for a diesel generator, the rest weighted by distance from Gridholm); `quote(...)` = buy/sell prices and stock (base × make 0.6 / want 1.6 × slow drift × glut from the stock shift, ± spread); `MarketState` (`char.market`, keys `villageId:good`, `{d, t}`) is the only state: `trade` adds crates sold (+) / bought (−), shifts decay with `MARKET.half` 36 h, `tidyMarket` prunes. On the multiplayer server this state is shared = global economy. `ui/market.ts` (merchant option 'trade' in every village, wide `#dlg .panel.wide` table): buys into the backpack then trunks of owned vehicles within 90 m (`stores`), sells from them, one crate at a time so each moves the price; `char.ledger[villageId]` records prices seen (best price elsewhere per good, distance, age); hearsay names the 2 nearest unseen markets within 9 km and what they want. Delivery contracts (`gen/contracts.ts`, pure): `offersAt(world, poi, seed, now)` = `CONTRACT.perVillage` 2 offers per posting (`CONTRACT.period` 12 h, fixed at the posting's start): supply (a good the village wants, n crates, pay = its sell price × `premium` 1.35) or haul (a good it makes to a village 2–9 km off, preferring one that wants it; pay per crate by distance and danger; deposit = n × buy price). `payFor` (a finished haul returns the deposit). `ui/contracts.ts` (merchant option 'contracts'): `char.contracts` (max 3) and `char.taken` offer ids; taking a haul pays the deposit and loads the crates trunks-first (`putAway(..., true)` from `ui/market.ts`, whose `stores` / `carried` / `takeFrom` take a place); handing over at the destination's store pays per crate and moves its market (`trade`); `updateContracts` fails overdue ones; `contractLines` in the tracker, `contractMarkers` in `questMarkers` (compass, maps). NPC caravans (`gen/caravans.ts`, pure, no state): every road edge has a timetable (`CARAVAN`: period 6–16 h by hash, 25% of departures skipped, departures alternate a→b / b→a, speed 7 m per game minute along `edgePath`), `caravanOf(world, edge, k)` (cargo: what home makes, preferably what the destination wants; 1–3 wagons), `departures`, `onRoad`, `caravanS`; `caravanShift` (departure −n, arrival +n, × `weight` 0.25, decaying with `MARKET.half`) feeds `quote` (`caravans` flag), `lastArrival` feeds the merchant's news. a caravan is a convoy (`CONVOY`: gun jeep, Mastodon truck with the cargo 12–30 crates, gun jeep; `back` offsets 0 / 8.5 / 17 m used by `caravanS`). `world/caravans.ts` draws the convoy (`convoyModel` from `world/vehicles.ts`: body, wheels, roof cannon; per-vehicle boxes `SIZE`; the jeeps' guns `aimGun`/`CONVOY_GUN` turn to the nearest raid bandit or, with none left, a barricade within 30 m, and fire: tracer, noise, `shootBandit` without bounty) on `regionRoads` near the player (`updateCaravans` in the main loop, rescans twice a second, keeps to the right, pitch from the terrain), `caravanHit` in `G.obstacle` plus a shove, `nearCaravan`; `ui/caravan.ts` (E): route, cargo, arrival time, buy crates at the midpoint of the two markets' prices (`char.caravans[id]` = crates bought). Raids (`world/caravans.ts`, runtime only: `Live` per caravan: `delay`, `hp`, `raid` bandits, `rolled`): a caravan coming within 160 m rolls once (`CARAVAN_RAID`: 12% + 4% per danger, from danger 1.2, through `mayspawn`); `startRaid` spawns 3+ bandits beside the lead wagon (`sight` 60) and a roadblock 16 m ahead (`placeRoadblock`); the convoy also halts while a barricade stands within 9 m ahead of it; while any are within 45 m the caravan halts (`delay` grows) and loses `drain` hp per bandit per second; at 0 it is plundered (`char.caravans['lost:<id>']`, never drawn again, nothing to buy); when the raid is over with at least half the bandits killed it is saved (gold, xp, `saved:<id>` → 30% off its cargo). Escort (`char.escort`, `takeEscort` from the drover's panel while the caravan has done < 35% of its road, pay `escortPay`): a guaranteed raid past 30% of the road, a second one (50%) past 72%; `escortTick` pays on arrival if you are within 400 m of the destination, fails if the caravan is plundered or you stay 350 m+ away for a minute; `escortLine` in the quest tracker. Maps: `mapWagons` (minimap and map, gold = your escort, red rim = raided), the big map draws every caravan on roads between known villages from the timetable (`caravanPos`, at most 2 new road paths per frame via `pathKnown`). The hero's house (Gridholm only): the house at plaza (3, 43) is `YOUR HOUSE` (`Building.mine`; the sign reads FOR SALE and the door is padlocked until the elder sells it: option 'house', `renderHouse` in `ui/dialog.ts`, `HOUSE_PRICE` 750 in `data/npcs.ts`, `char.houses` village ids; saves that already used the home chest get it free; the chest, bed and waking in your bed need it), `VillageMap.house` holds its bed and chest (world); `world/home.ts`: E at the chest opens the transfer window on `char.containers['home:chest']` (`HOME_CHEST_SLOTS` 24), E at the bed sleeps (`sleepSpan` / `SLEEP` in `data/survival.ts`: from 20:00 to 06:00 until 07:00 with full HP, else a 2 h nap; kcal/water/stomach run on at the sleeping rate); recall to Gridholm arrives by the bed (`Arrival` tavern `bed`); death wakes you in your cryo-pod (see the story). Only Gridholm (`VillageMap.home`) has Kuba's yard for now; every village has a notice board. Safe zones, holstered weapons and the no-spawn margins apply around every village (`villageContaining`, `villageDist`). The Recall Beacon takes you to the nearest village you have explored (`toVillage`); death (`toVillage('death')` with no id) wakes you in your cryo-pod aboard the crashed ship; console `home` = Gridholm. Future multiplayer: villages (and later crash sites) are the spawn points.
- Wall-walk and defence works: `WALL_TIERS[t].walk` (palisade 3.3 m, stone 5.6 m; none behind a stake fence). `generateVillage` gives `VillageMap.walkway` (`WalkRun`: the fence runs on the wall's middle line trimmed clear of corner towers (4.5 m) and a stone wall's gate towers, `nx/nz` into the village, deck `WALK.in0`..`in1` 0.5–1.7 m inwards), `walkLadders` (`Ladder.walk`: one per run, two on runs > 28 m, nudged to free ground by `clear`) and `mounts` (`Mount`, turret spots on the wall top: by the gates first, then run middles, `MOUNTS_MAX` 6), all from `wallWalk` in `gen/village.ts`. `world/walkways.ts`: `setWalkways` / `dropWalkways`, `walkFloor` in `G.floor`, `walkHit` (inner rail open at ladders, end rails) in `G.obstacle`; ladders register with the towers' (`ladderHit` skips `walk` ones); `walkwayDeco` in `world/level.ts` draws deck, planks, posts with knee braces, rail and toe board. A palisade's stakes stay 3.2–4 m so you shoot over them from the walk. Defence works (`gen/town.ts` `WORKS`, `workPlan` / `handOverWork`, `TownState.works` / `wgiven`, `worksOf`): 'turret' (Turret Kit + circuits + wire + scrap, up to the village's mounts; `BASE_ITEMS` no longer hides the kit), 'siteGuard' and 'plantGuard' (sandbag barricades); the elder's fortify panel lists them (`defenceText`, `defenceRows`, `giveWork`, `data-work`) and `reloadStruct` rebuilds. `world/wallguns.ts` (runtime; loads through `villageHooks` in `world/overworld.ts` to keep overworld free of the player/enemies import chain): a braced platform with a steel ring, ammo box and twin-barrel head per turret, targets hostile `foes()` within `WALL_GUN.range` 60 m in sight (`rayWorld`), fires via `damageFoe`; idle it sweeps outwards. `world/siteguards.ts` `drawGuards` (with the village group): sandbag walls 3 courses high round the site / plant (open towards the village), steel hedgehogs and spiked timber outside, `guardHit` in `G.obstacle`. `GUARDED`: bandit damage at a guarded site/plant in a live raid ×0.35, a lost raid's damage ×0.5 (`siteCondition`, `raidHurt`).
- Economy plan stage 1 (0.90, `PLAN_GOSPODARKI.md`): new goods clay / limestone / lead (raw) and lumber (made) come as a village's optional third make (`IndustrySpec.extra` {goods, chance}: farm clay 25, mine limestone / lead 60, lumber lumber 65, fishery clay 40; `profileOf` rolls it with `hash(seed, 0x5e7a1)` after the usual picks, and wants are picked from `WANT_POOL` = the goods before 0.90, so old villages keep their makes and wants); new processed goods iron, bricks, cement, chems (Industrial Chemicals), aluminium, leadbar, batteries, made for now by the existing works (Smelter iron / lead, Glassworks bricks / cement, Chemical Works chems from crude + salt + sulfur, Alloy Foundry aluminium from bauxite, Electronics Shop batteries). Recipe inputs are `Stuff` = `Good | Rare` (`gen/plants.ts`), so works take rare crates. Tests: every raw good is dug (pools, extras, rares), every recipe pays ≥ 1.2× (rares valued by `RARE_VALUE`).
- Economy plan stage 2 (0.91): `PLANTS` has 16 kinds (`PLANT_KINDS` in the elder's order: tier 1 sawmill, brickworks, cementworks, smelter (iron / copper ingots / lead), glassworks, wiremill, refinery, textile; tier 2 with `PlantSpec.tech`: steelworks 'furnace' (iron + coal + limestone → steel), chemworks 'chemistry' (propellant first, then chems), polymer 'chemistry' (crude + chems → 2 plastic), alworks 'aluminium' (bauxite → aluminium, `DRAW` 120), batteryworks 'batteries', electronics 'circuits', machineshop 'forging', foundry 'alloys' (steel + aluminium + nickel → alloy, now named Advanced Alloy)); `plantProblem(s, k, known)` refuses a tier 2 works without its plans (works built before stay); `fixPlant` (in `loadChar`) resets a recipe index that no longer exists; the elder's works panel groups 'Works anyone can build' / 'Works that want the old plans'. New techs at the end of `TECHS`: aluminium (2), batteries (2), alloys (3), listed in `VILLAGE_TECHS`. Farm crops flax → `fibre`, sheep → `wool` (market goods, raw); `drawFarms` draws flax rows and a fold with sheep. `world/works.ts` draws every kind (open saw shed, ring kiln, rotary kiln and silos, brick mill, converter and hall, reactors and column, potroom with vents, casting shed and acid tanks). Bricks / cement / lumber are in the needs of the new works, the smelter, glassworks, the Stone Wall (`FORTIFY[1]`), the coal plant and the reactor.
- Economy plan stage 3 (0.92): the industry site draws power (`SITE_KW` in `gen/energy.ts`: farm 3, fishery 3, lumber 8, oil 10, salvage 10, mine 12, workshop 12, refinery 30; `siteKw` 0 while a refinery is unbuilt); `balance` feeds the village, then the farms, then the site (`Balance.site` / `sitePowered`), then the works; `sitePower` = the mean `sitePowered` over the last day (12 samples, cached per game hour and per power state); `production` in `gen/industry.ts` × (`SITE_UNPOWERED` 0.5 + 0.5 × `sitePower`). The villagers patch their own plant (owner's choice): wear alone never takes it below `PATCHED` 60 (`powerCondition`), a lost raid's damage fades over `PLANT_HEAL` 4 days (`raidHurt`); `BASE_KW` 55 / 55 / 52. Shown in the elder's power text and the terminal's POWER tab (INDUSTRY).
- Economy plan stage 4 (0.93): the Ancient Facility model on the enrichment plant and the chip foundry (`gen/installs.ts`): `INSTALL_DRAW` (uranium 200, chips 100 kW), a power hall back with `HALL_STAGE` 2 (`hallReady`), `HALL_SETS` coal boiler 120 kW / diesel sets 100 kW / the plant's own reactor 250 kW on `nfuel` (uranium only, `hallSets`), `InstallState.pw` (fuel per set, burnt only per batch: batch / burn), `hallPick` (one set if enough, else coal + diesel together), `hallKw`, `fuelHall`, `canRun`; `runInstall` steps batch by batch while `canRun`. `INSTALL_WORK` inputs: uranium + chems → nfuel; glass + copperbar + chems + rareearth → microchip. Stages: I steel / cement / parts, II cable / boards / parts / chems (+ glass at the foundry) and the hall, III as before. `ui/install.ts`: `screen` (the plant's terminal block: POWER x/y kW, the hopper, PRODUCTION, NEXT BATCH or why it waits) and `hall` (the sets, their bunkers, `data-ins="fuel"`). `world/installs.ts` `HALLS` / `drawHall` (a shell, then a generator house with a stack, coal bin and fuel tank; walls collide).
- Economy plan stage 5, part 1 (0.94): the owner's rule: the player supplies every input of the Ancient plants (nothing arrives on its own). New installations appended to `INSTALLS`: 'propellant' Old Propellant Plant (10–18 km, r 30; `INSTALL_WORK` 3 fuel + chems + sulfur → `n` 3 propellant per 180 min, bay 30; draw 120; stage III tech 'propellant') and 'battery' Old Battery Plant (14–22 km, r 30; lithium + nickel + copperbar + chems → `powercell` per 240 min; draw 150; stage III tech 'powercells', appended to `TECHS` and `VILLAGE_TECHS`). `InstallWork.n` = crates per batch. The Chemical Works no longer blends propellant. `powercell` (Power Cells, off-market good); cell orders in `gen/contracts.ts` (`CELLS`, `cellBuyer` = a salvage village ≥ 5 km out, `cellVillages`, `cellOrder`, part of `specialOrders`; `Cargo` / `offMarket` include it; `BUYERS.battery` in `ui/install.ts`; the terminal's 'Salvagers' row). `world/installs.ts`: `drawPropellant` (mixing house, three spheres on legs, two columns, a flare stack lit when done, pipe racks) and `drawBattery` (sawtooth-roofed cell hall with glazed faces, electrolyte tanks, a brine basin); `DESKS` / `HALLS` for both; shared `cylAt` / `sphereAt`.
- Economy plan stage 5, part 2 (0.95): every installation stands `INSTALL_SCALE` 2× its plans (`gen/installs.ts`): sites are still searched at the plan's `INSTALLS.r` (so none moved), `InstallSite.r` = plan r × scale (the bare ground, `inInstall`, `installAt`, console `sites`); `world/installs.ts` draws in plan units (`hOf` gives the ground in plan units), scales the plant's group by `SC` and adds the desk at human size (`drawDesk(hb, ...)`, not scaled), `walls` / `rings` stay in plan units (`installHit` divides by `SC`; `nearInstallDesk` uses the desk × `SC`). New installations appended: 'optical' Old Optical Works (14–24 km; glass 2 + rareearth + chems → `sensor`, 130 kW, tech 'sensors') and 'alloy' Old Alloy Complex (16–26 km, r 32; steel 2 + aluminium + nickel → `ancalloy`, 180 kW, tech 'ancmetal'); `INSTALL_MORE` = further recipes (alloy: clay 3 + aluminium + chems → 2 `ceramics`), `installWorks`, `workOf(k, s)` (`InstallState.rec`), `setInstallRec` (only with an empty bay); the desk's 'What it makes' buttons (`data-ins="rec"`). New off-market goods `sensor`, `ancalloy`, `ceramics`; techs 'sensors' (3), 'ancmetal' (4) appended. `drawOptical` (glass-roofed grinding halls, crystal tower, kilns, stack) and `drawAlloy` (casting hall, arc furnaces with electrode gantries, twin stacks, slag heaps, conveyor).
- Economy plan stage 5, part 3 (0.96): 'precision' Old Precision Works (18–28 km, r 30; steel 2 + ancalloy + microchip → `precision` Precision Components per 300 min, 160 kW, tech 'precision') and 'robotics' Old Robotics Plant (20–28 km, r 34; microchip + sensor + precision + powercell → `automation` Automation Units per 360 min, hopper 20, bay 10, 200 kW: the boiler and the diesel sets together or a reactor), appended to `INSTALLS`; `installSites` keeps installations from index 7 on off the ice (|z| < `POLAR_Z` − 2 km; the older ones keep their places). Techs 'precision' and 'automation' (tier 4) appended to `TECHS` and `VILLAGE_TECHS`; off-market goods `precision`, `automation`. `world/installs.ts`: `beam(pb, a, b, w, c)` (a square prism between two points), `drawPrecision` (barrel-vaulted machine hall with rows of machine tools, lattice test tower, white measuring dome, stack), `robotArm` and `drawRobotics` (arched assembly hall with a great door and a line inside, gantry yard with bridges from stage 2, three giant arms raised when restored (the first a 'spin' group that turns), a walled test arena with a walker frozen mid-step, a control tower).
- Economy plan stage 6 (0.97, the components put to use): `gen/improve.ts` (pure) `IMPROVE` village improvements, one-off, built from the hall's stock through the elder's option 'improve' (`ui/improve.ts` `improveHTML` / `improveClick`, `data-imp`, trust `TRUST.improve` 12; `TownState.imp` / `igiven`, `improvePlan` / `handOverImprove`): 'bank' Battery Bank (`bankKw`: `BANK.back` 0.5 of the renewables' shortfall below their rating, at most `BANK.kw` 60; `renewables` in `gen/energy.ts`, `Balance.bank`; drawn as cell racks by the plant in `drawPower`), 'lamps' Battery Lamps (`villageKw` 25 → 15 replaces `VILLAGE_KW` in `balance` and the UI; lamps stay lit when the plant is down, `updatePower`), 'automation' Automated Site (`autoMult` ×1.5 and `autoStaffing` ≥ 1 in `production`), 'sights' Sensor Sights (`SIGHTS` range ×1.5, rate ×0.65 in `world/wallguns.ts`), 'armour' Armoured Wall (needs wall tier 2; `armourHold` +0.06 in `raidOutcome`, `armourDrain` ×0.6 in the live raid). `HALL_SETS` appended 'powercell' Cell racks (160 kW, 480 min a crate, bunker 12); `hallPick` tries one set, then the first pair that gives the draw, then all. Radar v2: `RADAR_UP` (sensor 4 + microchip 2 + cable 6 → `radarRange` 20 km; `InstallState.up` / `upgiven`, `radarUpPlan` / `handOverRadarUp`, the desk's `data-ins="up"`; `radarPlaces(world, s, r)`). Items `drivetrain` (Precision Drivetrain, in `ENGINE_UPGRADES`: `engineBoost` ×1.2 / ×1.25, `hurtEngine` ×0.5; order tech 'precision') and `scanner` (Sensor Compass, a tool: shows the compass strip and marks the nearest unrestored installation and the nearest found fog within `SCAN_R` 30 km; order tech 'sensors'). `PLANT_LEVELS[3]` needs 2 precision. Terminal: 'Improvements' (VILLAGE), 'Of it, battery bank' (POWER).
- Economy plan stage 7 (0.98, the Chariot as the last buyer): `gen/shuttle.ts` `STAGES` now want the old plants' goods (`ANCIENT_GOODS` / `AncientGood`: microchip, sensor, ancalloy, ceramics, precision, powercell; `ChariotGood` = `Good | AncientGood`) and a sixth stage 'power' (Power System: powercell + microchip + cable) before 'fuel'. `ShuttleState.done` (stages finished for good; `giveToStage` marks them and refuses more) and `v`; `LEGACY_NEEDS` = the needs before 0.98; `fixShuttle` (once, in `loadChar`) marks the stages complete under them as done and returns the crates the unfinished stages no longer take, which `loadChar` puts in Gridholm's hold. `drawShuttle` in `world/hangar.ts` adds cell racks beside the plinth, a cable into the belly and a lit bus along the fuselage once 'power' is done. `gen/campaign.ts`: `Need` includes `AncientGood`, `CHARIOT_WEIGHTS` has 6 entries, the machine hall's second good from `PROC2` (with aluminium, batteries, chems), the heart adds an old plant's good (`ANCIENT`, 1 + danger / 3); `CHARIOT_SCALE` 0.7 (tools/campaign.sim.ts prices the old plants' goods by their inputs + `PACE.plantTrip`; ~80 h alone, ~50 for 2, ~33 for 4).
- Processing economy: raw goods come from each village's land (its industry's `pool`; mines and fisheries also dig `sand`), made goods as before, and 9 processed goods (`GOOD_INFO[g].proc`, `PROCESSED`: steel, copperbar, plastic, glass, cable, boards, parts, alloy, propellant) that no industry makes and that pay well over their inputs (test: every recipe ≥ 1.2× at base prices, every input dug or made). Farms have `fertility(world, poi, seed)` 0.6–1.6 (`gen/industry.ts`; `production` is multiplied by it, so rich fields fill the storehouse faster and sell food cheaper and in bulk: `quote` divides the make factor by √prod above 1). Works (`gen/plants.ts`, pure): `PLANTS` (smelter, refinery, glassworks, wiremill, electronics, machineshop, foundry, chemworks: recipes, `batch` minutes, build `needs`, `fee`, xp), `PLANT_SLOTS` 2 per village, any village any kind; `TownState.plants` (`PlantState` {k, rec, inp, out, t}) and `pbuild` (the one going up); `runPlant` settles batches by game time (one per batch while fed and the bay has room; idle time does not bank), `feed` / `collect` / `setRecipe`, `HOPPER` 40, `OUT_CAP` 40; `startPlant` / `handOverPlant` (materials bit by bit, the fee paid at the end). `plantSite(seed, industry, slot)` = the W/E/S side with neither the power plant nor the industry site. The elder's option 'works' (`renderWorksPanel` in `ui/dialog.ts`: the land's goods and fields, `data-pnew` to start one, `data-pgive` to hand over / pay). `world/works.ts` draws each kind (blast furnace, columns and flare, kiln dome, wire drums, electronics block with mast and dish, sawtooth machine hall with a crane, foundry with crucible gantry, spherical tanks), a hopper and an output pallet, staked plots and the building site; `updateWorks` shows the glow while it runs; `nearWorks` / `worksPrompt`, E → `ui/works.ts` (pick the recipe, load inputs from the backpack and trunks by the village via `carried` / `takeFrom`, collect into the trunks first).
- Power (`gen/energy.ts`, pure): works need electricity. `balance(world, poi, seed, state, t)` = made (`baseKw`: the village's own plant, `BASE_KW` by `powerKind` × condition, solar by `daylight`, wind by `windAt(seed, t)`; 0 once down) + stations; the village takes `VILLAGE_KW` 25; the rest powers the works in build order while each has work to do (`DRAW` kW per kind; `powered[]`). Stations (`STATIONS`: Solar Farm 70 kW by day, Wind Farm 80 × wind, Coal Power Station 140 while its bunker has coal (a crate per 2 h), Diesel Generator Bank 110 on fuel (a canister per 2.5 h), Small Reactor 250 on fuel rods (a crate per 4 days, see the installations); `STATION_SLOTS` 2, `BUNKER` 30) are `TownState.stations` (`StationState` {k, on, fuel, t}: `fuelAt`, `loadBunker`, `switchStation`), built through the same `pbuild` commission as works (`isStation`, `specOf` in `gen/plants.ts`); `stationSite(seed, industry, slot)` = the far end of the power plant's side (0) or the industry site's side (1). `runPlant(p, now, pw)` / `feed` / `collect` / `setRecipe` take `pw` = `poweredAt(...)` (a batch without power at its end passes idle); `powerOf(w)` in `world/works.ts`. The elder's works panel shows the balance (`powerText`) and offers stations first. `world/stations.ts` draws them (panel rows and an inverter; three turbines with rotors turning with the wind; boiler house, cooling tower, stack, coal heap and conveyor; generator containers, stacks and a fuel tank), a lamp while it makes power, `nearStation` / `stationPrompt`; E → `ui/stations.ts` (output, the balance, the works' power, load the bunker, switch on/off).
- Village life (`gen/growth.ts`, pure; **not wired into the game yet**, tuned with `npm run sim`): `LifeState` per village (pop, food, gold, guards, happy, `built` counts of house / farm / works / granary / barracks / market, one `building` at a time, wall tier, `focus`, `log`), `LifePlace` (seed, danger = `ringDanger` of the distance from Gridholm, fertility, `foodIndustry`), `LifeMarket` multipliers. `stepLife` = one game hour: farms feed (`food` × staffing × fertility), jobless forage, everyone eats `LIFE.eat`; works earn gold (each further one `WORKS_FALL` less), upkeep × `sprawl` (1 + 0.025 per building: the soft cap on growth) + guard wages; food traded daily (sell beyond 5 days' need, buy down to 3); logistic growth to the housing by mood, starvation, leaving when unhappy, `minPop` floor; raids rolled from `hash(seed, day)` (`raidOdds`: chance by danger × wealth, strength vs wall/guards/mood); the governor (`decide`, every `LIFE.decideEvery` h) picks by needs and focus and only builds what pays (`pays`), `shrink` pulls down what does not (or ploughs fields under in a food glut). `runLife` catches a village up. ~1.2 µs per village-hour. Findings so far: worlds grow ~7× in ~9 months and level off (~33k people over ~130 villages), no village dies out; the world's food ends in a glut (price at its floor) until real inter-village trade and player demand come in.
- Civilisation layer (`gen/civ.ts`, pure, on top of village life; **not in the game yet**): `MAT` (28 materials incl. new rares bauxite / sulfur / lithium / rareearth / uranium, processed aluminium / batteries, exotic `relic`), `RECIPES`, `TIERS` Settlement → Town → City → Metropolis (housing `cap` via `LifeState.popCap`, `rise` goods, daily `use` per 100 people, works allowed), `WORKS` (batches, cost, upkeep, `wear` in parts/cable), `DIG`. `newCiv(seeds, players)`: villages dig their industry's goods + a rare deposit likelier further out (`rareOf`), 20 projects (`PROJECT_NAMES`) anchored by farthest-point sampling, every village joins its nearest (region), 3 stages each (the last needs relics), the Chariot (`CHARIOT_STAGES`, players only). `civDay`: 24 h of `stepLife` per village (goods factor from its digs' prices), then per village: dig (output follows the price), run works while the margin pays, buy daily use (unmet sours the mood), gather for the next tier, build a works (score: margin × own needs × inputs available × local inputs ÷ makers; a poor village's works can be paid by the region's richest, `invested`), give to its project; players (`playersDay`): purse, relics 2/day each, haul for the Chariot, relics to projects' last stages and to cities rising. One shared market (`Pool`: stock → price by `TARGET`, 0.6% leaves a day). Findings (world 12345): without players the economy runs (~65 works of 11 kinds, all processed goods have buyers) but stalls after ~1 year (119/127 still settlements, unmet use rising to ~38%), only the core region's project reaches its relic stage and 18 of 20 regions never start; with 4 players the Chariot is done by day ~230 and a regional wonder is finished, relics are far too plentiful (thousands found, dozens used).
- The campaign (`gen/campaign.ts`, pure, from the world seed; **not in the game yet**; the design direction after the economy experiments: simple fixed rules tuned to target play times, not a simulated economy): the Chariot (`gen/shuttle.ts` STAGES × `CHARIOT_SCALE` 1.4 × `CHARIOT_WEIGHTS` light → heavy), 20 wonders (`WONDER_KINDS`: name, regional perk, the part the Chariot may need; anchored by farthest-point sampling over the villages, each the nearest ruin to its village; 3 stages: clearing (timber, tools), the machine hall (2 processed goods), the heart (relics + 1 processed; `TWISTS` texts), scaled by danger) and 3 surprises (`SURPRISE`: after Chariot stages 1, 2 and 4 the Chariot stops until a wonder 7–12 / 13–20 / 21–30 km out is restored, picked by hash; `Blocker` with the crew's words and a clue: direction, km, the village it is near). The owner's intent: the surprises force the player off the main quest into side wonders (no finale attack). `visible(c, progress)` shows only what has happened (surprises met, a wonder's current stage, `blocked`, `ready`). Timing (player bot, `PACE` guesses): ~80 h alone (79–90 over 8 worlds), surprises at ~16 h, ~38–46 h and ~60–71 h, ~35 h of the 80 in the forced wonders; ~50 h for 2, ~33 h for 4; side wonders 7–20 h each. Owner's targets: 80 h alone, surprises not plannable in advance.
- The village computer: furniture `'terminal'` in the elder's hall (`B()` in `gen/village.ts`, drawn by `drawPiece` in `world/houses.ts`: desk, monitor with text lines, keyboard, case). `world/terminal.ts` `nearTerminal` (stand ~0.6 m in front of it, facing `n`) → E in `world/interact.ts` → `ui/terminal.ts` `openTerminal` (dialogue panel, `data-tt` tabs, `data-tclose`, clicks via `terminalClick` in `ui/dialog.ts`): VILLAGE (industry, site, storehouse, works, defence, raids), POWER (`balance`, sources, works' power, 24 h forecast), TRADE (contracts, recent trade from `char.market` shifts, caravans on its roads, its market), VILLAGES (explored, in the ledger or road-linked: industry, goods, wall, works), CHARIOT (stages). Read only.
- The shuttle (`gen/shuttle.ts`, pure): POI type 'hangar' (`HANGAR` rect, `HANGAR_ID` = packId(0, 0, 1), added in `baseInfo` for region 0,0; the N and E neighbour ruins keep to the side away from it), `STAGES` hull / engines / avionics / shield / power / fuel, each needing processed goods and the old plants' goods (see stage 7); `char.shuttle.given` (save), `giveToStage`, `stageRows`, `stagesDone`; the locals call it the Chariot of the Ancients (`CHARIOT`); nothing is sold at the hangar: `updateChariot` in `ui/shuttle.ts` (main loop) unloads whatever a stage still needs from your backpack and trunks within `SHUTTLE.reach` 25 m of the hangar, xp per crate, no gold. `world/hangar.ts`: `hangarOps` (voxel side and back walls, a low plinth), `drawHangar` (arched ribs and skin, back gable, slid-aside doors, gantry crane, lamps, crates, the project desk `deskOf`), the shuttle lofted by stage (bare ribs → plated hull, engine bells, lit cockpit windows, belly tiles, glowing fuel lines); `loadHangarStruct` in `world/overworld.ts`; E at the desk (`atShuttleDesk` in `world/interact.ts`) → `ui/shuttle.ts` (the stages, read only; `reloadStruct(HANGAR_ID)` when a stage completes). Gridholm's elder's lore adds `LORE_SHUTTLE`. Maps draw the hangar as an arch. The flight itself is still to come.
- The story and the opening: the game is co-op; the players are castaways, astronauts from the future whose ships came down on this planet, and only they (not the villagers) want the Chariot to fly: it is their way home. `char.intro` (false for `newChar`, true for saves from before it and for v1/v2 migrations): Play (`ui/menu.ts`) runs `playIntro` (`ui/intro.ts`, its own THREE scene rendered by `renderIntro` instead of the world in `main.ts`'s frame; `G.intro` counts in `uiOpen`; `body.intro` hides the HUD; ~24.5 s: cruise with streaming stars, captions typed out, the meteor stream from `T.alert`, the hit at `T.hit` (flash, the starboard wing tumbles off, the broken model), the tumble towards a line-globe planet, the entry from `T.entry` (nose down, plasma streaks, a grid rushing up, ALT counter), the crash flash, black 'SYSTEMS OFFLINE', '<NAME>. WAKE UP.', the world fades in; Space / Enter / click / Esc skip, Esc leaves the menu up because the pointer is free; dev `window.__introJump(s)`). The ship: `world/lander.ts` `drawLander(pb, {broken, landed})` (SV-9 Kestrel, `SHIP_NAME`: octagonal hull stations `ST`, swept low wings, twin nacelles, fin, canopy; broken = starboard wing snapped, roof gash, fin bent, engine crumpled; landed = open starboard hatch with the door on the ground, console, seats, lockers, no belly where the deck is the ground), `landerWalls` (plan segments for collision). The crash site: `gen/landing.ts` (pure) `landingSite(t)` = the first of 160 hashed candidates 260–430 m from Gridholm whose ship and furrow lie on dry gentle ground away from places (flat + 45 m), roads (18 m) and lakes (`misfit`); `inScar(t, x, z, m)` (a capsule along the furrow, 6 m wider by the ship) keeps trees, rocks and plants out (`chunkTrees`, `chunkRocks`, `chunkPlants`). `world/crashpod.ts`: `setCrash(terrain)` in `openWorld` / `dropCrash` in `closeWorld` (the broken ship, berms of the furrow `LANDING.furrow` 46 m, scorch lines, debris, the torn wing lying beside it, a blinking emergency lamp, smoke columns from the gash and the engine in `updateCrash` (0.237.1: `world/smokecolumn.ts`)), `podHit` in `G.obstacle`, `crashSpawn` (inside, facing the hatch: `loadOverworld` uses it for a 'new' arrival, or 'saved' with no `ow`, while `!char.intro`), `nearLocker` → the transfer window on `lockerBox()` ('ship:locker': 2 medkits, 2 flasks of water, 3 bread, a fire kit, a compass), `nearConsole` → `ui/logbook.ts` (the flight recorder: what happened, status, the surface scan naming the settlement's distance and direction). The welcome (`world/guide.ts`): `char.guide` (0 waiting at the ship, 1 leading, 2 done; old saves and v1/v2 migrations get 2): Wiktor (`GUIDE`, a villager-coloured `makeFigure` with a name label, not in `W.npcs` or `foes()`, so nothing can hurt him) stands at ship-local (7.5, 0.4) (`crashLanding` / `crashWorld` in `world/crashpod.ts`) facing the hatch, calling out every 10–15 s while you are within 70 m (`LINES.call`) and not talked to; he speaks only when you talk to him (E: `nearGuide` / `talkGuide` in `world/interact.ts`): the first time the greeting and the advice to take everything from the ship (`Guide.talked`, runtime only), the second time `LINES.go` and he leads (`updateGuide` in the main loop): a path to Gridholm's gate facing him (`gateToward`, now exported, and `gatePoint` from `gen/roads.ts`: 14 m out, the gate, 14 m in), `GUIDE.speed` 3.3 m/s, stops and calls when you are more than `lag` 15 m behind, on the way he names the places he passes (`sight`: ruins, wrecks, camps, the hangar within 150 m, fresh lakes within 90 m, once each, `LINES.sights` with `sideOf` 'on your left / right / ahead of us') without stopping, and the three `road` lines when he has been quiet for 12 s; his lines go through a queue (`speak`, one at a time; shouts and the arrival cut in); at the gate he says the arrival line, `guide` = 2 and he walks in and is dropped. Firing within 45 m makes him shout (`LINES.shot`). Reaching Gridholm on your own also ends it. A save mid-way spawns him 4 m ahead of you. `guideLine` (quest tracker, first) and `guideMarker` (in `questMarkers`: maps and compass). Speech: `ui/speech.ts` `say(who, text, secs)` (`#speech` at the bottom). Cryo-pods (0.106): the castaways were not the crew: the pilot `PILOT` Commander Ilse Varga died in the crash (`drawPilot` in `world/lander.ts`: slumped over the console in the left seat, helmet on the console, cracked visor; `setCrash` draws it) and the survey team slept in 4 cryo-pods (`PODS` ship-local, two aft starboard, two forward port; `drawPod(pb, i, open)`: plinth, shell, closed = a frosted door with the sleeper's outline and a cyan strip, open = the door swung about its hinge, a lit interior, vapour; `landerWalls(true)` collides them). `world/crashpod.ts`: `podOf(id)` = (id − 1) % 4 (the mp id; solo pod 0), `myPod`, `openPods` (yours and every peer's), `syncPods` (from `setCrash` / `updateCrash`: rebuilds the pods when the open set changes, small lights in the open ones), `crashSpawn(pod)` stands you in front of your pod facing the hatch. `Arrival {kind: 'pod'}` = waking in the pod: death (`toVillage('death')`: full HP, kcal ≥ 1500, water ≥ 50, toast) and `switchWorld` in main.ts use it. The intro's captions (crew in cryo-sleep, the pilot at the controls, 'The pilot did not make it. You did.') and the logbook (`ui/logbook.ts`: the pilot's last entries, no life signs, the cryo bay's status) tell it. Beyond 4 players pods are shared. Not yet: other players' ships, the ending.
- Technologies (`gen/tech.ts`, pure; the development plan: technologies come from old data carriers, not xp): `TECHS` (16: id, name, `area`, `tier` 1–4, blurb), `CARRIER_NAME` (floppy / disk / crystal by tier), `TIER_BAND` (600 m–3 km, 3–8, 8–16, 16–30 km from Gridholm). `techSites(t)` (cached per world): for each technology a hashed point in its band, then the nearest unused ruin, wreck or cave system (`regionCaves`, first mouth) within 700 m lying in the band (40 tries, band widened 15% after 20); `siteIn(t, place)`, `carrierChest(world, place, n)`. Saved: `char.tech` (tech id → game time found). `world/datacarriers.ts`: `placeCarrier` (from `loadDungeon`, both the voxel and the cave path, only depth 1 sector 0,0): a violet (`DATA_C`) pedestal beside the hashed chest of `W.chests` (by `i`), or with no chests at a hashed `W.spawnCells` cell; the carrier model and a beam while not taken; `nearCarrier` / `takeCarrier` (E in `world/interact.ts`), `spinCarrier` in the main loop, `dropCarrier` in `clearLevel`, `carrierSpot` for tests. The village computer's ARCHIVE tab (`archivePage` in `ui/terminal.ts`) lists them. Leads (`pickLead` / `leadText` in `gen/tech.ts`, `MAX_LEADS` 2, `LEAD_RANGE` 12 km): the dialogue option 'oldtech' ("Seen any old machines out there?": innkeeper, elder, dealer, guard, villager) → `askLead(vx, vz)` in `world/datacarriers.ts`: with 2 open leads (heard, not recovered) it repeats the nearest one, else tells of the nearest unheard, unrecovered site within range of the village (hashed story per place kind, distance and direction from the village) and saves it to `char.leads`; `openLeads`, `leadMarkers` (violet diamonds on the maps, held at the big map's edge, and on the compass), `leadLines` in the quest tracker. Craftsmen's orders (`data/orders.ts`, pure: `ORDERS` {tech, out, n, needs}, `VILLAGE_TECHS` = the technologies meant for the villages themselves, later; `orderState`, `placeOrder` through `craft` from `data/crafting.ts`, so big things go into empty hands): the blacksmith's option 'make' ("Make something for me.") → `ui/orders.ts` (`ordersHTML` in the dialogue panel, `data-make`, `ordersClick` in the dialogue click handler): the orders of the technologies you have, materials from the backpack, instant, free; the lost plans listed. Basic orders (`tech` '' , `known`): hatchet, pickaxe, fire kit, flask with no plans; Forged Tools gives hammer, saw, screwdriver, pliers. Crafting by hand is frozen (`CRAFTING_OPEN = false` in `data/crafting.ts`): the blacksmith's 'craft' option is hidden (`renderTalk`), a wild workbench's window shows no recipes (pack up only); `RECIPES`, `craft` and `ui/craft.ts` stay for reuse (orders go through `craft`). Not made anywhere now: medkits (shops sell them), ore crates, the EMP and relic modules (loot and shops).
- Standing (`gen/standing.ts`, pure; the development plan's simple economy: a village shares what its land makes with those who helped it): `TownState.trust` and `share {d, n}` (game day, crates taken). `TRUST` points per kind of help (wall 20, work 10, store 12, works 12, refinery 15, power 6, site 5, raid 15, contract 4, quest 5), `TRUST_TIERS` Stranger 0 / Known 25 (2 crates a day) / Friend 60 (4) / Honoured 100 (8); `addTrust` (returns the new tier or -1), `trustTier`, `shareLeft` / `useShare` (renews at game midnight). `earnTrust(villageId, why)` in `world/standing.ts` (log line, toast on a new tier) is called by `giveWorks` / `giveWork` / `giveStore` / `giveRefinery` / `giveFortify` in `ui/dialog.ts`, `repairPower`, `repairSite`, a won live raid (not the console's test raid), a fulfilled contract (`contractsClick`) and every quest reward (`reward` in `world/quests.ts`, the posting village). The elder's option 'share' → `ui/share.ts` (`shareHTML` / `shareClick`: tier, progress, crates left today, the village's `makes` goods, Take 1 / Take all out of the storehouse (`takeStore`) into the trunks first, then the backpack (`putAway`), what does not fit goes back). The terminal's VILLAGE tab shows the standing. The market stays alongside.
- Population (`gen/people.ts`, pure; the virtual population of the development plan, not wired to `gen/growth.ts`): `basePeople(seed, home)` = 40 + hash % 51 (Gridholm 70), `peopleTarget` (the base for now; farms will raise it), `TownState.people {n, t}` = one anchor, `peopleAt` eases from it to the target with `PEOPLE.tau` 3 days (closed form, nothing stepped), `losePeople` / `setPeople` re-anchor; `workersAt` = 60% of the people, `staffing` = workers / the base's (1 at the base, clamped 0.3–2) multiplies `production` in `gen/industry.ts` (so the storehouse, the market's stock and the share follow it). Losses: a villager killed in a live raid −1 (`killVillager`), a live raid lost −12% (`PEOPLE.overrun`), via `lose` in `world/villageraid.ts`. Shown in the terminal's VILLAGE tab (people, workers, staffing, growing / shrinking to) and the elder's share panel.
- Farms (`gen/farms.ts`, pure): `FARM` (max 3 a village, 15 people each × `soil(seed)` 0.7–1.3 = `farmPeople`, needs 8 logs + 6 stones, 40 gold, 60 xp), `TownState.farms` / `fgiven`, `farmPlan`, `handOverFarm` (bit by bit; on completion re-anchors the population curve first so the count does not jump, then `farms++`), `farmPlot(seed, i)` (plaza-local 12 m fields outside the wall corners, order hashed; clear of the side sites, the gates and Kuba's yard). `peopleTarget` in `gen/people.ts` adds farms × the same people per farm (repeated there to keep imports one way; a test checks they agree). `world/farms.ts` `drawFarms` (with the village: fence posts and rails, crop rows over furrows, grain or leafy by field, a scarecrow). The elder's option 'farms' → `ui/farms.ts` (`farmsHTML` / `farmsClick` / `showFarm`: people now and target, soil, the next farm's materials, handed over from the backpack and trunks by the village via `carried` / `takeFrom`; built → the dialogue closes and `reloadStruct`), trust `TRUST.farm` 8. Power and steel ploughs: `FARM.kw` 3 per plain farm, `UPGRADE` (tech 'plough', 5 scrap + 4 wire, ×1.6 yield, 8 kW; `TownState.fup` / `ugiven`, `upgradePlan`, `handOverUpgrade`, `upgradedOf`), `farmsKw`; `balance` in `gen/energy.ts` feeds the farms first (`Balance.farms`, `farmsPowered`) then the works; `farmPower` = the mean `farmsPowered` over the last day (12 samples); `farmTarget(seed, home, s, p)` = base + farm people × (plain 0.6 + 0.4p, upgraded 0.6 + 1.0p; `UNPOWERED` 0.6). The population anchor carries its target (`TownState.people.tg`): `retarget` re-anchors where the count is (no jumps), `targetNow`; `peopleAt` heads for `tg` (else the plain `peopleTarget`). `updateFarms` (main loop, every 5 s) / `syncFarmVillage` in `world/farms.ts` retarget the loaded villages with farms; the farms panel does it on a build. `drawFarms` adds a pump mast with a tank, a pipe and a steel plough on upgraded fields. Shown: the farms panel (draw, share of power over the day, the upgrade row, or that the plans are lost), the elder's power text, the terminal's POWER tab (FARMS).
- Power plant upgrades (`gen/plantup.ts`, pure): `PLANT_LEVELS` (0 as built ×1, 1 Overhauled ×1.5: 6 scrap + 4 wire + 2 logs and the plans for the plant's kind (`tech`: wind 'rotor', solar 'solar', a generator none), 2 Rebuilt with old electronics ×2: 4 circuits + 1 power core + 6 wire, 3 Automated ×2.5: microchips, see the installations), `TownState.pup` / `pupgiven`, `plantLevel`, `plantMult` (multiplies `baseKw` in `gen/energy.ts`), `levelTech`, `plantUpPlan` (rows, `plans` known), `handOverPlantUp` (nothing without the plans; bit by bit). The elder's option 'plantup' → `ui/plantup.ts` (`plantUpHTML` / `plantUpClick` / `showPlantUp`: output now and at best, the next level, materials from the backpack and trunks by the village; built → the dialogue closes, `reloadStruct`), trust `TRUST.plantup` 10. `drawPower` in `world/power.ts` shows the level: blades 22% longer per level, a third panel row (1) and battery cabinets (2), a second stack and a radiator bank (1) and a second generator container (2).
- Rare deposits (`gen/deposits.ts`, pure): `RARES` (uranium from 15 km 6%, rare earths 11 km 8%, lithium 8 km 11%, sulfur 5 km 18%, bauxite 3 km 30%, nickel 10 km 14% (rolled last so the older deposits stay); `RARE_VALUE` (what a crate is worth to the works; `isRare`); the odds grow a little further out, capped 40%), `depositOf(world, poi)` = at most one per village (the rarest that rolls, `hash(world, id, i, 0xde90)`), none at Gridholm; ~12–30 villages per world for each. New items (type 'good', not in `GOODS`, so no market: `bauxite`, `sulfur`, `lithium`, `rareearth`, `uranium`, crates in `BULK`). The share (`ui/share.ts`) adds the deposit: `RARE_SHARE` [0, 1, 2, 3] crates a day by trust tier, `rareLeft` / `useRare` (`TownState.rshare`, renews at midnight), `data-rare`, dug on demand (not from the storehouse). The terminal shows the deposit (VILLAGE row, VILLAGES list). For the later big works (uranium processing etc.).
- Great installations (`gen/installs.ts`, pure; the development plan's big projects, the uranium plant first): `INSTALLS` (k, name, blurb, distance `band`, radius `r`; 'uranium' = Old Enrichment Plant, 15–25 km, r 34), `installSites(t)` (cached per world: the first of 120 hashed spots in the band that fits `installMisfit`: no mountain (mask ≤ 0.12), water, lake within 70 m, place within flat + 60 m, road within 25 m, or more than 4 m of height difference across the radius; yaw a hashed quarter turn), `inInstall` (bare ground: `chunkTrees`, `chunkRocks`, `chunkPlants` skip it), `installAt`. Not a POI: `world/installs.ts` `updateInstalls` (main loop, every second) draws a site within 900 m and drops it past 1100 m (a group at the site, rotated by its yaw; `drawUranium`: broken perimeter fence with a gate and a lime trefoil sign, the centrifuge hall x −8..14 z −6..10 with a doorway, a collapsed corner, bare trusses and rows of centrifuges (some toppled), two hyperboloid cooling towers (one snapped), a stack, pipes on trestles), `installHit` in `G.obstacle` (hall walls, towers, stack), `dropInstalls` in `closeWorld`. `placeName` names it; the maps draw a lime hexagon once its chunk is explored. The search takes seconds, so `openWorld` starts `primeInstalls` (`world/installs.ts`: a module Worker `world/installworker.ts` runs `installSites` on a pure `Terrain` with the claims and `seedInstallSites` caches the answer); the per-frame users (`updateInstalls` (near home), the maps' hexagons, the scanner, `openInstallLeads`) take `installSitesReady(world)` (null while unknown or mid-search); `inInstall` / `installAt` return early within `NEAREST` 9.5 km of Gridholm; other callers still compute it on demand. Console `sites` (where they are, as `tp` commands). Restoration (`INSTALL_STAGES`: clearing log/stone/scrap, the centrifuge hall steel/cable/circuit, the core pcore/alloy/circuit + tech 'enrichment'; gold and xp each), `InstallState` {stage, given, inp, out, t} saved as `char.installs[k]`, `installPlan` / `handOverInstall` (bit by bit, nothing without the plans) / `runInstall` (`INSTALL_WORK`: 3 uranium → 1 `nfuel` (Nuclear Fuel Rods) per 360 game min, hopper 30, bay 10; idle time does not bank). `drawUranium(..., stage)`: cleared (no rubble, toppled centrifuges or fallen trusses, fence mended), roofed (walls whole, full trusses, sheeted roof, every centrifuge), done (glowing centrifuges, ridge and clerestory windows, a lamp); the live group is rebuilt when the stage changes (`redrawInstalls`). The control desk `DESK` (plant-local −6, −25, collides) → `nearInstallDesk` → E in `world/interact.ts` → `ui/install.ts` `openInstall` (stages, hand over from the backpack and trunks within 90 m via `carried` / `takeFrom` / `putAway`, whose `at` is any `{x, z}`; load ore, collect fuel into the trunks first; clicks via `installClick` in the dialogue handler). The fuel feeds the 'reactor' station (Small Reactor, `STATIONS.reactor`: 250 kW, `fuel` 'nfuel', `burn` 5760 min = 4 days a crate, `bunker` 4 (`bunkerOf`), `tech` 'enrichment': `plantProblem(s, k, known)` / `startPlant(..., known)` refuse it without the plans; the elder lists it disabled until then); `fuelWords(k)` names what a station burns; `world/stations.ts` draws it (domed containment, turbine hall, squat cooling tower, fence). The terminal's POWER tab lists the installations (`installRows`: stages restored, distance and direction from the village). Fuel orders (`gen/contracts.ts`, pure): `oldReactor(world, poi)` (none within `FUEL.from` 8 km of Gridholm, `FUEL.chance` 12% further out; ~8–17 a world, `reactorVillages`), `fuelOrder(world, poi, now)` = one supply contract per game day (skipped 35%): 1–3 `nfuel`, pay `FUEL.pay` 520 + 70 × `ringDanger` of the distance from Gridholm, due 3–6 days, id `fuel:<vid>:<day>`. `Contract.good` is `Cargo` = `Good | 'nfuel'` (no market move for fuel); `carried` / `takeFrom` / `putAway` take any `ItemKey`. `localOffers` lists it at the store, `boardContracts` adds the 2 nearest within `FUEL.board` 20 km; `describe` calls it a Fuel order. The plant's desk lists the 3 nearest reactor villages (`buyers` in `ui/install.ts`); the terminal marks them (VILLAGES) and shows the day's order (VILLAGE). The second installation: 'chips' = Old Chip Foundry (12–20 km, r 32; installations keep 3 km apart in `installSites`). Its stages: opening the block (log/stone/scrap), air and water (glass/cable/steel), the etching line (boards/circuit/pcore + tech 'chips'). `INSTALL_WORK[k]` = `{inp: [item, n][], out, batch, hopper, bay, what}` (chips: 2 glass + 1 copperbar → 1 `microchip` per 240 min, hopper 30 each, bay 12); `InstallState.inp` is per input (`fixInstall` in `loadChar` turns the old number into `{uranium: n}`), `batchesIn`, `loadInstall` (up to the hopper; an idle one starts its batch then). `world/installs.ts`: `drawFence` / `drawDesk` shared, `DESKS[k]` (chips −6, −23), `drawChips` (clean-room block x −14..14 z −8..6 with an air lock porch, window band, roof units, tank farm, water tower; rubble / fallen roof corner / slabbed lock / toppled tank / roofless tower until cleared, fans and a whole tower once aired, lime windows and fans when done). `ui/install.ts` loads each input (`data-insk`); the fuel buyers only at the uranium plant. Microchips are used by the Chariot's avionics, the power plant's level 3 (`PLANT_LEVELS[3]` Automated ×2.5: 4 microchip + 4 cable + 2 circuit; `drawPower` adds a control cabinet with a lime screen and a radio mast, blades stop growing past level 2) and wanted by chip orders (`gen/contracts.ts`: `chipBuyer` = a 'workshop' village ≥ `CHIPS.from` 3 km out, ~7–12 a world, `chipVillages`, `chipOrder` = one supply contract a day (skipped 45%): 1–4 `microchip`, pay `CHIPS.pay` 680 + 45 × `ringDanger`, due 3–6 days, id `chip:<vid>:<day>`; `specialOrders` = fuel + chip orders, used by `localOffers` and `boardContracts` (the 3 nearest within 20 km); `Cargo` adds 'microchip', `offMarket` guards `trade`). `BUYERS` in `ui/install.ts` lists each installation's nearest buyers; the terminal shows 'Workshops' (VILLAGE) and 'orders microchips' (VILLAGES). Installation leads: `pickInstallLead(sites, leads, found, vx, vz)` (the nearest within `INSTALL_LEAD` 16 km not heard of, `installLeadId` 'install:<k>' in `char.leads`, and not found) and `installLeadText` (hashed story per kind) in `gen/installs.ts`; `askLead` in `world/datacarriers.ts` tells an installation first, then the data carriers; found = its chunk is discovered (`installFound`); `openInstallLeads`; `leadMarkers` carry a colour `c` (violet carriers, lime installations) used by the maps and the compass; `leadLines` lists them first. The third installation: 'radar' = Old Radar Station (18–28 km, r 30; last in `INSTALLS` so the others keep their places). Stages: clearing the compound (log/stone/scrap), power and cable (cable/steel/circuit), the dish and the console (boards/circuit/alloy + tech 'radio'). No `INSTALL_WORK` entry (`INSTALL_WORK` is partial; `runInstall` / `loadInstall` / `batchesIn` do nothing for it). `radarPlaces(world, site)` = every POI within `RADAR.r` 12 km (~3–4.5 thousand, ~50–100 ms); the desk's sweep (`sweep` in `ui/install.ts`, also on completion) `discover`s their chunks and those of installations in reach. `drawRadar` in `world/installs.ts` (bunker x −12..−2 z −6..2, lattice tower at 8, 4 with a 13 m platform, guyed mast at −16, 14, generator shed at 2, 14; the dish lying on its back until done, then a group named 'spin' on the tower that `updateInstalls` turns every frame; the mast snapped and the shed roofless until wired, cable runs after); `DRAW[k]` picks the drawing; `DESKS.radar` (−6, −21); `FINISH[k]` texts; `BUYERS` is partial.
- Village halls (the owner's direction: a new settlement (`progressive`) has no store building until its warehouse is built (`hallStands` in `gen/hall.ts`): the elder keeps the stores (`SMALL_HALL.vol` 800 L; the elder's option 'stores' → `openStoresHere` in `ui/hall.ts` inside the dialogue, `data-hback`; `drawHall` draws only the staked plot, `drawSite`); the warehouse (`VEHICLE_HALL`, door 8 m) and the established villages' `HALL` are plank barns (`drawHall`: posts with knee braces, boards on both faces, a gable roof over the doors, open double leaves that collide, a plank floor, racks, a painted bay); vehicles drive in, `hallCeiling` keeps the chase camera inside and under the eaves (`WorldHooks.ceiling`); only `storable` things go in (type 'mat' / 'good', plus `turretkit` and `engine`): personal things belong in the house chest, `unloadCargo` leaves them in the trunk. Earlier: every village has one main store from the start, and its builds draw on it; steps: 1 hall and terminal, 2 builds from the stock, 3 farms making chosen crops, 4 the village shop's food from the stock (all done)): `gen/hall.ts` (pure): `HALL` (plaza-local x 2..14, z −19..−11 outside the north wall west of the gate, clear of every side site, farm plot and Kuba's yard; door 1.6 m in the south wall facing the village; `vol` 6000 L), `HALL_TERMINAL` (against the back wall, `stand` in front), `TownState.hold` (item → count), `holdVol` / `holdRoom` (litres by `BULK`), `deposit` / `withdraw`. `world/hall.ts`: `drawHall` (with the village group: plank walls, gable roof, shelving, the terminal, a VILLAGE HALL sign over the door; crates by fill in their own group, `refreshHall`, offset against the village group's `localize`), `hallHit` in `G.obstacle`, `nearHallTerminal`, `hallRect`, `floorPickups` (W.pickups on its floor). `ui/hall.ts` `openHall` (E in `world/interact.ts`): the hold (Take 1 / Take all → `putAway` backpack first), the village's own storehouse goods (read only, shared by the elder), what you have here (backpack and trunks via `stores(at)`, the floor) with Store 1 / Store all / Store everything (floor pickups first, then `takeFrom`); weapons, relics, keys and quest items are not stored; `hallClick` in the dialogue handler. The stock (`stockOf(world, poi, seed, s, now)` → `Stock` {own, prod, has, ownOf, take, takeOwn, full}) = the hold + the village's own goods: `OWN` (rate 1.2 × production a game hour split over the goods it makes, `use` 0.7 an hour out, each good capped at `cap` 60; `full` = every own good at the cap: the site stands still, workers idle), an anchor per good in `TownState.own` (hashed start 15–55% for unvisited villages; old saves' `TownState.store` carries over), `ownAt`, `settleOwn`; `take` uses the hold first, then the own goods; `takeOwn` (market, share, hauls) never sells what you stored. Every village build draws on the stock only (`ui/stock.ts`: `stockAt`, `stockHas`, `stockTake`, `hallNote`): the elder's wall, refinery, defence works, works and stations (`ui/dialog.ts`), farms and ploughs (`ui/farms.ts`), the power plant upgrades (`ui/plantup.ts`) and the blacksmith's orders (`ui/orders.ts` `ordersHTML(head, vid)` / `ordersClick(t, vid)`: materials from the stock, the result into your hands or backpack). Repairs, loading works and station bunkers, and the installations still take from your backpack and trunks. Farm crops (`gen/farms.ts`): `CROPS` (wheat → grain 4/day, carrots 4, potatoes 5, hens → `eggs` 3, cows → `milk` 3; `eggs` / `milk` are new off-market goods), `TownState.crops` per farm, `cropOf` (unset: wheat / potatoes by turns), `farmYield(seed, s)` (crates an hour by output × `soil` × `UPGRADE.mult` for the first `fup` farms; power does not enter it). `stockOf` adds the farms' outputs to the own goods (`own` = industry `makes` ∪ farm outputs; a farm-only good has no use and no hashed start, `full` looks at `makes` only); change the farms only between `settleOwn` (before) and `anchorNew` (after), as `ui/farms.ts` does for a build, an upgrade and sowing (`data-crop="i:crop"`, the village is redrawn). `drawFarms` draws rows (grain / carrots / bushier potatoes, scarecrow) or a pasture with a coop and hens or a byre and cows. The food shop (`gen/foodshop.ts`, pure): `MENU` (bread ← grain ×10 portions a crate, 8 g, `fallback` 16 g from bought flour when the hall has no grain; stew ← potatoes or carrots ×8; `eggsB` ← eggs ×12; `milkC` ← milk ×10; `cheese` ← milk ×5), `TownState.pantry` (portions cooked), `portions` / `priceOf` / `sellPortion` over a `Larder` (the stock's has/take: the hold first, then the own goods); `ui/foodshop.ts` `foodHTML` / `foodClick` (`data-food`) replace the grocer's shop (`renderShop`; `stockFor('grocer')` keeps only the flask of water). `world/industry.ts`: the storehouse shed is now a loading pallet with crates by the own goods' fill; `storeOf` / `storeWealth` read the own goods.
- House doors (`world/housedoors.ts`, runtime only): every building's door is a leaf on a hinge (`setDoors` / `dropDoors` with the village, `updateHouseDoors` in the main loop swings it), E opens/shuts it (`nearHouseDoor`, `toggleHouseDoor`; the choice holds until the next dawn 06:00 or dusk 21:00, `phase`). Shops, the hall and the tavern stand open by day; at night shops and the hall are locked, the tavern only shut; folk's houses are shut, openable by day, locked at night; the hero's house is locked until bought; from inside a building its door always opens. A leaf not fully open collides (`doorHit` in `G.obstacle`), a shut one stops rays (`doorRay` in `G.rayBlock`); NPCs pass through.
- Village houses (`Building.walls`, `HOUSE` in `gen/village.ts`): timber-framed like bases, thin walls (0.25 m) on the footprint's edge with a doorway (`doorW` x `doorH`) in the door side, not voxels; `world/houses.ts` draws them (`drawHouse`: planks, sill, posts, windows with shutters, open door, gabled shingle roof with eaves and boarded gables, chimneys, shop awnings, the hall's bell turret) and collides them (`setHouses` / `dropHouses` per loaded village, `houseHit` in `G.obstacle`, `houseRay` in `G.rayBlock`, `houseSolid` in `G.solid`, and in `npcFree`). Furniture (`Building.furniture`: `Furn {k, x0, z0, x1, z1, h, n}`, `FurnKind` counter / table / bench / stool / shelf / barrel / crate / sack / anvil / hearth / bed / desk) is laid out per role in the building's own frame (u from the back wall to the door, v across) in `B()`; `drawFurniture` in `world/houses.ts` draws it (wares on the counters by role), `setHouses` collides every piece but stools (`furnSolid`). The hero's house gets its own set (table, stool, shelf) beside the bed and chest. Gridholm's residents: Marta (tavern), Elder Maciej, Oskar (blacksmith), Zofia (store), Jan (food), Kuba (vehicle dealer), Jakub (captain of the guard), villagers from `VILLAGER_NAMES` (12; other villages 8 from `FOLK`). Every village has a guard (`NpcRole` 'guard', spear and shield on the figure, walks `Npc.route` = walk cells within 6 m of the wall, stops when you are near; option 'watch' → `renderWatch`: wall tier and `raidNews`).
- Line styles: dungeons keep the original 1 m grid on every face; surface structures use the outline style (`meshVoxels(..., outline)`: folds and edges, 2 m floor tiles, 4 m wall seams). Decorative non-voxel shapes (roofs, lookouts, gate arches, rocks) go through `PropBatch` — collision stays on voxels.
