- Roads (`gen/roads.ts`, pure): a network between villages only. `network(world)`: each village joined to its 1–3 nearest (hash; Gridholm 3) within `linkAt(v)` (`ROAD.link` 7 km near home, ~16 km far out where villages are sparse), union of the pairs. `edgePath` (cached): from the gates facing each other (`gateToward`, `gatePoint`), A* (`findWay`) on a 32 m grid (`ROAD.cell`) within `ROAD.margin` 1 km of the pair's box (retried at 2.8×): cell cost lazily per cell, mountains closed above mask 0.3 and costly below, the ice closed, ruins/camps/wrecks closed within 15 m and costly to 80 m (other villages 30 m); then string-pulled and Chaikin-rounded. `regionRoads(world, rx, rz)` lists the roads crossing a region (id `road:a:b`, `half` 2.3); `featuresIn` takes each road once and only its stretches near the rect. Drawn as a translucent shaded strip (`ROAD_FILL` in `world/overworld.ts`), trails too, no lines. `villageLinks` for maps/tests.
- Trees (`gen/trees.ts` pure, `world/trees.ts` models): candidate spots never change (`treeTrunks`, used for vehicle placement); each spot's kind comes from its own hash: pine (high ground), broad (default), and the big T-01 Ancient Twisted, T-02 Umbrella and T-03 Hollow Arch (walk-through; one collision circle per leg, `Tree.cols`). Big trees (`TREE_SPAN`) clear small ones around them, also across chunk borders, and keep off roads and places. Models are faceted tubes, crowns and canopies in a PropBatch with a far LOD.
- Water (`gen/water.ts` pure, `world/water.ts` runtime): lakes per region (`regionLakes`: a lake in ~45% of regions (70% in low ground) plus a smaller pond in ~40% (own RNG stream); never on the ice, the mountains, near places, roads or each other), each a still surface (`level`) over a bowl carved into the terrain (`lakeBed`, applied in `Terrain.exact` via `Features.lakes`) with a low lip just past the shore so water never spills; kinds mostly fresh / murky (many low valleys) / toxic (often within 180 m of ruins, rarely anywhere). Ask `Terrain.water(x, z)` → `{ level, depth, kind }`. Wells: every village well (its 2×2 voxel only collides, left out of `shown`; drawn by `drawWell` in `villageDeco`) plus wild wells (`regionWells`, ~2 regions in 5, drawn per chunk, solid). Player: wading slows, deeper than `SWIM_DEPTH` you swim at the surface with weapons away (diving later), toxic water hurts. E at water/wells drinks (fresh +8 HP, murky may hurt, toxic refused) or fills an Empty Flask (→ Flask of Clean / Murky Water; the flask is kept when drunk). Vehicles stop at water deeper than their spec `wade` (Scout 0.6 m, Mastodon 1.2 m); creatures and bandits avoid deep water. Thirst/food will build on this (see `useWater`). Rivers and seas: see below.
- Ruins are alien temples (`gen/ruins.ts`, 60 m square, `RUIN_SIZE`; placed within ±64 m of the region's middle so neighbours' grounds never meet; the crypt and stairwell keep their 1 m scale, everything round them is 1.5× the old temple: side masses u 0..14 v ±2..8 (voxels) under a body `bodyW` 9.3 × `back` 15.4, voxels under the portal pylons, ring radius 13.5, obelisks every 6 m at v ±6): a cross plan with a round inner wall and four broken arms; the crypt with the stairwell down sits behind a stepped portal between two leaning pylons that carried a great ring; an avenue of obelisks leads in. Collision and the stairwell are voxels (`ops`); the alien shapes are `RuinMap.temple` (temple-local u/v coordinates, `templeXZ`) drawn by `world/temple.ts` as PropBatch solids that enclose the voxels (`drawTemple` returns `{g, blocks}`: `blocks` = world circles of the fallen pieces without voxels, kept on the `Structure` for vehicles). Everything is rolled weathered: snapped pylons, a fallen or missing ring, broken/leaning obelisks with their tops lying by them, ragged tops, fallen blocks, rubble.
- Crash sites (POI type `wreck`, `WRECK_LEN` x `WRECK_WID` rect along x or z; ~14% of regions, ≥ 400 m from villages and 110 m from other places, `packId(rx, rz, 3)`, placed in `regionInfo`): `gen/wreck.ts` (pure) `generateWreck` gives hull-local frame (`wreckXZ`, u along the ship with the nose buried, v across), collision voxels as 2 m slices inscribed in the octagonal hull (`hullR`, `hullY`, `octHalf`), a side hatch with a vestibule and a stairwell going down towards the tail; `world/wreck.ts` `drawWreck` draws the hull (open over the hatch), nacelle with rings, fin (maybe snapped), cockpit windows, crack, hatch collar and ramp, debris. Inside (`loadDungeon` picks `gen/ship.ts` `generateShip` when the dungeon's POI is a wreck): mirrored bays along a straight spine (`SHIP_CX`): engine room with reactor drums and an elite guardian at the stern, airlock with the stairs up (`V` portal), crew cabins, side compartments, cargo hold, bridge at the bow; bulkhead and stub doors, crash debris; one level only (`hatch: null`, no sector stairs). Ship chests add salvage (scrap, electronics, rare cores, food, parts). Robots guard the wreck: `DungeonMap.guards` (own RNG stream in `generateShip`: scouts in the spine and cabins, guardians in side compartments and on the bridge, guardians + a repair drone or a sentinel in the cargo hold, an assault construct or sentinel with a repair drone in the engine room); `loadDungeon` sets the robots' env `{ indoor: true }` (no spawning, no despawning, walls and ceilings block them: `roomFor` checks the footprint up to the robot's height `ROBOTS[k].h`, so the heavy ones stay in tall rooms) and calls `spawnGuards` at the wreck's danger (≥ 2); only 2 stray drones. `updateRobots` runs underground too and moves their bolts there (`updateBolts`). Mounted defence turrets (`gen/mountedturrets.ts` `dungeonTurrets(map, space, max)`: wall / floor / ceiling, a ship keeps `MOUNTED_TURRET.wreck` 1, labyrinths 3; `world/mountedturrets.ts`): the domed head turns at `turn` 1.5 rad/s, fires once within `aim` of you after `warning` 1.3 s in sight, in bursts of `burst` 3 every `gap` 0.14 s then `pause` 2.2 s, each shot along the head with `spread` (hits if it passes within 0.45 m of you). A wreck also gets its robot crew again (`spawnGuards` with `spawnAuthority`, `setRobotEnv` indoor in `loadDungeon`; `clearLevel` clears robots).
- Angled dungeon decoration (`world/dungeondeco.ts`, from `DungeonMap.boxes` and `style`): all above head height, collision stays on voxels. Temple rooms (h ≥ 5) roll chamfered ceilings, pointed vaults over big halls, raking struts with corner squinches, or plain walls; ship corridors and rooms are always chamfered (octagonal section) with hull ribs, gaps at door frames.
- Seas (`gen/seas.ts`, pure): `seaMask(world, x, z)` 0..1 from a 2-octave fbm at ~22 km (`wrap` scales) plus a finer coast wobble (1.8 km, ±`WOBBLE`); pushed down within `SEA.clear` 4.5 km (+ `fade` 3.5 km, a blob by angle) of Gridholm and near the ice (`SEA.pole`); the threshold is per world (`seaThreshold`: the quantile of 18k samples between the ice caps so `SEA.share` 25% is sea; ~150 ms once per world). `seaSink` (in `Terrain.base` after `mountainLift`) lerps the land to a bed down to `SEA.deep` 38 m below `SEA.level` 1 m; `Terrain.water` returns `{kind: 'sea'}` where the mask is > 0 and the ground below the level (after lakes). `inSea(world, x, z, margin)` keeps off villages (160 m at the cell's centre), ruins, camps, wrecks, lakes, wells, installations (`installMisfit` 'sea'), caves and trailheads; roads: closed above mask 0.08, costly below; trees stop at the beach (`forest`). 'sea' water: salt (no drinking, no flask), swim only (`WaterKind` has 'sea'). `world/water.ts` `seaSheet` (from `buildChunk`, part of the chunk group): a translucent quad at the level, ripple dashes (shared `SEA_A` / `SEA_B`, breathing in `animateWater`), the shoreline by marching squares on the lattice. `world/sky.ts` `shapeHorizon` sinks the decorative horizon ranges towards the sea (reshaped every 250 m). Maps: worldmap tiles, area map, the dev map's whole-planet layer (`seas()` in `ui/devmap.ts`). Next: bridges / harbours, canyons later.
- Rivers (`gen/rivers.ts`, pure, computed once per world by `riversOf(world)`, ~0.4–0.8 s: `openWorld` calls it while the world loads): a drainage map of the planet between the ice caps on `RIVER.cell` 250 m cells (`drainage`): Dijkstra from every sea cell (seaMask > 0.5) outwards, 8-connected, cell cost = 0.15 + 14·a³ + 3·b² (slow noises at 3.6 / 1.1 km, so the ways wind along the noise troughs) + 14 × mountain mask, +400 for cells within `RIVER.clear` 2.6 km of Gridholm or `RIVER.village` 600 m of any village (`allVillages`; closed cells never carry a river); `par` = where a cell drains, `acc` = cells draining through it. A land cell with `acc ≥ RIVER.acc` 90 carries a river; from every mouth the main stem goes up its biggest arm, the other arms are queued as tributaries (`into` = the parent's index, ending on the parent's nearest point); chains shorter than `minCells` 6 are dropped. Points: Chaikin ×3 then `meander` (midpoint displacement by hash down to `step` 16 m); `half` 2 + 2.2·√(acc/A) (max 16 m), `depth` 1 + 0.6·√(acc/A) (max 4 m); the level = 0.8 × the running minimum of the natural ground − 0.8 m (sampled every third point via `naturalHeight`) + 0.2 × a straight fall to the end, smoothed, joined to the end level (sea level or the parent's level at the joint) over the last 10 points, never rising (`River {id, name '<X> River', x, z, lv, half, depth, into}`; ~150–200 rivers, ~1000 km a world). `RiverSeg` stretches in buckets per region (canonical column; inflated by half + `valley` 45 + 8), `riverSegsIn(world, rect)` (shifted to the copy of the planet asked for; none near Gridholm without computing), `riverNear(segs, x, z)` → `RiverHere` (d, level, half, depth, flow direction and `speed` 0.9–2.2 m/s by slope), `nearRiver(world, x, z, margin)` (no allocation; false near Gridholm without computing). `riverCarve` (in `Terrain.exact` after pads and claims, before lakes; `Features.rivers`): banks raised to level + `bank` 0.35 within half + 6 (not at sea level), the valley cut down to the bank + `side` 0.35 × distance fading over `valley`, the channel a parabola `depth` deep; a shallow `ford` 0.45 m where a road passes within half + 8. `Terrain.water` checks rivers before the sea: fresh water within `half` below the level, with `flow` [x, z] m/s (`WaterHere.flow`; `world/player.ts` adds it to the wanted velocity, fully when swimming, 35% when wading). Avoidance: ruins/wrecks `nearRiver` 110, camps 90 (`regions.ts`), lakes r·2 + 50 and wells 20 (`water.ts`), installations `r + 80` ('river'), caves 40, trails dropped if any point within 12 m; roads: +30 cost near a river (they cross at fords). Import loop: the natural heights live in `gen/heights.ts` (`naturalHeight`, which `Terrain.base` returns), and the region's forest/roughness in `regionClimate` (`gen/regions.ts`: the first two draws of the region's stream, same as `baseInfo`), so regions → rivers → heights never touches terrain or `regionInfo`; `allVillages` reads `baseInfo`. `world/water.ts` `riverSheet` (from `buildChunk`, stretches whose middle is in the chunk; skipped in the open sea): a ribbon at the level half + 0.8 wide (normals averaged per point), waterlines at ±half, three sets of ripple dashes along the flow (`RIVER_RIP`, lit in turn by `animateWater` so they seem to run). `placeName` names a river within half + 25 m. Maps: worldmap tiles (`f.rivers`), the area map draws the stretches as wide as they are, the dev map every river. Tests: `tests/rivers.test.ts`. Bridges and piers: see below; canyons later.
- Bridges (the owner's direction: bridges and harbours are built by the players; stage 3a = bridges at the fords, 3b = bridges anywhere over a river (placement like the flag), 3c = piers on the sea coast): `gen/bridges.ts` (pure): `regionFords(world, rx, rz, ground)` (cached per canonical region; shifted to the copy asked for) = every crossing of a road's polyline (`regionRoads`, not trails) with a river's middle line (`riverSegsIn`), merged within `BRIDGE.merge` 30 m per road, kept by the region holding its middle; `Ford {id 'bridge:<road id>:<river>:<stretch>', x, z, dx, dz (road direction), river, road, level, half, end = half + reach 10, g0 / g1 = the ground at the deck's ends}`. The deck (`deckY(f, s)`): flat `clear` 1.4 m above the water to `half + flat` 2, then a straight ramp to the ground at ±end; `BRIDGE.w` 5.4 m; `bridgeLocal` (s along, v across = (−dz, dx)), `deckAt`. Materials by the deck's length (`BRIDGE.per`: a log per 1.6 m, a stone per 4, nails per 2, rope per 6; ~26 logs for a 40 m deck), `bridgeRows`, `bridgeProgress`, `handOverBridge` (bit by bit; `done` = game time), `bridgeXp` 12 a metre. Saved: `char.bridges[id]` (`BridgeState {given, done?}`). `world/bridges.ts`: `updateBridges` (main loop, twice a second) draws the fords within 420 m (dropped past 520; redrawn when progress moves on by 5% or it is built: `revOf`): a site = yellow stakes at the deck's corners and lines across, piles appearing with progress and a stack of logs; built = piles every 4 m under the flat part with cross beams, a planked deck 0.25 m thick, posts every 2 m with top and mid rails; two signs (the group's +x side, i.e. −v) with the river's name. The group sits at the ford turned by atan2(dx, dz), local (x, y, z) = (−v, y, s). `bridgeDeck` (built only) / `bridgeFloor` (in `G.floor`, only from above: swim under it) / `bridgeHit` (rails, ends open; in `G.obstacle` and the vehicles' `blocked`); the vehicles' `height` takes the deck and `water` is 0 on it; caravans roll on it (`surface` in `world/caravans.ts`). E at a sign (`nearBridgeSign` → `ui/bridge.ts` `openBridge`, `bridgeClick` in the dialogue handler): the deck's length, the rows, hand over from the backpack and trunks by the site (`carried` / `takeFrom`). The ford under a built bridge stays (terrain is generated). A finished bridge keeps `BridgeState.at` [x, z, dx, dz, end] for the maps. Stage 3b, bridges anywhere: the Bridge Kit (`bridgekit`, type 'cons', Oskar `TOOL_PRICE` 80, 3 kg / 4 L; not gated by `BASES_OPEN`) → `useItem` → `startBridgePlacing` in `world/bridges.ts` (a mode like the flag's: `updateBridgePlacing` in the main loop, click / E = `confirmBridgePlacing`, right mouse / Esc = `cancelBridgePlacing`, `bridgePlacingHint` / `bridgePlacingOk` in the prompt; `aim` stops at the ground or the water surface, 70 m). `planBridge(world, x, z, px, pz, ground)` (pure): the nearest river within `PLACE.reach` 40 m, its middle line under the aim, straight across the flow, pointing away from the player; `road` ''. `bridgeProblem(world, f, others)`: no river, half > `PLACE.maxHalf` 12, at sea level (estuary), a ramp steeper than `PLACE.ramp` 0.45, another river within end + 10, a village within 120 m or another place within end + flat + blend + 6, a bridge or site (fords via `fordsNear`, and `char.bridgeSites`) within `PLACE.apart` 60 m. Confirmed: the kit is used, the plan saved to `char.bridgeSites` (id 'bridge:own:<pid>:<time>:<n>'; x as placed, drawn on the player's copy via `nearX`) with `char.bridges[id]`; the hologram draws the deck's edges, cross lines, piles and corner posts (depthTest off, red with a problem). The window (`ui/bridge.ts`) for your own unfinished site adds 'Pull up the stakes' (`data-brg="drop"`: the handed-over materials go back via `putAway`, what finds no room is left behind; the kit back; the site removed). Maps (`drawArea` in `ui/worldmap.ts`, minimap and M): finished bridges as a white bar across the river (`at`), your unfinished sites dashed gold.
- Piers (stage 3c, built by the players): `gen/piers.ts` (pure): `planPier(world, px, pz, fx, fz, ground, water, others)` → `{p, problem}`: from the player along the view (horizontal) the first 'sea' water within `PIER.look` 45 m (fresh water before it → 'Piers are for the sea'), the root `PIER.back` 3 m short of it on the beach, the length the first s in `min` 10 .. `max` 45 where the water under the head's middle is ≥ `PIER.depth` 1.8 m ('too shallow' otherwise); problems: a root higher than deck + `steep` 3 m, a place within half the length + flat + blend + `place` 25 of the pier's middle, another pier's middle within `apart` 45 + both half-lengths. `Pier {id 'pier:<pid>:<time>:<n>', x, z (root), dx, dz (out to sea), len, g0 (ground at the root), given, done?}` saved whole in `char.piers` (x as placed, drawn on the player's copy via `nearX`). The deck (`pierY`): `SEA.level + clear` 1.3, ramping from g0 over the first `ramp` 5 m; `w` 3.2, the head the last `head` 5 m `headW` 6 wide; `pierLocal`, `pierAt`. Materials: `PIER.per` (a log per 1.4 m, a stone per 5, nails per 2, rope per 4) + `fittings` (3 scrap, 2 wire), `pierRows`, `pierProgress`, `handOverPier`, `pierXp` 14 a metre. `world/piers.ts`: `updatePiers` (main loop, twice a second, within 420 m): a site = yellow stakes at the corners, lines out over the water, piles appearing with progress, a log stack; built = piles every 3 m, a planked deck, the head with 4 bollards, a lamp post with a gold lamp and a gold crate; a PIER (SITE) sign on the beach (`SIGN`, group +x side = gen's −v, like the bridges). `pierDeck` / `pierFloor` (G.floor) / `pierHit` (crate, lamp post; G.obstacle); vehicles' `height` / `water` take the deck. `nearPierSign` → `ui/pier.ts` `openPier` (`pierClick`, `data-pier`: hand over from the backpack and trunks by the root, pull up the stakes: materials back via `putAway`, the kit back); `nearPierCrate` → the transfer window on `pierCrate(p)` = `char.containers[p.id]` (`PIER.crate` 16 slots). The Pier Kit (`pierkit`, 'cons', Oskar `TOOL_PRICE` 90) → `startPierPlacing` (mode like the bridges': `updatePierPlacing` replans when the yaw or position changes, `confirmPierPlacing` / `cancelPierPlacing`, `pierPlacingHint` / `pierPlacingOk`; hologram of the deck outline, head, cross lines and piles). Maps: piers as bars with the bridges (`drawArea`). Tests: `tests/piers.test.ts`. Boats: see below.
- Lights seen from afar and lighthouses (0.204, the owner's rule: every light lights a small reach but is seen by the others from far off; a lighthouse whose turning light is seen from very far, lighting nothing near it, its speed set by the player; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server `FIELDS` + 'lighthouses'): `world/glows.ts` `drawGlows(list, show)` (fog-free additive sprites, `sizeAttenuation` false, nearest `GLOW.max` 64 within `far` 2500 m (or a glow's own `far`), one further than `GLOW.edge` 130 m drawn at that distance in its true direction so the camera's 200 m and the horizon rings (150 / 162 m, far peaks 145) do not hide it), `hideGlows`; `updateLight` turns every lamp of the light map's list (place lamps, providers, town lanterns: incl. the other players' lights and headlights) into a glow (`GLOW_C` by kind, size 0.06) once dark (`show` from `dark`, 1 below ground) and adds `addGlows` providers (non-lighting). Fix: `net/client.ts` now copies `lt` and `oh` from the snaps (they were dropped since 0.199 / 0.201, so the others never saw your light or shield). `gen/lighthouses.ts` (pure) `Lighthouse {id, x, z, y, given, done, p (seconds a turn), on, by}` in the shared `Char.lighthouses` (`SHARED` list, the server's FIELDS), `LH` (h 22, r 3.3, needs, xp 400, `periods` 3–30 s, `p0` 8, `see` 9000, `village` 150, `apart` 80, `steep` 3), `lhRows`, `lhProgress`, `handOverLh`, `beamAngle` (from the game clock: the same on every game), `flashAt` (two beams, cos^60), `lhProblem`. Item `lhkit` (Lighthouse Plans, basic order). `world/lighthouses.ts`: `updateLighthouses` (towers within 700 m: site stakes and a tower rising with the hand-over, finished: striped tapered tower, gallery and rail, glazed lantern room, red dome, a core and two beam cones in a 'spin' group), its glows (`far` LH.see, size and brightness by `flashAt` towards you), `lighthouseHit` (in `G.obstacle`), placement (`startLhPlacing` from `useItem`, `updateLhPlacing`, `confirmLhPlacing`, `cancelLhPlacing`, `lhPlacingHint`), `nearLighthouse` / `lighthousePrompt` / `openLighthouse` (the window: hand over from the backpack and trunks via `carried` / `takeFrom`, pull up the stakes, the period buttons `data-lh="p:<s>"`, light / put out; `lighthouseClick` in the dialogue handler). Tests: `tests/lighthouses.test.ts`.
- Street lamps and headlights (0.203, the owner's direction: lamps the heroes build one by one, electric (easier to keep) or oil; vehicle lights switched on and off; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `gen/streetlamps.ts` (pure) `StreetLamp {k 'electric' | 'oil', x, z (metres from the village's middle), oil, t}` in the shared `TownState.lamps`, `STREET` (reach 110, apart 4, max 30, kw 0.5, tank `cap` 2400 min, `flask` 360, `can` 1440, h 3.6, r 16 / `ro` 12), `isNight` (20:00–06:00), `nightBetween`, `oilLeft` (closed form: the tank burns only by night), `fillLamp`, `lampLit`, `streetKw` (added to the village's own needs in `balance`), `lampProblem`. Items `elampkit` / `olampkit` (cons, basic orders, `basicSmith`). `world/streetlamps.ts`: `updateStreetLamps` (main loop: lamps of villages within 450 m, redrawn when the list changes, heads and glows lit by `lampLit` with `powerUp(vid)` from world/power.ts), a light provider, placement (`startStreetPlacing` from `useItem`, `updateStreetPlacing` / `confirmStreetPlacing` (under `withTownStock`) / `cancelStreetPlacing`, `streetPlacingHint`), `nearStreetLamp` / `streetLampPrompt` / `openStreetLamp` (the window: pour a flask or a canister, take it down; `streetLampClick` in the dialogue handler). Headlights: `VehicleState.lights`, `headlightModel` (lenses and faint beams named 'headlights', also on `convoyModel` ghosts), `toggleHeadlights` (L at the wheel via `lockKey`, driver only), `refreshLights`, `headlightSpot`; the light map's cone only while they are on (`setDrivingHook`); parked own vehicles and peers' cars with lights on add a lamp ahead (main.ts provider); `PeerCar` [11] = lights (the server keeps 12 entries; an older one drops it). Tests: `tests/streetlamps.test.ts`.
- A bare start (0.202, the owner's rule: no vehicles until the mechanic comes, no electricity at first, the player builds the first farm; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `vehiclesOpen(c)` in `gen/settlement.ts` (pure of the shared `towns`: an old world, `mechanicHere` at Gridholm, or a vehicle owned / ordered); without it `syncFound` shows no abandoned vehicles, `spawnRaidersMaybe` sends no raider cars, `updateCaravans` draws no convoys and the big map shows no caravans (the timetables still move the markets). A progressive village draws no electric lamp posts (`lamps` in `world/level.ts`) until its power plant stands (the job's `reloadStruct` brings them). MP: every game reads the same shared state. Tests: `tests/vehiclesopen.test.ts`.
- Two hands (0.201, the owner's rule: a weapon in one hand, unless it is two-handed, and a light or a shield in the other; more backpack slots; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `Char.off` (one slot, personal; `loadChar` adds it and pads `inv` to `INV_SIZE` 24, was 12), slot id `o:0` (`listOf('o')`). `data/items.ts`: `OFF_HAND` (flashlight, oillamp, firebrand, woodshield, riotshield), `SHIELD` (woodshield 0.15, riotshield 0.3 of a hit), `TWO_HANDED` (blaster, smg, shotgun, rifle, spear, sledge, pickaxe, shovel, welder and `HANDS_ONLY`). `character.ts`: `offHeld`, `bothHands`, `offFree`, `shieldNow` (folded into `G.S.def` by `calcStats`), `stowOff`, `takeInOff(k)` (a whole bunch of torches, one of anything else; what the hand held goes to the backpack; a two-handed weapon goes on your back via `stowHeld`); `takeOne` / `hasItem` / `carriedKg` count the other hand. `world/light.ts`: a light burns only from the other hand (`toggleLight` takes it there), goes out when it leaves the hand or a two-handed weapon is drawn; a torch burns down in the hand (the bunch's next one is lit, then one from the backpack). Items `woodshield` (basic order 4 planks + hide, `basicSmith`; Oskar `GEAR_PRICE` 50) and `riotshield` (tech 'forging': 2 steel + plastic; 220). `world/weapons.ts` `offLooks` / `animateOff` (left-hand models: flashlight with a lit lens, lantern with a wick, torch with a flickering flame, the shields raised by `guardK` when aiming / firing / swinging), `setOffRule` (world/level.ts, like the tool rule). The backpack window: the 'Other hand' slot (`.idle` when both hands are on the weapon), Take in other hand / Into other hand / Light / put out / Put away. MP: `PeerState.oh` (the other hand's item while usable; the server's whitelist takes it, an older one drops it): a peer's figure carries a shield (`makeFigure(..., shield)`); a crew death drops the other hand too. Tests: `tests/hands.test.ts`.
- Light sources (0.200, the owner's rule: the flashlight uses batteries; other lights are torches, oil lanterns, campfires and town lanterns; below ground only now and then electric light or torches; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): items `battery` (Battery, cons, stack 20; Zofia 10, orders 2 ← scrap + circuit, 12 ← a `batteries` crate) and `firebrand` (Torch, cons, stack 10; Zofia 4, orders 4 ← log + rope; crash locker 4; both in `basicSmith` and chests). `world/light.ts`: `LightKind` 3 = torch, `FUEL` (what each burns, `LIGHT.flash.perBattery` 180 / `lamp.perOil` 360 / `torch.per` 45 min, the tool), `Char.light.torch`; L cycles 1 → 2 → 3 → 0; `flicker` breathes flame reaches (the torch's `uEye`, lamps with `flickers(k)`); `fixtureModel` (caged bulb), `torchModel` (wall bracket, flame in `flames`, `animateFlames`), `postLantern` (a town's oil lantern; `townLanterns` lights `LIGHT.lantern` 11 m from every one on the scene, surface only). `gen/darkness.ts`: `Lamp.k` electric / torch / fire / oil and `n` (a torch's facing), `UNDER_LAMPS` (labyrinth rooms 0.2 / 0.12 / 0.06 by depth, 30% of them electric, the rest `wallTorch`; tunnels × 0.35 torches; ship 0.18 electric; metro stations 35% powered, tunnel work lamps 7%, scavengers' torches 5%), `villageLanterns(map)` (two posts 5 m in from every gate, one by the door of every non-house building; drawn in `lamps` in `world/level.ts`). MP: `PeerState.lt` 3 (the server's whitelist takes it; an older one drops it), `LIGHT.peer[3]`; everything else from the seed. Tests: `tests/darkness.test.ts`.
- Darkness and light (0.199, the owner's direction: light matters, dark tunnels you cannot pass without a light, darker nights; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `world/darkshader.ts` (imported by `world/render.ts` before any material compiles) appends to three's fog chunks: every fogged material's colour × mix(1, `darkLit()`, `uDark`), `darkLit` = max(`uAmb`, your light round the eye `uEye.w`, a flashlight cone `uCone` (xyz the view, w range), `uLamps[LAMP_SLOTS 12]` camera-relative xyz + reach); positions camera-relative via `uViewInv` (the camera's world matrix); the uniforms are one shared `DARK` set given to every material by `THREE.Material.prototype.onBeforeCompile` (a fog-free material or the viewmodel's scene is never darkened). `gen/darkness.ts` (pure): `LIGHT` (flashlight range 34 / spill 4.5 / `perCell` 180 game min an Energy Cell; lamp r 13 / `perOil` 360 a Lamp Oil; headlights 52; peer lights; village lamp 16; fire 11; `slots` 12 within `near` 95), `NIGHT` {amb 0.24, cloud 0.13, from 0.45}, `surfaceDark(day, cloud)`, `UNDER` (labyrinth depth 0.42 / 0.2 / 0.07, wreck 0.12, metro 0.035, cave 0.03), `underAmb`, `dungeonLamps(map, depth)` (own hash stream: room lanterns 55 / 38 / 22% by depth, ship 35%, torches in long passages; metro: half the stations powered (3 lamps over the platform), tunnel work lamps 16% per 30 m), `caveLamps` (mouths r 15, crystals r 4.5), `nearestLamps`. `world/light.ts`: `Char.light` {on 0 / 1 flashlight / 2 oil lamp, flash, oil minutes left} (personal), `carriedLight`, `toggleLight(k)` (refills from `ammoE` / `lampoil` in the backpack), `lightKey` (L via `lockKey` in `world/interact.ts` when no lockable door is near; the backpack's Use of `flashlight` / `oillamp` / `lampoil` in `useItem`), `setPlaceLight(under, amb, lamps)` (`setLocationLook` for the surface, `loadDungeon`, `buildCave`), `addLights(provider)` (registered in main.ts: `fireSpots` (world/cooking.ts), `campFires`, `litLamps` (world/power.ts: the lit village lamps), `peerLights` (world/peers.ts)), `setDrivingHook` (headlights), `updateLight(dt)` (main.ts, before drawing: burns the light, sets the uniforms, the 'pitch dark' hint once a place, `#lightbar`), `lanternModel` (fog-free bright lantern drawn at each dungeon lamp; ships red). Items `flashlight`, `oillamp` (tools), `lampoil` (cons): Zofia (`SUPPLY_PRICE`), basic orders appended (oil lamp, 3 oil ← fuel, flashlight), `basicSmith` adds the lamp and oil, the crash locker (10 slots) an oil lamp + 2 oil. Stealth: a light on counts as 0.85 daylight in `myVis`. MP: `PeerState.lt` (1 / 2; the server's whitelist passes it, an older server drops it), drawn as a lamp at the peer; the place's lamps come from the seed, the same for everyone. Not synced: nothing else. Tests: `tests/darkness.test.ts`.
- Bigger polygon villages (0.198, the owner's rule: no square villages or camps, more room inside; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `villageSides` = 6 / 8 / 12 by hash (Gridholm 8; `Sides` keeps 4 for the unused square code paths); `VILLAGE_R` 48 (was 36.5) = the flat sides' apothem, plaza coordinates keep `VILLAGE_OFFSET` −36 (centre 36) and now run `WALL_LO` −12 .. `WALL_HI` 84; `apothem` exported; W / E gate cells at the polygon's flat sides; `ringLayout` for every village (Gridholm too: its first house gets `mine`), searched out to `VILLAGE_R` + 6, lanes / trees / walk cells bounded by `LO` / `HI`. Moved with the wall: `VILLAGE_RECT` ±50, `GATE_POINT` (−14 / 86), `HALL` z −31..−23 (`HALL_TERMINAL`), the side sites (`powerSite`, `industrySite`, `plantSite`, `stationSite`: `WALL_LO + 1 − off` / `WALL_HI + off`), `farmPlot` corners ±16 / 88, `YARD` 12 m north, `reservedZones` village rect −26..98, the worldmap outline scale; the guard's route uses `insideWall`. `openWorld` moves a vehicle standing inside a village's footprint 12 m outward (Kuba's old bays). Camps (`gen/camps.ts`): `CampMap.ring` (6–8 sides by `hash(world, id, 0xca9b)`, flat north), `inPoly`, `ringCells`; the palisade is one op per ring cell (gates: the 4 columns round the middle), tents step in from 6 m until they fit, crates and spawns inside; `loadCampStruct` takes a stake run's direction from its neighbours. Tests: `tests/shapes.test.ts`.
- The staked-out warehouse (0.197, the owner's rule: no fixed plot or WAREHOUSE SITE sign; anywhere outside the wall with room for vehicles and convoys; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `SettlementState.wh` {x, z, d} (plaza-local middle, `d` the door side 0 S / 1 E / 2 N / 3 W) and `whWait` (shared in `towns`). `buildProject(s, 'warehouse', have, at)` with every material in and no `wh` sets `whWait` and returns `wait`; `placeWarehouse(s, spot, now)` saves `wh` and starts the job. `gen/hall.ts`: `HallSpot`, `WAREHOUSE` {reach 320, apron 14, wide 14, maxCut 4.5}, `warehouseSpot(s)` (the staked spot, or the old `VEHICLE_HALL` plot for a warehouse done or going up without one, else null), `warehouseAt`, `doorDir`, `hallPoint(h, a, b)` (the hall's own frame: a across, b back wall → doors), `apronOf`; `hallSpec` carries `d` (`HALL` / `SMALL_HALL` 0), `hallTerminal` turns with it. `gen/fields.ts` `warehouseProblem(t, poi, seed, s, spot, claims)` (reach, `reservedZones` with `Zone.k` 'village' / 'warehouse' (its rect + apron), the apron clear of zones and the wall, fields and bases, places, other villages, roads, stones, water, steepness of the hall and the apron). The Survey Stake (`fieldstake`) starts it (`world/fields.ts` mode 'warehouse', preferred over a waiting field: the doors face the player, a hologram of the footprint, doorway posts, the apron and an arrow; `confirmWarehouse` under `withTownStock`). The elder's development panel shows the wait and `data-devstake`. `world/hall.ts` `drawHall` draws in the hall's frame in a group turned by `d` (nothing before it stands; `drawSite` is gone), segments and the door beam (`Hall.door`) in world coordinates. The pad (`padsOf`), the job site and `reservedZones` use `warehouseSpot`; `townLook` includes `wh` / `whWait`. Tests: `tests/warehouse.test.ts`.
- Server admins and the mail test (0.196.0; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `server/mail.mjs` (`makeMailer(env, log)` shared by main.mjs and the test: SMTP or sendmail, `SMTP_INSECURE=1` skips the certificate check, `send.verify`; `parseEnv` reads an env file like systemd; `adminsOf(env)` = `ADMINS` names lower case). `createMp(..., {admins})`: `isAdmin(acct)`; `deleteRoom` lets an admin close any room but 'main' (players told 'closed by an admin'); the `profile` reply carries `admin`. Client: `ui/mp.ts` fetches the profile for every logged-in account, `isAdmin()` adds every room to `yours` and ' · admin' to the account line. `server/mailtest.mjs` (`sudo /opt/gridworld-node/bin/node /opt/gridworld/server/mailtest.mjs you@x`, reads `/etc/gridworld.env` or `GRIDWORLD_ENV`). `deploy/install.sh` adds commented `ADMINS` / `SMTP_INSECURE` lines (appended to an older env file once). SERWER.md: the step-by-step guide. Tests: 'an admin (ADMINS) closes any player's server' in `tests/mp.test.ts`.
- Fix 0.195.1: main.ts sets `#warp` to 1 when you die and only an arrival (`arriveVia`) lifts it; `toVillage('death')` now calls `arriveVia(null)` after the crew death too, and `useChar` clears `G.trans` and the warp, so a death online no longer leaves the screen black (also after moving to another server).
- The map editor (0.207, the owner's request; plan in `PLAN_EDYTORA.md`, stage E1 done; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `editor.html` + `src/editor/` (plain DOM and canvas; `main.ts` tabs, file bar (name, New, Save, Open, Delete, Export / Import JSON, Test in the game), canvas input (left button = tool, right / middle / Space + drag pans, wheel zooms, F fits, Ctrl+S); `common.ts` `View`, `h`, fields, `History` (undo by JSON snapshots); `dungeon.ts`, `world.ts`, `village.ts` (`library()` = the game's own lists: village buildings, `POWER`, `PROJECTS`, `PLANTS`, `STATIONS`, `CROPS`, `WORKS`, lamps, lighthouse); `preview.ts` (its own three.js scene: `meshVoxels` of the expanded ops, markers); `editor.css`). It never imports `world/` (render.ts makes the game's renderer). `gen/mapfile.ts` (pure): `MAP_FORMAT` 1, `EDITOR_KEY` (localStorage keys per kind), `DungeonFile` {style 'temple' | 'ship' | 'metro', danger, drones, `pieces` (`Piece` {k, x, z, w, d, y?, h?, name?}), `marks` (`Mark` {k spawn / exit / chest / crystal / boss / guard / door, x, z, kind?}), `ops` written on save}, `PIECES` (per kind: styles, solid, tunnel deco, fixed size, `across` for passages), `expandDungeon(f)` → `DungeonMap` (ops: rooms in order, the stations' platforms and columns (`stationParts` now exported from `gen/metro.ts` and used by `generateMetro` too), solids and blockages, the exits' `stairOpsFor` raised to the room's floor (a station's long wall: the platform); portals 'V<n>' with `y0`; `MetroInfo` for a metro (stations, rails, blocks, steps, decks); deco boxes for floor-0 pieces; bosses with their room, guards, `doorCands` by the room's axis; the spawn by `standY`), `exitStairs` (the nearest wall of the room the marker stands in), `roomAt`, `dungeonProblems` (noRooms, noExit, badExit, outside, unreached via `reachable`: a metre up or down at a time), `WorldFile` {cell, w, h, `land` heights row by row, rivers, lodes, places (`PlaceKind`)}, `VillageFile` {sides, wall, items}, `readMapFile` (validates JSON), `storedMaps`. The game: `world/custommaps.ts` (`customDungeons`, `customDungeon`, `customId` negative save ids, `takeTest` / `testWaiting` for `?test=<name>`), `DungeonPos.custom`, `loadDungeon` plays `expandDungeon` of it (lighting by style, guards from the file, `custom.drones`, no mounted turrets, level `danger`, its 'V' stairs → `exitCustom`, a vanished map → back to the surface), `enterCustom(name)` in `world/level.ts` (in at the start marker or the first stairs), console `custom [name]`, main.ts takes a waiting test once playing (after the frame) and skips the opening film. Tests: `tests/mapfile.test.ts`. MP: the editor's files live in the author's browser; a test level online is the author's alone (Not synced: editor maps, until stage E5).
- Editor worlds in the game (0.208, `PLAN_EDYTORA.md` stage E2; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `gen/customworld.ts` (pure) `customSeed(name)` (FNV of the name, in `CUSTOM_SEED` [-2e9, -1e9]: negative, so `townsWorld` rules apply), `isCustomSeed`, `setCustomWorld(world, file | null)` (the registry: `CustomWorld` {f, sx / sz the start marker in the file (else its middle), `places` / `lodes` / `rivers` in game metres (canonical x; rivers unwrapped), `byRegion`, `lodeByRegion`}), `customWorld(world)`, `placesOf`, `placesIn`, `fileHeight` (Catmull-Rom over the cells, clamped to the 4 nearest; `OFF_MAP` −40 beyond the file; wraps only when the file is as wide as the planet), `customSeaMask` (`CW.sea`: 0 above 1 m, 1 some 8 m under), `customMountainMask` (`CW.mountain` from 42 m, full 80 m higher). Hooks (each a `customWorld(world)` branch, the seed's path untouched): `naturalHeight` (SEA.level + the file, on land the seed's hills × 0.4 faded in over 10 m and 0.3 × `mountainLift` by the mask; the ice as before, `ice()`), `seaMask`, `mountainMask`, `isleOf` (none), `baseInfo` (a village at the region's middle, not within 2 regions of Gridholm, named from the file; else a ruin clamped to ±64 m of the middle), `regionInfo` (`customPlaces`: camp, wreck), `allVillages`, `riversOf` (`customRivers`: Chaikin + meanders, half = w / 2, into a river drawn earlier when it ends within its half + 60 m, else the sea or a pool; `levelsOf` shared with the seed's rivers; `bucketed`), `citySites` (r 200–1200), `installSites` (one per kind named in `install`), `worldMegaliths` (designs in turn, at most `MEGALITH_COUNT`), `rawCaves` (facing downhill), `regionLode` / `startLodes` (only the file's, one a region, its `rich`), `gateNetwork` (the ancient gates sampled over the drawn grid; never throws, the first dry spot met as a fallback); the early outs near Gridholm (`riverSegsIn`, `nearRiver`, `cityAt`, `inInstall`, `installAt`, `megalithsNear`, `gatesNear`) are skipped. `Char.cw` (the file's name) → `syncCustomWorld(c)` in `world/custommaps.ts` (`customWorlds()`, registers from the editor store; false when missing: a toast, the seed's land) from main.ts after `loadChar` and in `useChar`; `primeInstalls` sends the file to the worker (`installworker.ts` registers it). Menu: `#cwRow` (`#cwPick`, `#cwPlay`: twice, `newWorld(customSeed(n), n)`; `MenuHooks.newWorld(seed, cw?)`), `worldAsked` (?world=<name> from the editor's world tab 'Test in the game' preselects it). The editor's world tab warns (`warnings`: no start, start / village in the sea). MP: see the 0.211 note (the room's maps). Tests: `tests/customworld.test.ts`.
- Editor village layouts in the game (0.209, `PLAN_EDYTORA.md` stage E3; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `gen/village.ts` `VillageLayout` {sides, wall, items (editor metres from the middle, `rot` degrees: 0 door +z / 'S', 90 'W', 180 'N', 270 'E')}, a registry by village seed (`setVillageLayout`, `villageLayout`; `villageSides` reads it first, so `villageGates`, roads, `powerSite`, the maps and the guard's route agree), `layoutFields(seed)` (the 'farm:<crop>' items in plaza coordinates). `generateVillage` with a layout: the well, the notice board and the map board at their 'b:well' / 'b:board' / 'b:map' items (`spot`, else the old 36,37 / 41,45 / 31,45; `blocked` and the outputs use them), `placeLayout` (the first 'b:tavern' / 'b:elder' / 'b:smith' / 'b:store' / 'b:food' and every 'b:house' inside the wall and clear of those before: rounded, footprint swapped for a quarter turn, at least 8 m (shops) / 5 m (houses), the label upper-cased on the sign), then `ringLayout` adds only the missing roles and houses up to `HOMES_MIN` (a village without a layout: unchanged, the same RNG stream). `farmPlot` takes the drawn field i (6–12 m half sizes). `gen/layouts.ts` `applyLayouts(c)` (from `openWorld` after `initializeSettlements`): once per town (`TownState.lay`), not for a settlement (Gridholm): `wall` = the layout's, `farms` / `crops` = its fields' crops (up to `FARM.max`). Registration: `setCustomWorld(world, f, villages)` (gen/customworld.ts) sets the layouts of the start (`start.layout` → seed `world`) and of the villages (`GamePlace.layout`, seed `villageSeed(world, packId(rx, rz, 0))`), `CustomWorld.laid` clears them again; `customVillages()` in `world/custommaps.ts` (from `syncCustomWorld`, and to the install worker). The world editor offers a layout for the start too; the village editor says what the game uses. Not yet: the layout's power plant, projects, works, stations, defences, lamps, hall, towers and gates. MP: the layouts go with the room's maps (0.211). Tests: `tests/villagelayout.test.ts`.
- Editor dungeons under a drawn world's places (0.210, `PLAN_EDYTORA.md` stage E4; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `WorldPlace.dungeon` (a dungeon file's name; the world editor offers it for ruins, wrecks and cities: `WorldTab`'s `dungeons` list, and warns when a named layout or dungeon is not saved). `gen/customworld.ts` `GamePlace.dungeon`, `placeDungeon(world, id)` (a ruin `packId(rx, rz, 1)` / a wreck `packId(rx, rz, 3)`: the first of its kind drawn in its region, as `baseInfo` / `customPlaces` place them), `cityDungeon(world, i)` (the file's cities in `citySites` order). `world/custommaps.ts` `editorDungeonAt(world, id)` (a metro entrance through `metroCity`; only when the file is in this browser). `enterDungeon` in `world/level.ts`: with one, `c.dungeon = {ruinId: the place's id, depth 1, gx 0, gz 0, custom}` and in at its start marker or 'V0'; `exitCustom` leaves through `exitToRuin` when the ruinId is a place's (not `customId(name)`), so you come out where you went in. `dungeonKey` appends '@' + the file's name for any editor level (its chests and kills apart from the place's generated level and from the same file elsewhere; the 0.207 test levels' saved state starts over once). Each metro entrance keeps its own state. MP: the dungeons a world names go with the room's maps (0.211); players under the same place share `dungeonKey`, so they meet. Tests: `tests/editordungeons.test.ts`.
- Editor maps in multiplayer (0.211, `PLAN_EDYTORA.md` stage E5, `PROTOCOL` 15): `server/mp.mjs` `cleanMaps` (an object {world, villages, dungeons}, each file an object, at most `MP.maps` 2 MB as JSON, else null), `room.maps` (a dedicated room made with `create.world` takes `create.maps`; the hosted 'lan' room takes the first hello's `maps` with its world), the `welcome` carries `maps`, `list()` rows `map` (the world's name), `save()` keeps `maps` (server.json); `mp.d.mts` `RoomMaps`. Client: `net/client.ts` `RoomMaps`, `connect(..., {create.maps, maps})`, `onRoomMaps(f)` called first in every welcome (before the shared world's hooks). `world/custommaps.ts`: the room's maps are read with `readMapFile` and registered (`setCustomWorld(world, f, villages)`; `room.dungeons` merged over the browser's in `customDungeons`, so `customDungeon` finds them), `roomWorld`, `mapsFor(c)` (the character's drawn world, its registered layouts (`CustomWorld.villages`) and the dungeons its places name, from this browser) sent by `start` in `ui/mp.ts` with Host / Join (the server uses it only when it takes the hello's world) and with Create 'in my own world'; the server list shows ' · editor world X'. The install worker takes the registered layouts (`CustomWorld.villages`). Not synced: test levels opened with Test in the game / `custom` stay the author's; a player who played the same seed before without the file keeps that game's generated caches until a reload. Tests: '(15) a room on a world from the map editor...' in `tests/mp.test.ts`; checked with two browser contexts (the joiner without files gets the same land, villages, layouts and editor level).
- Solid rocks and foes' collision (0.211.1, the owner's report: foes walked through trees, city ruins, house walls and fences and hid in walls; the player walked into rocks; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `gen/rockshape.ts` `ringReach(ring, a)` and `rockSurface(shape, dx, dz)` (the stone's top over a point: the crown inside the top ring, the faces down to the shoulder and the foot, -Infinity off it). `world/overworld.ts`: `rockTop` (a body of radius r meets the highest part under its circle; shapes cached per `Rock`), `rockBlocks` (in `treeHit`: stones taller than `ROCK_STEP` 0.6 block while the feet are below their surface − `ROCK_TOL` 0.1), `rockFloor` (in `G.floor`: a small stone always, a tall one only once you are on it, so a jump lands on top and its steep faces cannot be walked up). Foes: `foeRules.body(x, y feet, z, r)` = `foeBody` in `world/overworld.ts` (trees and rocks, `cityHit`, houses, halls, settlement yards, walkways, ladders, sandbags, installations, lighthouses, outposts, the crash ship, bases, megaliths, gates, doors, fog sites, and every voxel structure sampled on its circle at y + 0.3 and y + 1.2; not vehicles, boats or caravans); creatures (`bodyR` 0.6 × r, max 0.8), robots (0.55 × r, max 1; outdoors) and bandits (`BODY_R` 0.4) test it; `core/footstep.ts` `footStep` (pure: straight, either axis, then turned ±0.7 / ±1.4 rad; a foe standing inside something walks out unless that way is forbidden) is their step. MP: every foe's owner moves it against the same seeded geometry; nothing new is sent. Tests: `tests/rockcollision.test.ts`. Camp gates (0.211.2): `gen/camps.ts` `campGates(c)` (the two openings where x = cx crosses the ring) and `campWay(c, x, z, tx, tz)` (the next waypoint when the palisade stands between: crossing it, the nearer gate's point 2 m on your own side, then while in the gateway (|dx| < 0.9, |dz| < 2.3) on through to 3.2 m beyond; both outside, `round`: straight unless the line passes within 1.6 m of the ring, else the next corner pushed 3 m out the shorter way round by angle, stepping 1.5 m away from the camp while the way to it still hugs the wall); `world/bandits.ts` keeps `campMaps` (from `spawnCamp`) and `walk` steers a camp bandit by it.
- Sound (0.212, the owner's request: simple 8-bit effects he can replace later, room for his own music, volume settings; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `src/audio/synth.ts` (pure) `SFX` recipes (`Voice` {w square / tri / saw / noise (a 15-bit LFSR), f sweep [from, to] Hz, d, t, a, v, curve, duty, vib}), `render(name, take)` (normalised to 0.9, crushed to 16 levels; takes > 0 detuned), `toWav` (8-bit mono 22 050 Hz), `VARIANTS` / `sfxFiles` (step_ground ×3, step_hard ×3, step_water ×2, robot_step ×2, shot_smg ×2), `defaultSoundList`, `MusicList` / `MUSIC_MOODS` (menu, world, night, underground, fight). `tools/sfx.sim.ts` (`npx vitest run --config tools/sim.config.ts tools/sfx.sim.ts`) writes `public/sfx/*.wav` and, only when missing, `public/sfx/sounds.json` (name → {files, vol}: the owner's list; several files = one at random; wav / mp3 / ogg) and `public/music/music.json` (empty lists); Polish `CZYTAJ.txt` in both folders. `world/sound.ts`: one `AudioContext` made on the first key / click (`initSound`), buses master → sfx / music (`volumes` {master, sfx, music, mute} in localStorage 'gridWorld.audio', `setVolumes`), every sound first synthesized from its recipe, then replaced by its files from sounds.json when they load; `play(name, {at, vol, rate, jitter, far})` (distance fade over `far`, stereo pan by the camera's right, `MAX_VOICES` 28, a suspended context is resumed); `updateMySteps` (main.ts, on foot: by the distance really walked, `STRIDE` walk 1.55 / run 2.1 / crouch 1.0, `surface`: water, hard (dungeon, a `G.floor`, a voxel place), ground; jump / land), `updateFoeSounds` (robots' steps and servos by model, bandits' and ground creatures' steps, creatures' voices every 5–15 s (`VOICE`), the other players' steps from `net.peers` in `myLoc` via `setLocHook`), `updateSoundFrame` (being hurt from an HP drop, the music's mood: menu / fight (a foe within 30 m) / underground / night 20–06 / world; `updateMusic` fades between moods over 2 s, a list of one loops, an empty list falls back to 'world'). Hooks: `shoot` (shot_<look>, quieter with a suppressor), `slash`, `reload`, the dry click, `ray`'s hit, `fireBolt` / `ghostBolt` (robot_shot by the robots' colour, else enemy_shot), `fireOrb`, mounted turrets, `addBlast` (explosion), `shootCannon`, `grabPickup` / `takeDrop`. `ui/sound.ts` the menu's Sound panel (`#soundOpen`, `#soundBox`: sliders, mute, test sound, what is loaded). `server/main.mjs` `TYPES` serves wav / mp3 / ogg / m4a / flac. MP: every game plays what it sees itself; nothing is sent. Not synced: the other players' gunshots (no shot event is relayed yet). Tests: `tests/sound.test.ts`.
- More sound, heard by the others (0.213, the owner's report: no sound on his PC; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server `RELAY` + 'snd'): `initSound` makes the `AudioContext` at once and wakes it on every pointerdown / mousedown / keydown / touchend / click (capture) and on `visibilitychange`; `updateSoundFrame` retries `resume()` every second while it is not running; any `button` click plays 'click'; `played` counts started sounds (`soundInfo` {state, played, rate}; the Sound panel's status line; console `sound [name]`). New recipes: engine (a loop), cannon, door, chest, eat, drink, thunder (2.4 s), coin, levelup, chop / mine (2 takes), splash, click, die_creature, die_bandit, hammer; `tools/sfx.sim.ts` now merges new names into an existing `sounds.json` (the owner's entries kept). Loops: `hum(key, name, vol, rate, at?, far)` must be called every frame (a key not called for a frame is stopped by `endLoops`); `engineAt(key, at, dt, heavy, vol)` (pitch from the measured speed) for raiders (`updateRaiders`) and the other players' cars with someone aboard (`PeerCar` [8]); your own engine in `updateDriving`. Hooks: `toggleHouseDoor` / building `swing` (door), `openTransfer` (chest), `nourish` (eat / drink), weather `strike` (thunder after d / 340 s), gather `blow` (chop / mine), `fallCreature` / `fallBandit`, job site builders' blow (hammer), gold / level rises in `updateSoundFrame`, entering deep water in `updateMySteps` (splash). MP: `PlayOpts.share` (metres it carries) queues `[name, x, y, z, vol, rate, far]` (at `at`, else your position) in `outbox`, flushed every 0.05 s as `relay({t:'snd', loc, l})` (at most 24); receivers in the same `myLoc()` play them at that point (`onRelay` in world/sound.ts; client.ts's relay switch lists 'snd'). Shared: shots (130 m, suppressed 30), swings, reloads, the dry click, the cannon, jumps, landings, splashes, chopping, doors; steps and engines come from the snapshots' movement (a peer's step skips only a jump over max(4, 12·dt) m). An older server drops 'snd' (others hear no shots).
- Convoys are developed (0.214, the owner's rule: villages do not send convoys from the start; the player builds them up after the mechanic comes; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): settlement `Project` 'depot' Convoy depot (planks 40, stone 12, scrap 16, wire 6, parts 2; 12 h; `projectProblem`: `mechanicHere` and the warehouse; in `optionalProjects`; `RESOURCE_PLOTS.depot` (−20, −120); drawn in `world/settlement.ts`: gravel yard, a truck and two gun jeeps, repair shed, dispatcher's hut, barrier, lamp mast; no yard crew), `SettlementState.depotAt` (set by `finishJob`). `convoyStartOf(towns)` in gen/settlement.ts: −Infinity when Gridholm is not `progressive` (old worlds as before), else the earliest `depotAt` of any settlement, Infinity while none. gen/caravans.ts keeps a per-world `setConvoyStart` / `convoyStart` registry (unset −Infinity: tests, workers): `caravanOf` returns null and `departures` skips any departure before it, so `onRoad`, `caravanShift` (the market's `carCache` key carries the start), `lastArrival`, `settleConvoys`, `convoyNeed`, the terminal, the maps and the escort all follow. `syncConvoyStart` in `world/convoyfuel.ts` (main loop twice a second; `openWorld` at once). MP: from the shared `towns`, the same on every game. Tests: `tests/convoydepot.test.ts`.
- Power and people for the settlement's services (0.215, `PLAN_PRAD_I_LUDZIE.md` stage Z1; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `JOBS` adds warehouse 2, fuelpump 1, charger 1, depot 4, all whole-crew (`WHOLE`); `postsOf` mans the warehouse right after the power plant (`YARDS`) and the pump, charger and depot after the stations (`SERVICES`). `gen/energy.ts`: `SERVICE_KW` (warehouse 3, fuelpump 2, charger 0 (it charges from the spare power), depot 6) added to `siteKw`; `serviceOf(world, v, seed, s, k, t)` → {hands, power, why '' | 'hands' | 'power'} (works with the whole crew and `sitePower` ≥ `SERVICE_POWER` 0.25 over the day; established villages and unbuilt services always on). Effects: `settleConvoys` keeps a settlement's departure home (`dry` entry [id, t0, 'depot']) unless its own depot is built and `serviceOf` passes at the departure time; the fuel pump window refuses (`world/fuelpump.ts`), `updateCharging` skips and `chargerPrompt` says why (`world/charging.ts`), the hall's 'Unload vehicles' is replaced by the reason (`ui/hall.ts`; storing by hand stays). `serviceWhy(vid, k)` in `ui/villagestats.ts` (the words; the elder's status panel lists them), the terminal splits the convoys that stayed home by reason, `updateFarms` logs it. Crews drawn at the depot, the pump and the charger (`YARD_RING`, `KIT` in `world/settlement.ts`). MP: from the shared `towns`, the same on every game. Tests: `tests/services.test.ts`.
- Ports (0.216, `PLAN_PRAD_I_LUDZIE.md` stage Z2; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): a port is an entry of the shared `outposts` under `portId(dock id)` = 'port:<dock id>' with `k` 'port' (`OutpostKind` = `LodeKind | 'port'`, `OutpostState.pier`, `hold`), so the outposts' grid (`grids`, `demandOf` + `PORT.kw` 10), life (bunks, fields, settlers; `crews` puts `PORT.crew` 4 at the warehouse once `done.port`; `pantryOf` counts the warehouse as a shed), locks (`withOutpost`) and the two-hourly `settleAll` work for it; `partProblem` allows only 'port', 'power', 'bunks', 'field' there; `worthRobbing` is false (no raids until Z7); `finishDue(o, null, ...)`. `gen/outposts.ts` `PORT` (needs, crew, kw, `power` 0.25, `vol` 6000 L, `back` 18 m). `gen/ports.ts` (pure): `portPoint(p, a, b)` (dock frame: a out to sea, b across), `portSpots(p, dry)` (warehouse, crane, power, 3 bunks, 3 fields: first dry candidate clear of the others), `portProblem` (dry, ≤ 4 m of relief), `newPort`, `portName`, `portVol` / `portRoom` / `portPut` / `portTake`, `portWorks` → {hands, power, why '' | 'unbuilt' | 'hands' | 'power'}. `world/ports.ts`: `updatePorts` (main loop: drawn within 520 m, `finishPorts` for every port), the board (`nearPortBoard` → E → `openPort`), the shed (walls collide in `portHit`, doors to the dock, gables, racks, sign), the yard fence and crates by the hold, the crane (its jib a group that swings while `portWorks`), the outposts' `drawPowerSource` / `drawBunks` / `drawField` (exported from `world/lodes.ts` with `DrawInto`), settlers (hoes, the warehouse crew carrying), `portNameAt` for the HUD. `ui/port.ts` (`portClick` in the dialogue handler, handling `data-port` and the outposts' `data-op` rows from `partHTML` / `powerHTML` / `peopleHTML`, now exported with a `pos`): the hold (Take / Store by hand from the backpack always), the crane (`cunload` your trunks by the warehouse and your boats within 45 m of the dock's head; `cloadt`, `cloadb:<i>`) only while `portWorks`; `foundPort` / `foundProblem` from the dock's window (`portRow` in `ui/pier.ts`, `data-pier` found / port). Maps label it by name. Tests: `tests/ports.test.ts`.
- Cargo lines (0.217, `PLAN_PRAD_I_LUDZIE.md` stage Z3; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/shiplines.ts` (pure) `ShipLine` on the home port (`OutpostState.line`: `id` (opening time), `to` / `toName` / `tx` / `tz`, `km` (straight × `LINE.detour` 1.25), `ship` (`LineShip` {id, k 'ship' | 'steamer', fuel?, by}: a boat taken off `char.boats`), `t0`, `k` next voyage, `sent` cumulative, `only` goods, `last`, `trips`) and the other port's `OutpostState.rcv[rcvKey(from, line)]` (cumulative, so a new line counts from 0). `LINE` (reach 40 km, pace ship 6 / steamer 9 m a game minute, `perSlot` 2 crates a hold slot → `shipCap` 96 / 80, load 40 min, `lpkm` 2 L, `calm` 0.15), `voyageOf` / `periodOf`, `voyageFuel`, `assignShip` / `recallShip` / `fuelShip`, `shipAt`, `dueVoyages`, `settleLine` (under the home port's lock: every voyage that reached the other port, in order: 'idle' unless both `portWorks` at departure, 'dry' / 'calm', else loads the home hold (`only`) up to the cap into `sent`), `unloadLines` (under the other port's lock while its crane works: `portPut` sent − rcv), `arriving`, `lineTargets`, `voyageSails` / `shipPos` (read only, memoised; the drawing and maps). `world/ports.ts`: `tickPort(id)` (settle + unload + log lines; the window calls it on open and every click), `tickLines` (every 15 s, quiet `withOutpost(id, ..., true)` for ports with a due voyage or cargo its working crane can take), `drawLineShips` (each line's ship by `showcaseBoat` on the straight way between the docks' heads within 700 m, hidden over land). `ui/port.ts` `lineHTML` (open a line `lnew:<port id>`, hand over `lship:<boat id>` your moored ships within 60 m of the dock's head, hold empty, nobody aboard (`inBoat`, peers' `boat`), `lfuel`, `lrecall` (moored by `launchSpot`), `lonly:<good>` / `lall`, `lgive`; ids are taken from the attribute after the first ':'). Maps: a dashed teal line and the ship's dot. `bare` counts a line's ship. Tests: `tests/shiplines.test.ts`. Not yet: pirates, return cargo (open a line from the other port), lines between players' rival ports.
- Crews at the old plants (0.218, `PLAN_PRAD_I_LUDZIE.md` stage Z4; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/plantcamps.ts` (pure) `CREW` (uranium 8, chips 8, propellant 6, battery 8, optical 6, alloy 10, precision 8, robotics 10, aerospace 10; radar and powerplex none), `needsCrew(world, k)` (towns worlds only), `campId(k)` 'camp:<kind>', `campSpots(site, dry)` (`PortSpots`: board at r + 6, 3 bunks at r + 16, 3 fields at r + 36, first dry spot round the circle from behind the plant), `newCamp` (an `outposts` entry with `k` 'camp', `ik`, `cw` the crew), `crewOf` (`crews(o, n).dig` of the camp), `crewRuleFor(world, outposts)` (memoised). `gen/installs.ts` `setCrewRule` / `crewAt`: `canRun` also asks the rule (unset: always true, so tests, workers and old worlds are unchanged). Camps reuse the outposts: `partProblem` allows only 'bunks' and 'field', `crews` gives the plant `cw` workers after the fields, `pantryOf` counts a camp as a shed, `worthRobbing` false, `procOf` null, `portClaims` levels their bunks and fields; `finishPorts` in `world/ports.ts` finishes their builds. `world/plantcamps.ts` `updateCamps` (main loop: sets the rule for the world, draws camps within 600 m: a CREW CAMP board, `drawBunks` / `drawField`, building sites, settlers with hoes / hammers), `campHit` (foes and G.obstacle), `dropCamps` (closeWorld). `ui/install.ts` `crewHTML` (stake `data-ins="camp"`, crew x / y, `partHTML` bunks / field and `peopleHTML` from ui/outpost.ts) and `campClick` under `withOutpost`; the screen says NO CREW. Maps label camps by name. MP: the camp lives in the shared `outposts` under its lock; every game works the crew out the same way. Not yet: people or food by route. Tests: `tests/plantcamps.test.ts`.
- Power lines (0.219, `PLAN_PRAD_I_LUDZIE.md` stage Z5; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): an outpost or a port (not a camp) runs a line to a village. `gen/grid.ts` `PLINE` (reach 6000 m, cap 60 kW, poles every `gap` 40 m from `start` 18 m past the outpost to `end` 62 m short of the village's middle, `perKm` planks 12 / wire 10 / scrap 3, `ends` cable 2 + parts 1, `hours` 4 + 2·km, `xp` 40 + 20·km), `PowerLine` {v, name, x, z, km} in `OutpostState.pl` (part 'pline': `done.pline`, its needs by `plineNeeds(km)` in `partPlan`, `hoursOf` / `xpOf` in gen/outposts.ts replace `OUTPOST.hours` / `xp` in the windows), `newLine`, `plineProblem` (camp, one line an outpost and a village, reach), `linePoles`, `lined`; `grids` makes a lined outpost a grid of its own when no source reaches it; `lineFlows(world, outposts, t)` (memoised by t, cleared twice a second by the game and by `setLineBalance`): from the grids without lines (`gridState(..., raw)`) and each lined village's balance without its line, in id order, a village with `free` power feeds its grid's shortfall, a grid with spare feeds the village's `short`, at most `cap`; `gridState` adds the flows; `lineInto`, `lineOfVillage`. `gen/energy.ts` `Balance.line` / `short`, `balance(..., noLine)`, `setLineHook` (unset: nothing flows: tests, workers, old worlds). `world/powerlines.ts` `updatePowerLines` (main loop: registers both hooks for the world, draws lines within 700 m: yellow stakes, poles with crossarm, braces and insulators rising with the job, three sagging wires once up), `dropPowerLines` (closeWorld). `ui/outpost.ts` `plineHTML` / `plineClick` (`data-op` pl:<vid> / unpl / give:pline; also in the port window); the elder's power text (`lineText`) and the terminal's POWER page; maps: a pale yellow line. MP: in the shared `outposts` under the outpost's lock; the flows come from the shared `towns` and `outposts`, the same on every game. Not yet: lines between villages or to the old plants, chaining grids through a village. Tests: `tests/powerlines.test.ts`.
- Commuters (0.220, `PLAN_PRAD_I_LUDZIE.md` stage Z6; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `TownState.commute` (outpost id → people asked, the outpost's `workCap`), written under the village's lock by `ui/commute.ts` (`commuteHTML` / `commuteClick` in the outpost, port and crew camp windows, `data-op` commute:<vid> / commute:off). `gen/workforce.ts` `postsOf` adds 'commute:<id>' posts (kind 'commute', partial fill) after everything else, so only free people go. `gen/commute.ts` (pure) `COMMUTE.reach` 2000 m from the village's middle, `commuteVillages` (settlements only), `commuteFrom`, `setCommute`, `commutersAt(world, towns, id, t)` → {got, want, v}. `gen/outpostlife.ts`: `workCap(o)` (dig crew, port crew, a camp's `cw`), `crews(o, people, extra = commuters(o))` (commuters `walk` work the post first, the settlers farm then fill the rest), `crewFactor` (commuters count whole, settlers by `fed`), `setCommuteHook` / `commuters` (unset: none). `world/commute.ts` `updateCommute` (main loop: the per-outpost counts once a second for the hook, up to 3 walkers on the straight way within 400 m), `dropCommuters` (closeWorld). `crewOf` (camps) counts commuters with no life too; `crewRuleFor`'s memo key includes them. The elder's status names the posts 'Walking out to X' (`postName`). MP: the ask lives in the shared `towns` under the village's lock; the counts come from the shared `towns` and `outposts`, the same on every game. Not yet: walking by road and its time, commuters on the fields, villages that are not settlements. Tests: `tests/commute.test.ts`.
- Defence takes power and people (0.221, `PLAN_PRAD_I_LUDZIE.md` stage Z7; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): settlements (`progressive`) only: `gen/workforce.ts` 'guard:i' posts (`JOBS.guard` 1, whole crew) for each `works.turret`, after the services and before the commute posts; `gen/energy.ts` `GUN_KW` 4 a turret in `siteKw` (`sitePower`'s cache key carries `works.turret`), `wallGunsAt(world, v, seed, s, t)` → {n, work, manned, power, why '' | 'hands' | 'power'} (work = the manned turrets while `sitePower` ≥ `SERVICE_POWER`, else 0; established villages: all). `world/wallguns.ts` refreshes each village's `work` every 2 s and fires only turrets i < work (the rest sweep); `gunsWhy` in `ui/villagestats.ts` (the elder's status), postName 'Guard at wall turret n'. Outposts: `crews` in `gen/outpostlife.ts` adds `guard` (min of the settlers left after the fields and `turretsOf`, before the dig); `gen/grid.ts` `OUT_GUN_KW` 3 a turret in `demandOf`, `outGunsAt(world, outposts, id, t)` → {n, work, guards, power, why} (none off the grid or below `OUT_GUN_POWER` 0.25 of `powerShare`); `settleRaids(..., gunsAt)` / `raidResult(..., guns)` / `holdChance(o, s, guns)` count the working turrets at the raid's end; `world/outpostraid.ts` fires only those; the defence text says why. Lighthouses: `gen/lhpower.ts` `lhPower(world, l, towns, outposts, t)` → {ok, by 'old' | 'village' | 'grid' | '', name} (old worlds always; a towns world: a village within `LH.grid` 500 m with `LH.kw` 2 kW `free`, or an outpost source reaching it whose grid's share ≥ `LH.share` 0.25; it reads the spare, nothing is deducted); `world/lighthouses.ts` `shining` (power cached 2 s) for the beams, glows, prompt and window. MP: all from the shared `towns` / `outposts`, the same on every game. Tests: `tests/defencepower.test.ts`.
- Warnings from afar, stage L4 (0.237, `PLAN_SPAWNOW.md`; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/warnings.ts` (pure) `FAR_CALLS` per kind / robot model / 'bandit' {s sound, far m, every [s], vol} (ravager howl 260, leechwing 250, artillery 200, bandit talk 130...), `REST_TALK` (round a fire every 7–14 s, laugh 30%), `CALL_NEAR` 45, `farGain(d, far)`, `muffle(d)` (low-pass Hz), `PRINTS` (paw / small / hoof / boot / claw: `step`, `size`, colour), `TRACKS` {life 120 s, max 900, reach 160, dim 0.55, segs 8}, `printFade`, `printSegs(k, size, heading, side)` (pointing the way it went), `SMOKE` {join 25, linger 60, rise 2, life 22, puffs 16, far 1200, glow 1500}. `world/sound.ts` `PlayOpts.carry` (`farGain` and a `BiquadFilter` low-pass beyond 30 m); new SFX howl_ravager, bellow_bramble, chitter_gnawer, robot_hum, robot_chirp, bandit_talk, bandit_laugh. `world/farcalls.ts` `updateFarCalls` (overworld: foes not fighting call now and then, only past `CALL_NEAR`; one voice a bandit group). `world/foetracks.ts` `updateFoeTracks` (walkers on the ground, a print per `step`, one `LineSegments` batch with vertex colours faded every 0.5 s; none on water or a place's floor; cleared off the surface or on a jump > 300 m), `printCount`, `clearFoeTracks`. `world/restsmoke.ts` `updateRestSmoke`: bandits with `mode` 'rest' (set by `BANDIT_OPS.settle(b, 'wait')`; `IntentOps.settle(o, k)` now gets the plan's kind; 'rest' lasts until it fights, searches or flees) get a fire (stones, flames, `addLights` fire, `addGlows` at night) and a sprite smoke column (fog-free, daylight-scaled), drawn nearer at the same angle beyond 130 m with `depthTest` off and hidden by `clearView` when the land hides its middle; `restFires` (body heat), `clearRestSmoke`. MP: every game works from the foes it has; copies get `mode` through foesync [13], so everyone sees the same smoke. Tests: `tests/warnings.test.ts`. Smoke columns (0.237.1): `world/smokecolumn.ts` (three.js only) `ColumnLook` {puffs, life, rise, size, grow, c, op}, `CAMPFIRE`, `WRECK_SMOKE` / `ENGINE_SMOKE`, `WRECK_VENTS` (ship-local vents), `makeColumn`, `stepColumn(c, dt, wx, wz, alpha)`, `placeFar(c, x, y, z, cam, edge)` (nearer at the same angle, no depth test), `clearView(from, x, y, z, top, ground)`, `dropColumn`; used by `world/restsmoke.ts`, the crash site (`world/crashpod.ts` `cols`, replacing the smoke rings: within 2.5 km, hidden by the land, faint at night) and the film's crash shot (`ui/cinematic.ts`, a lighter grey).
- Lines in the terminal (0.261, read only; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `storesPage` in `ui/terminal.ts` lists, beside the drone `AIR LINES`, `AIRSHIP LINES` (`allSkyLines`, `skAt`, hull) and `SEA LINES` (ports' `OutpostState.line`, `shipAt`) touching the warehouses listed. MP: from the shared `towns` / `outposts`, the same on every game.
- The dangers of the air, stage F6 (0.260, `PLAN_LOTNICTWA.md`; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/airhull.ts` (pure) `HULL` {armour (airship 0.45, copter 1.1: hit → hull percent), strike (14 / 22), flak (9 / 16), kit 40, worn 50, slow 0.6, sink 2.2 m/s, storm 0.55, every 35 s, min 12}, `Airship.hull` / `Copter.hull` / `SkyShip.hull` (percent, absent = whole), `hullOf`, `hullHit` (an occupant's damage scaled), `hullDamage` (a fixed percent), `hullMend`, `hullSpeed` (1 above `worn`, falling to `slow` at 0), `strikeEvery(storm)`, `FLAK` {chance 0.35 of camps where the danger ≥ 2, range 170, every 3.2 s, hit 0.7, high 8 m}, `hasFlak(world, campId, danger)` (hash 0xf1a4), `flakHit(d, speed)`, `RISK` {base 0.015, per 0.014 a danger point, storm 0.12 over 0.3, max 0.3, dmg 35}, `tripRisk`. `world/damage.ts`: `setAircraftProtection` and a branch in `hurtPlayer` / `environmentalDamage` after the vehicle's (not while `proxied()`): everything that would hurt the occupant goes to `world/airhull.ts` `hit` (the hull of the machine you sit in, any seat; your HP stays; `initAirHull` from main.ts). `world/airhull.ts` `updateAirHazards` (main loop): the pilot's game rolls lightning (a machine over 6 m up, `strikeEvery(weatherAt(...).storm)`, flash and thunder) and flak (camps within 700 m with `hasFlak` and not cleared (`char.camps`), guns drawn within 600 m at the camp's (+5, +5): a turning twin barrel; a machine more than `FLAK.high` up within range: a tracer (`addBolt`), 'cannon', a hit rolls `flakHit`); `repairAircraft` (the Vehicle Repair Kit through `useRepairKit` when not driving: the machine you sit in or the nearest within 9 m, `hullMend`), `airAboard` / `copAboard` (the records), `flakGuns` (debug). `world/airships.ts` / `world/copters.ts`: speed and climb × `hullSpeed`, a dead hull gives no thrust (the airship sinks at `HULL.sink`, the rotorcraft settles at its fall rate and cannot lift), the hint shows the hull. `world/creatures.ts` `LEECH_CEIL` 70: a Leechwing neither sees nor keeps stalking a target higher than that over the ground. Sky lines (`gen/skylines.ts`): `SkyTrip` adds 'damaged' / 'wrecked'; a loaded trip rolls `tripRisk(danger half way, storm at the departure)` by hash: no cargo, `RISK.dmg` off the hull, at 0 'wrecked' stops the line (`skAt` parked) until recalled (`skRecall` keeps the hull) and mended; the window shows the hull. MP: the hull is in the shared records; each game's `hurtPlayer` hits its own occupant's machine; only the pilot's game rolls lightning and flak. Not yet: drones' own hazards beyond F1. Tests: `tests/airhull.test.ts`.

No mast, no timed job, no power or crew (a vehicle, built at a warehouse that `hubWorks`). `world/copters.ts`: model (lofted cabin, skids, tail boom and fin, a spinning rotor of 4.4 m and tail rotor), `Live` per rotorcraft drawn within 1200 m, `drives` (the pilot, else the lowest id within 700 m), `step` (W/S forward / astern, A/D turn, Space / C climb / descend, no key hangs; landed = skids within 0.05 of the highest of five ground points, no taxiing; it touches down only where `fitToLand` and slowly, else hangs 0.6 up with a toast; a flat tank settles at `fall`; the wind; `PROBES` + `blockedAt` (`foeBody` and `crownHit`: the rotor tips at 4.2 m) revert a move that newly blocks it, `Live.stuck` lets it move out of what it is caught in), `follow`, `tell` (the relay type 'air' with ids starting 'cop:'; `airships.ts` ignores them), `updateCopters` (main.ts beside `updateAirships`; true while aboard), `copterCamera` (13 m behind, or the seat; V `toggleCopterView`), `nearCopter` / `copterPrompt` / `boardCopter` (the pilot's seat first), `copterUse` (E seated: out only landed within 0.6 m of the ground, on dry clear ground), `myCopterSpot` / `copterSeatPoint` (`PeerState.boat` through world/peers.ts), `copterHint`, `openCopterHold` (F), `refuelCopter` (R), `coptersForMap` (a pale cross), `clearCopters` (clearLevel), `inCopter` (`inAir()` in world/airships.ts includes it: every rule that skips things in an airship skips them in a rotorcraft). `ui/copters.ts` `copterYardHTML` / `copterYardClick` (plans known and the warehouse working: start the frame, hand over from the stock via `store(id)` of ui/airships.ts, take it down; built it stands at `copterSpot` and gains xp): the hall window (`data-hcp`, in the dialogue's stock-locked clicks) and the port / wild warehouse window (`data-port="cp"`). - Airship lines, stage F4 (0.258, `PLAN_LOTNICTWA.md`; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/skylines.ts` (pure) `SKY` {reach 30 km, pace 10 m a game minute, load 45 min, perSlot 4 crates a hold slot (`skCap` = `AIRSHIP.hold` × 4 = 120), lpkm 2.2 L, kwhpkm 2.4 kWh a km, storm 0.45}, `SkyLine` {id, to, toName, tx, tz, km (straight), ship (`SkyShip` {id, drive, fuel, kwh, by, t}: the airship taken off the shared `Char.airships`), t0, k, only, sent (cumulative), last [k, 'ok' | 'dry' | 'storm' | 'empty' | 'nomast'], trips} on `TownState.zline` / `OutpostState.zline` (one a warehouse; `bare` refuses with a ship on it), `skKey` 'sk:<home>:<id>' (the other warehouse's `rcv` counter), `newSkyLine`, `skLeg` / `skPeriod`, `skEnergy` (round trip; diesel or cells), `skFuel` (canisters), `skAssign` / `skRecall` (the airship leaves the list / comes back moored at the home mast with its hold `containers[id]` and name), `skAt` (home / out / there / back / parked), `skDue`, `settleSkyLine(world, home, l, masts, charge, take, now)` (each due trip in order: no mast at either end 'nomast'; a storm over the home warehouse at the departure (`weatherAt`) 'storm'; a cell ship charges full when the home mast works; too little energy 'dry'; nothing to carry 'empty'; else loads up to `skCap`, burns, adds to `sent`), `allSkyLines`, `skyLineOf`, `skWaiting` / `skDrain` (the other warehouse takes in sent − rcv while it works, `noteArrival` for passing through), `skLoad` (goods waiting to go on by way 'sk' first). `gen/transit.ts` `Way` adds 'sk'. `world/hublines.ts` `runHub` settles the warehouse's sky line and drains incoming ones (needs the mast); `tickHubLines` ticks lines with a trip due or cargo waiting. `world/skyships.ts` `updateSkyShips` (main loop: `showcaseAirship` per line within 1500 m: at the home mast while loading, climbing over `CLIMB` 350 m to 40 m over the ground, down to the other mast), `skyShipAt`, `skyShipsForMap`, `clearSkyShips` (closeWorld). `ui/skylines.ts` `skyLineHTML` / `skyLineClick` (open a line to a warehouse with a mast, hand over an airship moored here with nobody aboard (`airOccupied` in world/airships.ts: seated, standing, on the ladder, or a peer with `boat[0]` = its id), pour canisters, goods `only:<k>` / `all`, recall (refused while another airship is moored at the mast), give up the line): the hall window (`data-hsk`, in the dialogue's stock-locked clicks) and the port / wild warehouse window (`data-port="sk"`); `ui/transit.ts` lists incoming sky lines and the 'sk' way. Maps: a dashed pale green line and the airship's stroke. MP: the line in the shared `towns` / `outposts` under the home's lock, the intake under the destination's lock with its own counter (never doubled); the airship leaves and returns to the shared list; every game draws it from the clock. Not yet: robbery and dangers (F6), the terminal's list of sky lines. Tests: `tests/skylines.test.ts`.
- Airships, stage F3 part 2 (0.257, `PLAN_LOTNICTWA.md`; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): the gondola is taller (`AIRSHIP.below` 9, `gond.h` 3.4, floor `FLOOR` 0.5 over its bottom) and walked like a ship's deck: `gondolaUnder` (standing in it, not seated), `carry` (with its move, in `updateAirships`), `gondolaFloor` (G.floor) and `gondolaHit` (G.obstacle: ends and sides, the doors amidships (|u| < `AIRSHIP.door` 0.8) open only while `low` (moored or the floor < 3.5 m over dry ground)); `local(l, x, z)` is `at`'s inverse; `newLive` places the model at once (else `carry` threw a walker by the origin). Seats: `seatHere` (the free seat nearest you along u); `boardAir` sits you there from the gondola (or climbs in from beside it as before); `airUse` seated: tie up (helm, mast near), else stand up (the helm left holds course and throttle; `stepOut` is gone: walk out of the open door). The rope ladder: `ladderDown(l)` (not moored or low, |sp| < `AIRSHIP.slow` 1.5, the floor within `AIRSHIP.ladder` 40 m of dry ground under the right-hand door at `LAD_V`), drawn per live airship as a `LineSegments` (`ladder()`, `setDrawRange` to the ground: `showLadder`); `atLadderTop` (at that door) / `atLadderFoot` → `startClimb` → `climb {l, d}` (feet under the floor) stepped by `updateClimb` (W up, S down at `AIRSHIP.climbs` 2.2 m/s, Space lets go; off at the bottom, in at the top); `inAir()` is seated or climbing; `airCamera` first person while climbing. Bumps: `PROBES` (gondola corners, the envelope's underside, sides, nose and tail) → `blockedAt` (`foeBody` + `crownHit` in world/overworld.ts: a tree's whole height and crown radius); `step` reverts a move that newly blocks it (bounce `sp × −0.25`, throttle 0, toast), `Live.stuck` lets it move out of what it is already caught in (set on cast-off, while following, and after a bump re-checked where it was). The throttle stops at zero (`Live.notch`; a fresh press goes on). Moored, the gondola rests on the ground (y raised over the ground under it). The village mast is staked out (like the pad): `TownState.amWait` / `amspot {x, z, a}` (a = the way its airship lies), `handOverAmast` returns `wait`, `placeMast`, `mastAt(world, h, towns)` reads the spot (old masts, ports and wild warehouses keep the old place); `gen/fields.ts` `mastLine` (circles at the foot and along the airship), `mastClaims` (soft 1, in `fieldClaims`), a 'mast' zone in `reservedZones`, `mastProblem` (`AIRMAST.near` 150 m, zones and claims along the line, places, roads and water at the foot, the ground under the airship ≤ `AIRMAST.rise` 1.5 m over the foot); `world/fields.ts` mode 'mast' (`mastHologram`: the foot, the mast, the airship's outline at the mast's head lying away from you, the kept ground; `confirmMast` under `withTownStock`); `villageMastHTML` / `villageMastClick(vid, 'stake')` (`data-hamast="stake"`); `world/share.ts` clears the trees when another player's `amspot` arrives. `liveAirships()` (debugging). MP: the doors, the ladder and the walkers' spots come from the shared pose (`myAirSpot` sends standing / climbing spots with seat −1); only the driver's game checks bumps. Tests: `tests/airships.test.ts`.

The mast: a village's `TownState.amast` / `agiven` (timed job 'airmast', `completeAmast` via `finishJob`; `JOBS.airmast` 1 whole crew, `siteKw` + kW, `amastOf` in `gen/energy.ts`), an outpost's part 'amast' (store kinds once `done.port`; `demandOf` + kW; `bare` refuses it). `gen/airships.ts` (pure) `amastPlan`, `handOverAmast`, `hasAmast`, `amastWhy`, `mastAt(world, h)` (34 m from the warehouse a quarter turn from the pad's side), `moorPose(m, ground)` (nose at the head, lying away), `AirBuild` {drive, given} on `TownState.abuild` / `OutpostState.abuild`, `airNeeds`, `airRows`, `handOverAir`, `Airship` {id 'air:<pid>:<t>', x, y (the envelope's middle), z, yaw, drive, fuel, kwh, moor (hub id), by, t} in the shared `Char.airships` (`SHARED` list; hold `containers[id]`), `newAirship`, `airEnergy`, `burnAir`, `chargeAir`, `fuelAir`, `airRangeKm`, `lowest`, `SEAT_U`. `world/airships.ts`: masts drawn within 700 m (lattice, cap, ladder, hut, a blinking lamp while `amastWhy` is ''), airships within 1500 m (envelope, cruciform fins, gondola, two engine pods whose props turn); like the boats one game drives each (`drives`: the helm, else the lowest id within 900 m), `step` (moored: held at `moorPose`, cells charge while the mast works; W or Space casts off; else throttle W/S kept, A/D turn, Space / C climb / sink, the wind from `weatherAt`, the gondola kept `min` over the ground under the nose, middle and tail, the ceiling), `tell` ({t:'air'} relay 10 / s while busy, else 1 / s), `follow`;
