V `toggleAirView`), `nearAirship` / `airPrompt` / `boardAir` (the helm if free, else a seat), `airUse` (E aboard: tie up at a mast within 16 m of the nose (slow), get out when low, leave or take the helm), `myAirSpot` / `airSeatPoint` (world/peers.ts draws riders through `PeerState.boat`), `airHint`, `openAirHold` (F), `refuelAir` (R: a canister from the hold or the backpack), `airshipsForMap` (maps: a pale stroke along its heading), `clearAirships` (clearLevel). `ui/airships.ts` `villageMastHTML` / `villageMastClick` (`data-hamast`), `airYardHTML` / `airYardClick` (keel:fuel / keel:cell, give from the warehouse's stock, drop an empty keel): the hall window (`data-hay`, both in the dialogue's stock-locked clicks) and the port / wild warehouse window (`partHTML(o, 'amast')`, `data-port="ay"`). `bare` refuses with a truck on it), `hlKey` 'hl:<home>:<id>' (the other warehouse's `rcv` counter), `newHubLine`, `hlLeg` / `hlPeriod` (`ROUTE` pace and loading), `hlEnergy` (diesel or kWh a round trip), `hlRob` (`ROUTE` robbery by the danger half way, the cannon), `hlFuel`, `hlAt` (home / out / there / back / parked; parked after a dry, silent or empty trip), `hlDue`, `settleHubLine(world, home, l, linked, charge, take, now)` (each due trip in order: 'wrecked' stops, 'silent' without the radio link now, an EV charges full at home if `charge`, 'dry', 'empty', else burns, rolls the hashed robbery (hull −`ROUTE.hurt`), else adds the cargo to `sent`), `allHubLines`, `hubLineOf`, `hlWaiting` / `hlDrain` (the other warehouse takes in sent − rcv, as much as fits), `takeHold` (the goods asked, most first). `world/hublines.ts` `runHub(id)` (under the hub's lock: settles its line with `linkedHubs` and `hubWorks`, loads from the village hold (`withdraw`) or the outpost (`portTake`), drains incoming lines while it works (`deposit` / `portPut`), log lines), `tickHubLines` (every 15 s, quiet `withTownStock(…, true)` / `withOutpost(…, true)` for homes with a trip due and warehouses with cargo waiting), `hubTruckAt`, `updateHubTrucks` (main loop: trucks by `convoyModel` within 600 m on the straight way), `clearHubTrucks` (closeWorld). `ui/hublines.ts` `hubLineHTML(id, attr)` / `hubLineClick` (open a line to a linked hub in reach, hand over a vehicle within 80 m with an empty trunk via `takeVehicle`, goods `only:<k>` / `all`, fuel, recall with `parkVehicle`, drop): in the hall window (`data-hhl`, in the dialogue's stock-locked clicks) and the port / wild warehouse window (`data-port="hl"`); `tickPort` runs `runHub`. Maps: a dashed amber line and the truck's dot. - Crates that come and go and the radio link, stage M3 (0.251, `PLAN_MAGAZYNOW.md`, the owner's rule: crates and barrels are not built; one crate and one barrel; they appear when a heap is too big for the pockets and vanish when emptied; bigger crates from materials later; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `data/cargo.ts` `CARGO` crateW / barrelW 100 L (crateM / barrelM kept, `ONE_KIND` turns them into the one kind), `POCKET` {litres 20, min 10}, `pocketOf(k)` (10 stones, 13 planks, 40 wire...), `autoBox(k)`, `boxOf(k, n)`, `dropEmpty(list)` (empty crates vanish, old kinds mapped; run by `loadChar` on the hands and every container's bays). The orders and Oskar's prices for crates are gone (`orderOpen` in `data/orders.ts`, indexes kept; `basicSmith`). `character.ts`: `handBox`, `countHeld`, `intoHandBox(k, n)`, `settleCrates()` (a crate with no more than the pockets hold goes back into them, loose k goes into the crate you hold, a heap over `pocketOf` becomes a crate in your hands (what they held is stowed), a second crate goes to `onCrateOverflow` = `dropAtFeet` in world/drops.ts); `addItem` packs into the hand crate when the backpack is full, `takeOne` / `hasItem` read it; main.ts settles every 0.3 s, the backpack's `changed` and a pickup too. `ui/market.ts` `crates(at)` (the hand crate and the cargo bays of vehicles within reach): `carried` counts them, `takeFrom` takes from them last (emptied ones vanish), `putAway` puts a heap that finds no room into the hand crate. `ui/transfer.ts`: a stack dropped into an empty bay becomes a crate there. `ui/racks.ts` only shows the racks already built and unloads a crate in your hands. Radio: `gen/radiomast.ts` `RADIO` (needs, reach 12 km, kw 2, crew 1, hours 6, xp 120); `gen/radio.ts` (pure) `mastPlan`, `handOverMast` (a village's mast from its stock: `TownState.mast` / `mgiven`, the timed job 'radio', `finishJob` → `completeMast`), `hasMast`, `mastWhy` (none / idle / hands / power), `radioNet` (union-find of working masts within reach), `linkedHubs`, `hubStock` (a village's hold and own goods, a port's or wild hold). Outposts: part 'radio' (store kinds once `done.port`; `demandOf` + kW, worked by the warehouse's crew). Settlements: `JOBS.radio` 1 (whole crew), `siteKw` + kW, `mastOf` in gen/energy.ts. UI: `ui/radio.ts` (`villageRadioHTML` / `villageRadioClick` in the hall window, `data-hradio` in the dialogue's stock-locked clicks; `linkedHTML` in the hall and port windows; `stockLine`), the terminal's WAREHOUSES tab reads the linked stocks. `world/radiomast.ts` `drawMast` on the hall's ridge (world/hall.ts) and the port's shed (world/ports.ts); `townLook` includes `mast`. MP: masts in the shared `towns` / `outposts` under their locks; the network from the shared state on every game; the hand crate is personal. Tests: `tests/radio.test.ts`.
- Carriers and racks, stage M2 (0.250, `PLAN_MAGAZYNOW.md`; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/racks.ts` (pure) `RACK` {gain 2.5, max 40}, `Racks` (item → count; `TownState.racks` for a village's hall / warehouse, `OutpostState.racks` for a port or a wild warehouse), `rackCount`, `rackVol`, `rackGain`, `rackProblem` (only an empty crate / barrel, while there is room), `putRack`, `takeRack` (only while the hold fits without it). `hallVol(s)` in `gen/hall.ts` (the spec's vol + racks once the hall stands; `holdRoom` uses it), `portCap(o)` in `gen/ports.ts` (`portRoom` uses it). `ui/racks.ts` `racksHTML(r, attr, pre)` / `rackClick(r, what, put, used, cap)` (put the empty crate or barrel in your hands on the racks, empty a loaded one into the hold, take one off into empty hands); in the hall window (`data-hrack`, in the dialogue's stock-locked clicks) and the port / wild warehouse window (`data-port` 'rack:...', under `withOutpost`). `rackDeco` in `world/hall.ts` draws them stacked along the back wall (also the ports' sheds, `sigOf` carries them). `gen/carriers.ts` (pure) `CARRY` {pace 70 m a game minute, pause 3, longest 1300, deposit 2}, `carrierRoutes(world, towns, outposts, lodeOf, x, z, reach, t)` (an outpost with its dig in a working hub's hex → the hub, oil 'barrel' else 'crate'; a settlement with its warehouse working: each farm field (cows barrel, hens crate, else sack) and each built yard quarry / mine / lumber / oil / raremine → the hall), `carrierAt(r, i, t)` (from the game clock: loaded there, a pause, empty back). `world/carriers.ts` `updateCarriers` (main loop: routes within 450 m rescanned every 2 s, routes over water left out, at most 24 figures: a crate in the 'carry' kit, a sack on the shoulder, a barrel rolled ahead), `clearCarriers` (closeWorld). MP: racks in the shared state under the warehouse's lock; carriers from the clock, nothing sent. Tests: `tests/racks.test.ts`.
- The warehouse network, stage M1 (0.249, `PLAN_MAGAZYNOW.md`, the owner's design: warehouses serve hex areas, one to a hex, standing in the wilds too, named, deposits delivering on their own; later carriers, crates and barrels for room, radio, convoys, ships, flying; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/warehouses.ts` (pure) `HEX` (pointy hexes, `cols` 120 round the planet so they close at the seam, `w` ≈ 1003 m, `r`, `dz`), `hexOf` (odd-r offset, `key` 'c:r'), `hexCenter`, `hexCorners(h, ref)`, `parseHex`; `Hub` {id 'v:<vid>' / outposts id, kind village / port / wild, x, z, hex, name, vid}, `hubs(world, towns, outposts)` (`villageHall`: an old village's hall, a settlement's built warehouse; ports and wild warehouses), `hubAt`, `hexProblem` (one warehouse to a hex: `warehouseProblem(..., others)` in gen/fields.ts, `foundProblem` for ports, the wild placement), `hubWorks` (old hall always; settlement `serviceOf` 'warehouse'; `portWorks`), `handOff` (world/lodes.ts `finishOutposts` every two-hour mark after `settleAll`: an outpost with its dig in a working hub's hex moves its whole crates into `OutpostState.out` {hub, since, n} (cumulative; a new hub starts a new `since`), keeping the on-site works' input), `waitingAt`, `drainVillage` (ui/stock.ts `stockAt`, the hall window: `TownState.rcv['<lode id>:<since>']`), `drainStore` (`tickPort` under the warehouse's lock; `tickLines` also ticks warehouses with crates waiting), `feeders`, `hubsNear`, `wildId` 'wh:<hex>', `wildName`, `villageHubName` / `TownState.whn`, `cleanHubName`. Wild warehouses: `OutpostKind` 'store' (`isStoreKind`; `WILD_STORE` needs, `partName` / `partNeeds` / `partProblem`, no procs, no raids), built as part 'port' like a port's warehouse, `OutpostState.fr` (its frame: `wildFrame`, `frameOf` in gen/ports.ts, `isStore`, `Frame`); `world/ports.ts` draws and finishes both (no crane for 'store', the sign is its name), `ui/port.ts` serves both (`wild()`, `WHY_WILD`, `carriersHTML`, `nameHTML` and `data-port="rename"` with `#whName`; boats at a dock are anyone's since 0.248). Placement: item `whkit` (Warehouse Plans, basic order, `basicSmith`) → `world/wildstores.ts` `startWhPlacing` / `updateWhPlacing` (hologram of the shed, the way in and the hex; doors face you; problems: the hex, water, relief > 4 m, cities, villages within 260 m, places, deposits, roads, old stones, other outposts) / `confirmWhPlacing` (under `withOutpost('wh:<hex>')`) / `cancelWhPlacing`. The village hall window: `networkHTML` (rename via `data-hname`, in the dialogue's stock-locked clicks). Outposts: `carriedHTML`. Terminal: WAREHOUSES tab (`storesPage`). - Vehicles belong to nobody (0.248, the owner's rule: every player sees every trunk and has full access to every vehicle: cars, ships and the flying machines to come; `PROTOCOL` 16, server `FIELDS` + 'vehicles'): `Char.vehicles` is a shared list (`SHARED` in `world/share.ts`, keyed by id) and a vehicle's trunk with its cargo bays is the shared container `containers['trunk:<id>']` (`trunkKey` / `trunksOut` in `save.ts`, run by `loadChar`; `Container.bays`; `VehicleState.trunk` / `bays` only for unlisted vehicles: raiders, found ones); `trunkOf(st)` (makes it, for actions), `trunkFor(v)`, `trunkHere(v)` (only looks: markets, the hall, caravans), `baysOf(v)` (looks) in `world/vehicles.ts`; `openTransfer` locks the trunk like any container and re-reads `bays` after the lock. `enlist` (found, bought, built, back from a route), `takeVehicle` / `sellVehicle` remove in place and drop the trunk. `syncVehicles` (main.ts twice a second) draws what the list holds (added, gone, moved, refitted or loaded by someone else). Live: whoever gets in first has it: `myCarList` = the vehicle you are in, sent as `PeerState.cars` / `carIds` as before (the server's seats and the gates' vehicle transit unchanged); `world/peers.ts` `syncCars` draws it as a ghost and `holdVehicle(v, held, at)` hides this game's copy (`Vehicle.held`: skipped by `vehicleSpot`, `vehicleHit`, `hullBlocked`, `rayVehicle`, refuelling, the pump, sales, the outposts' trucks, lights; its `st` pose follows the ghost); two at once: the higher id leaves (`Someone else got in first`). Only the game that moves a vehicle sends its place: `Field.mask` (`vehicleMask`) keeps x / z / heading at the last heard value unless `steeredHere(id)` (driving it, or left it and not yet sent: `unsent`, `poseSent`, `setSteered` from main.ts). Boats were shared already: `MAX_BOATS` 8 a world. Not synced: charging at a post runs only on the game that last drove the EV (`tendsHere`). Tests: `tests/sharedvehicles.test.ts`, '(0.248) the vehicles and their trunks' in `tests/mp.test.ts`.

The load rides on the slot: `Slot.b` {k, n} (save.ts); `stackable` / `putSlot` keep it apart, `weightOf` counts it. Items type 'cargo' (`ItemType`), `HANDS_ONLY`, `TOOL_PRICE`, basic orders (appended), `basicSmith` (the wooden ones). `VehicleSpec.bays` (scout 2, van 6, mastodon 16), `VehicleState.bays` (`fitBays`), `BAY_SPOTS`; `world/vehicles.ts` `refreshBays` (open beds only: crates and barrels drawn in the jeep's bed), `pourCanister` takes a fuel barrel in a bay first; `ui/transfer.ts` `TransferSpec.bays` (ids 'c:i', `PackSide.prefixes`): `cargoMove` / `takeBay` / `putBay` (hands ↔ bay, a stack dropped on a bay's crate fills it, a crate dropped on the trunk or the backpack empties it; crates never in a trunk with bays); `ui/backpack.ts` `packHeld` / `unpackHeld` (acts 'pack' / 'unpack', drag p → h / h → p), `boxLine`; slots show a gold fill bar and `data-b` (`itemTip(..., b)`); `gen/hall.ts` `unloadBays` (the warehouse's Unload vehicles). Drops: `NetDrop.b`, `sendDrop(..., b)`, `Pickup.b`, `give` puts a loaded crate into the hands; server/mp.mjs keeps `d.b`. The van: `VehicleModel` 'van' (UTV-3 Nomad: 6.2 × 2.4 m, 19 m/s, trunk 14, hull 200, tank 110, 0.9 L/km, closed cab, price 600), `SEATS.van`, `HULL_BOXES.van`, `vanBody`, `bodyOf`, `LAMPS`, `EV.van`, `ROUTE.cap.van` 18, `GARAGE` (appended), raiders (20%), `regionVehicle` (a quarter of the jeeps by `hash(..., 0x7a4e)`); `MODEL_ORDER` / `modelOf` number the models for `PeerCar` [0] (scout 0, mastodon 1, van 2; older games show a van as a scout). MP: the bays are the owner's (personal `char.vehicles`); a crate passes between players by hand or the ground. Not synced: the crates in a peer's jeep bed. Tests: `tests/cargo.test.ts`.

- Cracks in the ground (0.245.1, the owner's report: a slit by Gridholm's wall showed under the map; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `Terrain.exact` eases the ground back to every non-surface pad's floor height within `EDGE_EASE` 4 m of its hole (a place's voxel floor over a hole in the ground) after the claims, rivers, lakes, gates and megaliths (before the surface pads), so a field levelled near a village cannot pull the hole's edge off the floor; `VoxelGrid.surface` (`core/voxel.ts`) makes the floor slab `SKIRT` 6 m deep, so nothing shows under it. `Terrain.rev` (bumped by `setClaims` / `setSettlementPads`) → `recheckChunks` in `updateStreaming` (`world/overworld.ts`): every loaded chunk compares its `Chunk.lat` (the heights it was built on) with the new lattice, a budget a frame, and is rebuilt if they moved (`rebuildChunksNear` still does the nearest at once). MP: shared; whoever sees a body past its day removes it (the same on every game). Tests: `tests/bodies.test.ts`.

linking only within a generation and only mountain mouths); mouths `w` 6–8.5, `h` 5–7. `extraCave` (stream `hash(world, rx, rz, 0xcae6)`, id salt 0xca7f; `CAVES` {mountain 0.55, wild 0.07, home 900, village 260, place 30, road 18, river 45, outcrop 11}): a mountain region with no old mouth gets one at 55% (relaxed slope rules), a lowland region an outcrop cave at 7% (`wildMisfit`: sea, rivers, villages, cities, places, fog, lodes, crystals, megaliths, gates, lakes, roads; ≤ 4 m relief across 20 m), `nearOutcrop` keeps trees, rocks and plants off the heap (`chunkTrees`, `chunkRocks`, `chunkPlants`). `world/caves.ts` `drawCave`: a 10 m tunnel, framing boulders, a lintel stone, two pairs of great stones, rubble, hanging teeth; - Stones and beasts on slopes (0.237.2, the owner's report: stones stood level on flat shelves on hillsides, beasts stayed level on slopes; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `Rock.lean` [dy/dx, dy/dz] (gen/trees.ts `slopeAt`: the secant over ±r, capped at `LEAN_MAX` 0.9; `slopeSink`: the stone sits deeper by the most the ground at 0.9 r lies under that plane, at most 0.3 r), `leanY(lean, px, pz, y, h)` in `gen/rockshape.ts` (each point raised by the slope's rise, fully at the foot, `LEAN_TOP` 0.6 at the crown); `PropBatch.rock` / `vein` take `lean`; `rockTop` in world/overworld.ts adds it to `rockSurface`, so the collision follows the drawn stone. Creatures (`onSlope` in world/creatures.ts, not fliers or swimmers): pitch and roll from the ground `c.r` ahead / behind / to each side, capped 0.5 rad, eased (`Creature.slope`), added on top of the pose (taken off at the start of `animate`). MP: from the seed and the foes' positions; nothing sent.
- Cleared places stay cleared (0.236.2, the owner's call, meant to be easy to undo): `REFILL_ON` false in `gen/repopulate.ts` makes `refill` (its `on` parameter defaults to it) never bring a cleared labyrinth sector, wreck, cave or metro back; the `CLEARED` marks and `refillAt` are kept, so setting it true restores the 7–14 day refill (places cleared meanwhile refill on the next visit). Bandit camps (30 min) and city garrisons (45 min) are unchanged. MP: `killed` is shared as before; nothing new is sent.
- No lairs for now (0.236.1, the owner's decision: lairs and seeded patrol routes are too permanent and farmable; L3 is shelved in `PLAN_SPAWNOW.md`): patrols stay the L2 random crossings and road waits (a new spot on the ring and a new crossing point each time, nothing seeded or saved). Under consideration: cleared ruins and wrecks never refilling (today `REPOP` 7–14 days in `gen/repopulate.ts`). L4 (warnings) is next, without lair signs.
- Senses and intentions, stage L2 (0.236, `PLAN_SPAWNOW.md`, the owner's request: settle how each kind finds you: sight, noise, shots, scent that is hard to lose; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/senses.ts` (pure) `SenseKey` (creature kinds, robot models, 'bandit', 'drone'), `SENSES` {view 'cone' / 'wide' / 'all', dark (0 fooled by night and fog .. 1 thermal), hear (× a noise's radius), steps (footsteps heard within, m), scent (m, through anything), trail (s it follows your track once it had your scent), lose (s it searches)}: ravager scent 30 / trail 120, gnawer 18 / 45, bramble 10; leechwing, repair, drone 'all'; sentinel dark 1; bandit cone, dark 0, hear 0.8. `sensesOf`, `viewFacing`, `seenAs(s, vis, lit)`, `hears(k, r)`, `TRACK` {lag 6, every 2, keep 240}. `world/stealth.ts`: `sense(..., key)` uses them (`lit` = your visibility in good light; a smell sets `Mind.nose`, `last`, `lost` 0; hunting with a nose and lost < trail: `last` follows `trackPoint`), `loseOf(f, key)` (replaces `LOSE.*` in creatures and robots), `layTrail(dt, scented)` (main.ts: a crumb every 2 s, scented on foot out of the water, vehicles and boats), `myTrack(lag)`. Noise listeners use `hears`. `gen/spawnring.ts` `march` 180, `crossLead` 35, `crossOn` 90, `wait` 110–170, `leadOf(speed, secs)`. `world/spawnring.ts` `meetPoint(secs)`, `crossPoints(x, z)`, `setRoadAhead` / `roadsideSpot` (`roadsideAhead` in `world/raiders.ts`). `world/intents.ts` `addIntent(k, foes, ops, x, z, pts)` ('track' / 'cross' / 'wait'; `IntentOps` goto / engaged / alive / settle / drop / at: `BEAST_OPS` in creatures (investigate the point, `coming`), `ROBOT_OPS`, `BANDIT_OPS` (home there, 'return')), `updateIntents` (main loop once a second: the track `lag` grows to 40 s with distance, a broken track ends at its last scented spot; a crossing walks its two points; a wait settles at the roadside; engaged / dead groups dropped; after `march` the hidden, far ones `drop`, the others settle), `clearIntents` (closeWorld, the seam). Users: ravager and gnawer spawns 'track' (drawn by a shot: the noise as before), robot spawns 'cross', bandit patrols 'wait' on a road else 'cross'. MP: the spawning game runs the plans; a tracker after another player goes to where that player is (Not synced: other players' tracks). Tests: `tests/senses.test.ts`.
- Encounters from afar, stage L1 (0.235, `PLAN_SPAWNOW.md`, the owner's decisions: spawns further off and out of sight, foes come to you, warnings, lairs (cleared 6–9 days); all worlds; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/spawnring.ts` (pure) `SPAWN` (ring near = the view + `past` 15 within `near` 120..200, `span` 110; `minPlayer` 120; `despawn` 350 + `unseen` 5 s, `hard` 650; `threat` 350; `lead` 20 s / `leadMax` 120 / `jitter` 25; `raiders` 300–420; `ambush` 90–160; `march` 150 s), `ringOf(view)`, `leadOf(speed)`. `world/spawnring.ts`: `viewRange` (= `fog.far`), `spawnRing`, `hiddenFrom(x, y, z, h)` (no player in your place (`otherPlayers`) sees the feet or the head: past the fog or a `rayWorld` block), `farFromPlayers`, `ringSpot(a, t)`, `heading` (the way you move, else look), `meetPoint` (ahead on your way), `mayDrop(o, x, y, z)` (past `despawn` and unseen `unseen` s, checked once a second; past `hard` always). Users: `trySpawn` in `world/creatures.ts` (ring round you, towards the noise when drawn; `comeOver`: ravagers and gnawers `investigate` the meeting point for `march` s with `Creature.coming` (the den follows them until they settle or hunt), leechwings and skitterwings move `home` there; brambles graze where they are; the sea kinds hidden too), `trySpawn` in `world/robots.ts` (`home` = the meeting point, artillery 60 m back, state 'return'), bandit `patrols` (`home` = the meeting point, 'return'), `spawnRaidersMaybe` and `tryAmbush` (stepping along the road to the first hidden spot 90–160 m ahead) in `world/raiders.ts`; the culls use `mayDrop` (quest creatures and ambush / raid bandits keep 240 m, raiders `raiders[1]` + 100); `threat()` counts within `SPAWN.threat`. The spawn env's `forbidden` also asks `poisNear` past `STRUCT_LOAD` − 30 m (places not loaded yet). MP: the spawning game (`spawnAuthority`) checks every player's view and distance; nothing new is sent. Tests: `tests/spawnring.test.ts`.
- Cold and heat, stage B4a (0.234, `PLAN_BIOMOW.md`, the owner's decisions: all worlds, cold and heat only weaken (never HP), new clothes; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/temperature.ts` (pure) `BIOME_TEMP` (day / night °C per biome), `TEMP` (lapse 0.006 °C/m, rain 4, storm 6, cloud 3 by day, wind chill 6 below 10 °C, `under` dungeon 12 / cave 8, `comfort` 10..28, `span` cold 30 / heat 15, `tau` 90 s, fire r 6 up to +15, torch 3, lamp 1, `room` 18 with house 0.85 / village 0.5 / cab 0.8, swim −4 and clothes soaked to 0.3), `CLOTH_TEMP` (warm / cool per wear item: hide, woven, composite, the old armour; new `furHood` 4, `furCoat` 9, `furMitts` 3, `furTrousers` 4, `furBoots` 3, `desertRobe` cool 8, `shemagh` cool 4), `clothing`, `biomeAir` (bilinear over `biomeCell`s), `airTemp`, `feltTemp` (warm counts half in the heat, cool only in it), `bodyTarget`, `stepBody`, `BODY` (mild 30, severe 70), `bodyState`, `bodyEffects(b)` → multipliers {kcal, water, regen, effort, speed}. `world/bodystate.ts` `BODY_FX` (read by `updateSurvival`, `spendStamina` / `drainStamina` and the walking speed in `updatePlayer`); `world/bodyheat.ts` `updateBodyHeat` (main loop: samples the air (`seen` weather, `daylight`), the clothes, `fireSpots` / `campFires`, `carriedLight`, `insideHouse` (world/houses.ts: whole houses' rects), `inVillage`, an enclosed cab, swimming twice a second; `Char.body` −100..100, personal, saved; log lines on a change past the threshold; `#heatrow` under the food bar when |b| > 10), `airText` (the clock line's °C), `resetBodyHeat` (death). The new clothes are basic orders (appended at the end of `ORDERS`) and in `GEAR_PRICE` / Oskar's `BUYS`; the item tooltip shows ±°C. MP: personal, nothing sent. Tests: `tests/temperature.test.ts`.
- Biomes, stage B3 (0.233, `PLAN_BIOMOW.md`, the owner's decisions: new worlds only; swamps full of shallow murky pools; `PROTOCOL` unchanged; the server rolls new rooms' seeds in the new band, so update it for them to be shaped): `gen/worldrules.ts` `SHAPED` {lo 1e6, hi 999e6}, `shapedWorld(world)` (seeds −1e6 .. −999e6; `newWorldSeed` rolls there; server/main.mjs and mp.mjs `-randomInt(1_000_000, 999_000_000)`; older towns worlds −1 .. −999 999 and editor worlds ≤ −1e9 keep their land). `gen/heights.ts` `baseHeight` (the old land) and `naturalHeight` = `shapeLand` of it in shaped worlds. `gen/biomeshape.ts` (pure, imports no heights or biomes): `BIOME` (moved here, re-exported by gen/biomes.ts), `warmthAt`, `wetNoise` (unclamped, so B1's wetness is unchanged), `awayFromHome`, `SHAPE` (dune len 64 / amp 2.5–6 / wander; swamp over 2.2, keep 0.25, grain 26, bump 1.3, sink 0.26, wet 0.45, deep 0.85; tundra 1.6; crystal 6), `shapeWeights(world, x, z, h)` → {desert, swamp, tundra, crystal} (smooth weights from the climate without rivers, the mountains, the base height, home fade), `swampTable`, `shapeLand`, `swampWater` (the table where the swamp weight ≥ `wet`). gen/biomes.ts reads `baseHeight` (classification unchanged). `Terrain.swampLevel`, `Terrain.dry` (the road's height raised to level + 0.3), `Terrain.water` → 'murky' where the lattice ground is under the table and still natural (|heightAt − base| < 0.35). `world/water.ts` `swampSheet` (from `buildChunk`: murky surface over cells under the table, shores by marching squares, ripples), the map tiles colour the pools (`swampy`). Tests: `tests/biomeshape.test.ts` (old seeds unchanged, ~2/3 water in a swamp's core, ≤ 1.1 m deep, dunes > 2 m).
- Biomes, stage B2 (0.232, `PLAN_BIOMOW.md`: the looks, in every world; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/biomes.ts` `BIOME_LOOK` per `BiomeHere` (`line` ground lines / bark / stone, `leaf`, `fill`, `keep` share of generated trees, `tree` `TreeLook` pine / cactus / dead / palm / acacia / shard / lush / null, `big` the great kinds stand, `size`, `deco` reeds / grass / snow / pebbles, `haze` and `thick` for the air), `BIOME_CELL` 24 m, `biomeCell` / `biomeOfCell` (cached), `biomeTint(world, x, z, 'line' | 'fill')` (each cell's colour averaged with its 8 neighbours, bilinear between cells), `treeIn(b, seed, big)` → {look, size} or null. `world/overworld.ts` `buildChunk`: per-vertex colours for the ground's fill and lines (`sharedFillV` / `sharedLineV` in `world/props.ts`, vertexColors; snow mixes to pale above `SNOW_LINE` per vertex; polar chunks keep `ICE_COLOR`), trees through `treeIn` with `hash(t.seed, 0xb10e)` (a tree the biome does not grow is skipped after its index is known, so `tree:<cx>:<cz>:<i>` keys hold), drawn by `drawTreeLook` (`world/trees.ts`: `cactus`, `dead`, `palm`, `acacia`, `shards`, 'lush' = broad + vines) under `PropBatch.remap` (`tintOf(b)`: GRID → line, LEAF → leaf, VINE → dark leaf), rocks under their biome's remap, `groundDeco` (near chunks only, by the chunk's middle biome, spots from `hash(world, cx, cz, 0xdec0)`, skipped on cleared ground, pads, roads, water). `world/biomesky.ts` `tintBiome` (main.ts after `updateSky`): fog, background and dome lean towards `haze` by day (`HAZE` 0.42), fog nearer by `thick`, eased over ~2.5 s. `gen/crystals.ts` `CRYSTALS.wastes` 0.6: a region in the Crystal Wastes holds a field that often (`biomeAt` at the field's spot). Not synced: nothing (all from the seed). Tests: `tests/biomes.test.ts`.
- Biomes, stage B1 (0.231, `PLAN_BIOMOW.md`, the owner's decisions: 10 biomes and the giant crystals to start, colours may leave the green, terrain shape (B3) in new worlds only, rules later (B4); `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/biomes.ts` (pure) `Biome` plains / forest / taiga / tundra / desert / savanna / swamp / jungle / mountain / coast / crystal, `BiomeHere` adds 'sea' / 'ice', `BIOMES` (English name, map colour), `BIOME_KINDS`, `BIOME` (noise scales warm 9 km / wet 6.5 km / crys 5.2 km, `home` 3.5 km temperate round Gridholm easing over `fade` 3 km, `lapse` 1/320 a metre, thresholds `cold` 0.34 / `hot` 0.6 / `arid` 0.4 / `humid` 0.6, `mountain` 0.35, `coast` 260 m below `coastH` 10 m, `swamp` 0.72 below `swampH` 18 m, `crysAt` 0.7 from `crysFrom` 6 km), `warmth` (latitude, height, noise), `wetness` (noise, rivers via `nearRiver`), `biomeAt(world, x, z)` (order: ice, sea, mountain, coast, crystal, swamp, cold → taiga / tundra, hot → desert / savanna / jungle, else forest / plains), `biomeName`. `placeName` (world/overworld.ts) ends in the biome's name instead of 'Wilds' (`biomeHere`, cached per 32 m cell). `ui/worldmap.ts` `biomeLayer` / `toggleBiomes` (B on the open map, `ui/input.ts`), `drawBiomes` (128 m cells of explored ground, 8 ms a frame), `biomeKey` (the key of the biomes seen), a footer line. `ui/devmap.ts` `scanBiomes` (the whole planet at 500 m a pixel, a few rows a frame, under the seas). Tests: `tests/biomes.test.ts`. MP: from the seed, nothing shared.
- The journal (0.230, the owner's request: see what the character has taken on, switch its map and compass markers on and off; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `ui/journal.ts` (J in `ui/input.ts`, closed by J or Esc; the dialogue panel, `journalClick` in the dialogue handler, `journalOpen` checks the panel still holds it): the elder's step, the side task and the guide, `G.char.quests` (`questStatus` from world/quests.ts, now shared with the tracker; who gave it, the reward, the notice's text; 'Give it up' twice → `abandon`), the contracts (`contractLines`), the escort, the leads (`allLeadMarkers`); each entry with a marker gets `data-jm` Marked / Hidden (`.buy.off`), 'Mark everything' / 'Hide every marker' (`data-jall`). `gen/journal.ts` (pure) `markId` ('q:<quest id>', 'c:<contract id>', 'l:<lead id>'; plus 'elder', 'side', 'guide'), `toggleId`, `pruneMarks` (the journal drops ids of things no longer held). `Char.hideMarks` (personal, saved, not in `SHARED`), `markShown` / `toggleMark` in `character.ts`. `allQuestMarkers` / `allLeadMarkers` carry the ids; `questMarkers` / `leadMarkers` (the compass, minimap and big map) leave the hidden ones out; the tracker `#qtrack` still lists everything. Tests: `tests/journal.test.ts`.
- Surrounding and tight places, stage W7 (0.229, `PLAN_WALKI.md`; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/surround.ts` (pure) `SURROUND` (`slots` 12 on a `ring` 4.5 m, `wait` 8 m, reshared `every` 0.5 s or when the target `moved` 1.5 m, `probe` 1.8 m, `wall` 3 of 8 sides), `slotAngle`, `nearestSlot`, `shareSlots(bearings, order, open)` (nearest first; the open place nearest its bearing keeping 12 / n places apart, then any, else −1 = wait), `backToWall`, `HEIGHT` {from 1.5, full 4, max 1.3, flyer 2.5, upSpread 0.03}, `heightMult`, `upSpread`. `world/surround.ts`: `openAt(tx, ty, tz, a, r)` (`rayWorld` from the target's chest, the ground within 2.5 m of its level, `foeRules.body`), `slotFor(self, group, key, ring)` → {x, z, wait} (plans cached per group key), `sidesBlocked`, `updateSurround` (main.ts: the 'back to the wall' line once in 90 s with foes within 16 m), `atWall`. Users: the Ravager pack's 'hunt' (runs to its place, leaps from it, up to 6 s to get round; without a place paces at the wait ring), the Gnawer nest's 'hunt' (zig-zags to its place at 2.5 m, then straight in; without one waits), bandit bruisers (places at 1.6 m when two or more fight; the rest wait). `world/weapons.ts` `tactic` × `heightMult(G.pos.y − the ground under the foe)` (not a flier more than `HEIGHT.flyer` above its ground; 'From above ×' line); `fireBolt` adds `upSpread` × distance. MP: the group's owner shares the places against the turn's target; the shooter's game reckons the height; nothing new is sent. Tests: `tests/surround.test.ts`.
- Morale and retreat, stage W6 (0.228, `PLAN_WALKI.md`; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server `RELAY` + 'spare'): `gen/morale.ts` (pure) `MORALE` (start bandit 0.6 / leader 0.85 / ravager 0.75 / alpha 0.9 / gnawer 0.55 / leechwing 0.7 / skitter 0.4, `leaderNear` +0.2, `shock` ally 0.15 / leader 0.35 / wound 0.25 / cond 0.1 / half 0.2, `fire` {r 6, rate 0.05}, `rally` 0.3, `winning` {hp 0.3, lift 0.2}, `brk` 0.3, `regain` 0.02 after `calm` 10 s, `near` 25), `Fate` run / give / back, `FATE` and `fateOf(roll, cornered)` (give 30%, cornered within 14 m 50%; of the rest half 'back'), `BROKEN` (gone 70 m from every player, help after 35–70 s with 1–3 more at morale 0.75, a surrender waits 25 s, E reach 3), `DRIVEN_XP` 0.5, `TERRITORY` (ravager 90, gnawer 40, bramble 30, leechwing 160, lurker 70, skitter 50), `DEN` {r 10, floor 0.35, calm 20, heal 0.02, at 6}, `LEASH_POSE` 1.2, `REGROUP` {lost 0.5, time 9, at 5, hp 0.7}, `shocked`, `effective`, `breaks`, `pastEdge`. Bandits (`world/bandits.ts`): `morale`, `wounded`, `fate`, `fateT`, `came`; `groupSize` / `halfDown` / `rallied`; `moraleBreaks` (in 'fight' and 'search': the boss's shout once, else `breakBandit`: `mode` 'give' / 'rout', state 'flee', out of its camp (`campId` cleared, `campCheck` = the old 'Camp cleared' path), half-kill xp), `brokenTurn` (hands up facing you, or running; gone past `BROKEN.gone`, a 'back' one queued in `comebacks` → `updateComebacks` spawns them near the nearest player with `spawnAuthority`), `calmDown` (idle), `spareBandit` (drops gun, maybe ammo / medkit / scrap; 30% `askLead` tip on the searcher's game; a copy → `spareOwner`), `nearSurrendered` (E in `world/interact.ts`); killing one who gave up: no gold or bounty; the old 50% hurt flee is gone. `world/rig.ts` `Pose.hands` (hands up). Raids (`world/villageraid.ts`, `world/outpostraid.ts`, `world/caravans.ts`) skip broken bandits. Beasts (`world/creatures.ts`): `den` (fixed at birth), `morale`, `wounded`, `calm`; `beastMorale` before each kind's turn: den healing, regain, fire (`fires` in `world/baits.ts`, filled by `updateThrowing`: lit flares, a torch in your hand (`carriedLight` 3), peers' `lt` 3), break → 'rout' (ground kinds run to the den) or climb / roam home; past `TERRITORY` with the beast > half of it from its den → 'leash' (ground kinds: stand and call `LEASH_POSE`, walk home, resume when you are back within 0.85 of it) or roam home; pack shocks in `fallCreature` (`packSize`, `packHalf`), wound shock in `hurtCreature`. `applyFx` (world/conditions.ts) knocks morale on a stagger or a fall. Robots: `regrouped`, `mode` 'regroup' (`squadSize`; a squad down to half walks to its Repair Drone until 70% hp or 9 s). MP: the owner runs it all; `mode` 'give' / 'rout' and beast states sync as before; `spare` relay {nid} to the owner (`setSpareHook` in world/remote.ts); an older server drops it. Not synced: the den and morale of a foe taken over from a player who left start afresh on the new owner. Tests: `tests/morale.test.ts`.
- Conditions, stage W5 (0.227, `PLAN_WALKI.md`; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/conditions.ts` (pure) `HitFx` {impact, knock, cut, emp}, `POISE` per `FoeKey` (one hit's share of full health × impact to stagger), `FELLS` (ravager, gnawer, leechwing, scout, guardian, the bandit roles), `MACHINES` (EMP), `IMPACT` (sledge 1.6, spear 1.15, blade 1, machete 0.9, gun 1, rifle 1.3, shotgun 0.6), `COND` (stagger 0.7, down 1.9, stun 3.2 / heavy 2 s, `fell` 2.2 × poise without a knocking blow, `mult` down 1.3 / stun 1.25, `guard` 1.4 s), `BLEED` (share 0.12 of a cut a second, 5 s, cap 0.08 of max hp a second), `condOf`, `condTime`, `bleedDps`, `condMult`, `PLAYER_COND` (stagger 0.4, down 1.2, slow 0.35, heavy 0.18 of max HP, bleed 0.8 HP/s a wound for 8 s, max 3), `playerBleed`. `world/conditions.ts`: `CondFoe` fields (`cond`, `condT`, `condGuard`, `bleed`, `bleedT`, `bleedBy`; Creature / Robot / Bandit extend it, plus an eased `down` for the pose), `applyFx` (from `damageFoe(t, dmg, credit, fx, by)` on the owner's game: the condition, `breakOff` of the attack, a leechwing felled → 'grounded', a cut on a beast bleeds), `tickCond` (in the update loops of creatures, robots, bandits and drones: bleeding through the foe's own hurt, true while held), `setBleedRoute` (world/foesync.ts: another player's cut → `quietLoot` + `kill` to them), looks in each `animate` (a beast on its side kicking, a bandit on his back, a robot on its side, a stunned machine frozen with blue sparks, a stunned drone sagging, bleeding drips). `world/weapons.ts` `gunFx` / `meleeFx`, `tactic` × `condMult` with 'It is down!' / 'Short-circuited' lines; the EMP (`useItem` in world/loot.ts) passes `{emp}` to every foe within 6 m. The player: `knockDown` / `stagger` / `bleed` / `heavyHit` (on whoever the turn runs against: proxied → `peerFx` in world/remote.ts, sent with the turn's `hurt` as `fx`; `takeFx` on the receiver), `updateMyCond` (main loop, no bleeding in a safe zone), `playerDown` (updatePlayer: no input, no jump; `attack` refused; `cr` sent), `playerStaggered` (×`slow`), `downView` (camera drop and roll in main.ts), `stopBleeding` (a medkit, also at full HP), `clearMyCond` (death). Triggers: Bramble charge and robot blows with push ≥ 10 (Assault) knock down; grenades within `GRENADE.fell` 0.45 r knock down, further stagger; bites bleed (ravager 0.3, gnawer 0.12, lurker 0.45), Scout blades 0.2, bruiser blades 0.35. MP: `fhit` carries `fx` (`cleanFx`), `hurt` carries `fx` (strings), foesync entry [14] = cond, [15] = bleeding (drones [14]). Tests: `tests/conditions.test.ts`.
- Telegraphs and second attacks, stage W2 (0.226, `PLAN_WALKI.md`; `PROTOCOL` unchanged, server unchanged): `gen/tactics.ts` `STATE_MULT` (bramble `stun` all 1.6 / front 2, leechwing `grounded` 1.5, sentinel `shield` front 0.45 / `hot` 1.8, assault `exposed` 1.25 / back 1.4) and `hitMult(k, cos, u, melee, state)` (`why`); `world/weapons.ts` `tactic` passes `t.mode || t.state` and logs `WHY` lines; a feeding creature counts as unaware. Creatures (`world/creatures.ts`, new states): Ravager lead `howl` (`HOWL` 1.3 s, rears, `growl_ravager` shared; lone ravagers within 70 m join), dash `feint` 30% (breaks off after 0.45 s); Bramble paws on `threat` (`PAW` 1.4, dust), blocked in a charge → `stun` (`STUN` 2 s, sparks, 'thud'); Leechwing `screech` (`SCREECH` 0.8 s, 'screech_leechwing') before a dive at the player, a dive timing out near the ground → `grounded` (`GROUNDED` 2.6 s); a gnawer's death scatters its nest (`scatter` along `dir`, then a new flank). Robots (`world/robots.ts`, `Robot.mode` / `modeT`): Sentinel `shield` (`SHIELD` 0.8 s, arm raised) after a burst then `hot` (`HOT` 1.6, backs off, sparks); Artillery `mark` (`MARK` 1.2 s, `markBeam` a red line to `markAt`, then `lob(r, markAt)`; `aimShell`); Assault `swing2` (a second blow `SWING2` 0.5 s later, 0.8 damage), the charge locked to `chargeDir`, a miss → `exposed` (`EXPOSED` 2.4 s); Guardian `suppress` (3 bolts at `m.last` while unseen); Scouts in packs ≥ 3 circle at `SCOUT_RING` 6.5 m, the attacker rotating every `SCOUT_TURN` 4 s; Repair hides `HIDE_BEHIND` 4 m behind the nearest heavy robot. Bandits (`world/bandits.ts`, `Bandit.mode`): `tryGrenade` (gunners and leaders, out of sight, 6–24 m, `GRENADE.cool` / `group` via `groupNade`), 'flank' (`FLANK`: with ≥ 3 guns fighting the last walks to 13 m off the player ±1.75 rad from the group, up to 9 s, again after 22 s; the rest fire ×0.55 as often); `fireBolt(..., at?)`. Grenades: `gen/throwing.ts` `GRENADE` {fuse 2.2, r 4.5, dmg 22, push 7}, `lobVelocity`, `grenadeHarm`; `world/throwing.ts` `throwGrenade(from, at, dmg)` (a blinking, beeping ball with a red ring; `blowGrenade`: `addBlast`, noise 50, harm only on the thrower's game: you via `hurtPlayer` + a shove, peers via `hurtOther`); SFX 'beep'. MP: `foesync` entry [13] = `mode` (copies show the shield, laser, heat, stagger; hits there use it); creature states go in `state`; grenades ride the 'throw' relay (k 'grenade'). Tests: `tests/tactics.test.ts`, `tests/throwing.test.ts`.

Item `flare` (Signal Flare, cons, Zofia 12, basic order 3 ← lampoil + scrap, `basicSmith`). SFX throw / thud / hiss. MP: {t:'throw', loc, k, p, v} relayed; every game in the place flies it and makes the noise, flare and bait for its own foes; the stone pickup only from the thrower's game. Bandit gunners and leaders (`Bandit.cover` / `duck`: the group sinks `COVER.duck` and the legs fold) and outdoor Guardians (`Robot.cover` / `duck`, `GUARD_COVER` low 0.95, duck 0.6) use it; in cover they keep fighting 7 s without sight (`m.lost`). The player: `peekK()` (crouched and aiming) lifts the eye in main.ts (`CROUCH_DROP` × (1 − 0.85 peekK)), the bolts' body (world/bandits.ts), `aimHeight` and the sent `cr`; `coverHint` (once a game). k < `CITY_VAULTS` the entrances, the rest underground), `cityDecoys` (2–3 caved-in METRO entrances on the next street spots, ≥ 70 m from the real ones). `gen/metro.ts` (pure, cached): `METRO` (`span` 165: the city's radius squeezed to that many metres; hall 34 × 12 × 9; tunnels 4 wide, 7 high; 2–5 underground stations by farthest-point sampling, halls nudged ≥ 48 m apart; `loop` / `blocked` / `stub` / `niche` odds), `generateMetro(world, city)` → a `DungeonMap` with `style` 'metro' and `metro: MetroInfo` {stations (k, entrance or −1, name, x, z, alongX, hall box), rails (axis-aligned centrelines), blocks (train: a 14 × 4 × 5 solid; not next to a block), platform pillars, stairs up ('V<n>' portals, `stairOpsFor`) on a long wall of each entrance station, the boss in the underground station furthest from entrance 0 by open hops, guards (own stream `seed ^ 0x6a7d31`: entrance halls 45% a light one, underground halls 1–2 heavy ones and a chest, tunnel stretches ≥ 14 m 22% a scout / guardian). `metroCity(world, id)` (any entrance id → its city), `metroId` (saved under entrance 0's id). `world/level.ts`: `loadDungeon` normalises a city vault id to the metro (depth 1, gx/gz 0; `world/dungeondeco.ts` 'metro' (tunnels chamfered 1.5 with rings every 3 m, halls vaulted). `world/cities.ts` `metroEntrance` (opening with treads, parapet and rails, arch with a METRO board, the M pole; - Machine blasts and labyrinth robots (0.194; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `data/robots.ts` `BLAST` (`pulse` 1.15 s, `edge` 0.3, per `RobotKind` {r, hurt (player HP), foe (foe hp)}, `drone`, `turret`), `blastShare(d, r)`, `blastPulse(t)`. `wreckRobot` → `updateScraps`: the wreck (out of `W.robots`, so it no longer fires) pulses (scale and colour towards `PULSE_C`, quickening sparks) for `BLAST.pulse`, then `machineBlast` (`world/blast.ts`): `addBlast` in `world/fx.ts` (an additive see-through sphere, a wire icosahedron shell and a ground ring swelling over 0.32 s and fading over 0.55 s, sparks) and the hurt by `blastShare`: you through `hurtPlayer` (a shove; not in a safe zone), every non-remote foe in reach via `damageFoe(..., false)` (chains possible); then the collapse as before. Drones (`damageFoe` death), mounted turrets (`damageMountedTurret`, through the `setTurretBlast` hook that `world/blast.ts` sets) and raider wrecks (`wreckRaider`) blast at once. MP: every game that plays a wreck plays its blast and hurts only its own player and the foes it runs itself, so nobody is hit twice; nothing new is sent. Labyrinths: `gen/dungeonguards.ts` `labyrinthGuards(map, depth)` (pure, own stream `map.seed ^ 0x6a7d31`; `DEPTH_GUARDS` per depth: room chance 0.35 / 0.5 / 0.65, a second robot 0.12 / 0.3 / 0.5, mix depth 1 scout / guardian, 2 + repair / sentinel, 3 guardian / sentinel / assault / repair / artillery; not the first room or a boss room; `BIG` sizes keep heavy ones out of small rooms); `loadDungeon` always sets the robots' env indoors, spawns them with `spawnAuthority` at level `max(1, danger) + (depth − 1) × 0.5` (wrecks as before) and `populate` records their kills (`GUARD + i`); `labyrinthDrones` = rooms + 3 + depth (was 2·rooms + 2 + depth). - The body in first person (0.191; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `core/sway.ts` (pure) `SWAY` (stride 1.5 / run 2.1 m, walk bob 3.2 cm, side 2.2 cm, pitch / yaw / roll, `run` ×2.6, `crouch` ×0.55, `breathe` 0.0028 rad at `breatheRate`, `winded` ×3.2 by missing stamina, `aimKeep` 0.3, `lag` / `lagEase` for the gun), `stepSway(s, {speed, ground, run, crouch, aim, winded}, dt)` → camera offsets (`up`, `right`) and angles (`pitch`, `yaw`, `roll`) plus `step` / `side` for the hands. `world/weapons.ts` `updateBodySway(dt)` (speed from `G.vel`, sprint = `G.activity === BURN.sprint`, aim from `aimK`) is called by main.ts on foot only (not flying, driving, riding, boating), which adds the offsets to the camera (`G.yaw` / `G.pitch` stay as the player set them); shots leave along the camera, so the sway makes aiming on the move harder. `animateVM` bobs the gun and melee weapon by the stride and trails the view's turns (`lagX` / `lagY`), less while aiming. Tests: `tests/sway.test.ts`. MP: local view only, nothing sent.

- Convoy routes (0.187, `PLAN_PLACOWEK.md` stage O6; `PROTOCOL` unchanged: inside the shared `outposts` and `towns`): `gen/routes.ts` (pure) `ROUTE` (reach 25 km, detour 1.3, speed 12 m a game minute, load 30 min, `cap` scout 8 / mastodon 30, robbery 3% + 2.5% × danger half way (max 35%, × 0.6 with a roof cannon), a robbery takes the cargo and 30% of the hull, `catchup` 200 trips), `Route` (`OutpostState.route`: village id / name / middle, km, `truck` {id, model, parts, by} (a vehicle taken off its owner's `char.vehicles` by `takeVehicle` in `world/vehicles.ts`; `recallTruck` + `parkVehicle` give it back to whoever recalls), `t0` the timetable's start, `k` the next trip, `food`, `sent` cumulative crates delivered, `fed` cumulative food units taken in, `last`, `robbed`), `newRoute`, `legOf`, `periodOf`, `tripFuel` (from the truck's tank: `fuelTruck` pours canisters), `robChance`, `truckProblem` (no EVs yet, not a wreck), `convoyAt` (outpost / out / village / back / parked), `settleRoute` (trips that reached the village in order: the stock settled to departure, up to the load of the outpost's goods, fuel burnt, a hashed robbery; a dry tank misses the trip, a wreck stops it; from `settleAll`, which now takes `towns`), `drainRoutes` (under the village's lock from `stockAt` in `ui/stock.ts`: `TownState.rcv[route]` cumulative crates taken into the hall by `deposit`, what does not fit waits (`waiting`); food for `food` routes from the stock into `TownState.ship[route]` cumulative units, up to the truck's load and the pantry's room), `takeFood` (the outpost side: shipped − `fed` into the pantry). Two cumulative counters, each written by one side only, so stale writes delay but never double a delivery. `ui/outpost.ts` `routeHTML` (the 4 nearest villages in reach, `data-op` route / unroute / truck:<id> (your vehicles within 80 m, trunk empty) / rfuel / rfood / recall). `world/convoyroutes.ts`: `routeEnds`, `truckAt`, `updateRouteTrucks` (main loop: each route's truck drawn by `convoyModel` within 600 m along the straight way, tilted when wrecked; not solid), `clearRouteTrucks` (closeWorld). `partName` / `partNeeds` take the standing tier) and 'turret' (`TURRET`: turretkit + wire + scrap + circuit, up to 2, `turretsOf`), both `ManyPart`; `OutpostState.rk` (the last raid settled), `raids` (k → outcome fought live), `rlast`. `gen/outpostraids.ts` (pure): `OUTRAID` (period 3–6 days from the stake's `t`, warn 60, duration 120 min, none below danger `from` 1, `hold` by wall tier 0.3 / 0.55 / 0.75 / 0.88, + 0.06 a turret, + 0.008 a person up to 0.1, − 0.05 per strength over 2; a loss takes 60% of the crates, 50% of the pantry, 20% of the people), `strengthAt` (`dangerAt`), `outRaid`, `nextRaid`, `worthRobbing` (digs or people), `holdChance`, `raidResult` (the fought outcome, else a hash roll), `settleRaids` (every raid over by now in order: settles stock and life to its end first, then plunders; nothing to rob = passes by). `gen/grid.ts` `settleAll` runs them first and returns what was settled (`tellRaids` in `world/lodes.ts` logs it); the outpost window settles before taking crates. `gen/outpostlife.ts` `PESTS` (no wall: fields × 0.75, a 25% daily chance the pantry loses 20%). `world/lodes.ts`: `outpostSpots(l).wallR` (r + 38), `.gate` (the post's direction), `.turrets` (± 90°, r + 35); `drawWall` (leaning stakes and rails; pointed timbers with a fill; stone faces, top, courses, merlons; gate posts or towers), `Live.wall` ring collision open at the gate in `lodeHit`, `drawTurret` (a timber tower, the head its own object, `OutGun`), `outpostGuns`, `outpostRing`. `world/outpostraid.ts`: `updateOutpostRaids` (main loop; within `ORAID.reach` 450 m: a warning an hour before, then with `spawnAuthority` two waves of bandits from a hashed direction making for the nearest point outside the wall (or the middle); bandits within 9 m of it drain the defences by wall tier `ORAID.drain`; all dead → won (gold, xp), defences 0 → lost, the time over → by the defences; recorded in `OutpostState.raids` under `withOutpost`; leaving past 700 m drops it (then rolled)), the turrets (`OUT_GUN` range 80, only the authority's game fires; the others sweep), `outpostRaidLine` (tracker), `forceOutpostRaid` (console `oraid`, k 0, not recorded), `clearOutpostRaid` (closeWorld). The window: wall and turret rows, `defenceHTML` (strength, hold chance, next raid, last outcome, the animals). Tests: `tests/outpostraids.test.ts`.
- People at the outposts (0.185, `PLAN_PLACOWEK.md` stage O4; `PROTOCOL` unchanged: inside the shared `outposts`): `gen/outposts.ts` parts 'bunks' (`BUNKS`: planks, nails, stone, scrap; 10 h, up to 3) and 'field' (`FIELD`: planks, nails, rope, scrap; 8 h, up to 3), built more than once (`ManyPart`, `isMany`, counted in `OutpostState.n`; `countOf`, `maxOf`; `partProblem` 'All 3 stand.'); `finishDue` starts `OutpostState.life` on the two-hour mark the first one stood. `gen/outpostlife.ts` (pure): `LIFE` (step 120 min, catch-up 60 days, beds 8 a bunkhouse, `fieldCrew` 3, `field` 4 food a day × `fertility(world, o.x, o.z)` × the crew's fill, `eat` = `FOOD.eat`, pantry 30 / shed 90, `arrive` 0.25 a step, `leave` 10% a hungry step, `reserve` 2 days or fields making it with `margin` 1.1, `digCrew` 6, `boost` 0.5, `hamlet` 2 bunks + 2 fields + power + 16 people), `OutpostLife {t, n, food, fed, v}` stepped only on whole marks (`lifeNow` without writing, `settleLife`), so it is the same however often and on whichever game it is settled; `crews` (fields first, then the dig), `foodMade`, `foodEaten`, `loadFood` (food units by `FOOD.value`), `crewFactor` (multiplies `pwOf` and the two-hourly `settleAll`, which now also steps the people), `isHamlet`. `gen/lodes.ts` `LODE.clear` 40: small trees, rocks and plants keep that much further off a deposit (room for fields). `ui/outpost.ts`: bunkhouse and field rows, `peopleHTML` (people, beds, who works where, land, pantry, hunger, the hamlet rule), `data-op="food"` loads every food carried. `world/lodes.ts`: `outpostSpots(l).bunks` / `.fields`, `drawBunks`, `drawField` (fence with a gate, furrows and crop rows), `drawHands` (up to 12 settler figures: hoes on the fields, picks or hammers at the dig, idle by the bunkhouse; posed in `updateLodes`); `sigOf` includes the counts and the people; `finishOutposts` settles everything up to every finished build. Maps: the outpost label shows the people, a hamlet a double ring. MP: every game steps the life from the shared `outposts` the same way; food and builds go under the outpost's lock. Tests: `tests/outpostlife.test.ts`.
- Power grid (0.184, `PLAN_PLACOWEK.md` stage O3; `PROTOCOL` unchanged: inside the shared `outposts`): `gen/grid.ts` (pure) `POWER_SRC` (generator 40 kW / r 350 / fuel 180 min a can, bunker 20; wind 60 × `windAt` / r 500; solar 45 × `daylight` / r 250; coal 90 / r 700 / coal 120 min, bunker 20: name, text, needs), `POWER_KINDS`, `DIG_KW` (quarry 8, logging 6, mine 12, pump 10, shaft 25), `GRID` (unpowered 0.5, step 120 min, 12 samples), `fuelLeft` / `loadFuel` (`OutpostState.fuel {n, t}`, burns while it has any), `supplyAt`, `demandOf`, `reachOf`, `grids(outposts)` (union-find: sources whose circles meet, or that reach one outpost; outpost id → grid number, off-grid ones absent), `gridState` (supply, demand, share per grid), `powerShare` (the mean share over the last day on whole two-hour marks), `powerFactor` (0.5 + 0.5 × share), `pwOf`, `settleAll`. `gen/outposts.ts`: part 'power' (`OutpostState.pk`, `choosePower` only before anything is handed over, `partName` / `partNeeds` take `pk`, hours 8, xp 60), `stockNow` / `settle` / `takeCrates` / `finishDue` take the factor `pw` (default 1). `ui/outpost.ts`: the source choice (`data-op="pk:<kind>"`), the build, the fuel bunker (`data-op="fuel"`, after `settleAll`), the grid line (`powerHTML`). `world/lodes.ts`: `drawPowerSource` at `outpostSpots(l).power` (generator box with exhaust and drums, a lattice turbine whose `rotor` turns by `windAt`, panel rows, a brick boiler house with a stack and a coal heap), `finishOutposts` settles every outpost each two-hour mark (`bucket`) and before a source is finished. Maps: a dashed pale ring per source's reach. `data/uses.ts` and SUROWCE list the sources. MP: every client works the grid out of the shared `outposts` the same way; the two-hourly settle writes the same values everywhere. Not yet: villages and settlements on the grid. Tests: `tests/grid.test.ts`.
- Outposts (0.183, `PLAN_PLACOWEK.md` stage O2, `PROTOCOL` 13): `gen/outposts.ts` (pure) `OutpostState` {o owner ('crew' in co-op; a pid for O8), by, t, k, x, z, name, done {dig, store}, given, job {p, t, h}, stock {t, n}} saved in the shared `Char.outposts[lode id]` (`SHARED` and the server's `FIELDS`; the server's `wlock` takes 'outposts'; `lockTown(k, value, f)` / `unlockTown(k, value, f)` in `net/client.ts` keep an outpost's lock in `activeTowns` as 'outposts:<id>'; `withOutpost` in `ui/stock.ts`). `digOf(k)` quarry / logging / mine / pump / shaft, `DIG` (name, needs, text), `STORE` (Storage shed), `OUTPOST` (rate 0.5 crates an hour × richness, rare 0.12, second good × 0.4, `pile` 6 without a shed, `cap` 60 with one, hours dig 10 / store 8, xp 80 / 40), `newOutpost`, `mayUse`, `outputs`, `capOf`, `stockNow` (closed form from the anchor), `settle`, `takeCrates`, `partPlan`, `partProblem` (one build at a time), `handOver` (bit by bit; complete → `job`), `finishDue` (settles at the job's end with the old cap; the dig starts the stock anchor), `bare` (pull up the stake). `lodeById(world, id)` in `gen/lodes.ts`. `world/lodes.ts`: `drawOutpost` (8 stakes, a post with a board at `outpostSpots(l).post`, building sites (stakes, tape, a frame rising with the job, a shrinking stack), a timber or steel headframe with a sheave, winch house and ore cart, a pumpjack and tank, a quarry derrick with cut blocks, logging trestles and a log stack, the plank shed, crate stacks by the stock), `Live.boxes` / `sig` (redrawn when builds, the job's tenth or the stock's fives change), `finishOutposts` (every 0.5 s, whoever sees it first), `nearOutpostLode` (within r + 6, not by a vehicle) → E in `world/interact.ts` → `ui/outpost.ts` `openOutpost` / `outpostClick` (`data-op`: stake, give:<part>, take:<good>:<n> (trunks first via `putAway(..., true)`, what finds no room stays), drop). Maps: a gold ring per outpost (`drawArea`). `data/uses.ts` and SUROWCE list the builds. MP: shared state under the outpost's lock; finishing and production are deterministic, so every game agrees. Tests: `tests/outposts.test.ts`.
- Towns worlds (0.182, `PLAN_PLACOWEK.md` stage O1; old worlds unchanged): `gen/worldrules.ts` `townsWorld(world)` = a negative seed (pure, so workers, the server and every player agree), `newWorldSeed()` (newChar, the menu's reroll; the server rolls `-randomInt` for 'main' and new rooms; a typed seed keeps its sign). `gen/regions.ts`: in a towns world `candidate` skips the chance roll and `villageOfCell` keeps only `townCells` (`TOWNS` n 20, the first `near` 2 in `ring` 3.5–7 km by hash then farthest from each other, the rest by farthest-point sampling from Gridholm and the picks; cell key 'gx:gz'). `gen/roads.ts` `townNetwork` (Prim's tree from Gridholm + each town's 1–2 nearest (Gridholm 3) within `TOWN_ROAD.link` 24 km; a link whose straight line is > `TOWN_ROAD.wet` 6% over the sea (40 samples) is left out, the tree weighs sea share ×12; ~150 ms an edge). `initializeSettlements`: only Gridholm becomes a settlement; the towns get `wall` from `TOWN_WALL` [1, 1, 2] by hash. Contracts off in towns worlds (`localOffers` / `boardContracts` empty, `shownNow('contracts')` false). Deposits in the wilds (`gen/lodes.ts`, pure, none in old worlds): `LODES` (stone, grove, coal, iron, copper, lead, oil and every `RARES` kind: name, `gives`, weight, `from` m, cleared `r`, colour, blurb), `LODE` (chance 6% a region, 10% within 4 km; keeps 500 m from villages, places + 30, roads + 14, rivers + 18, lakes, fog sites, cities, the sea, mountains (> 0.35, groves / oil > 0.12), steep > 6 m across r), `startLodes` (stone, grove, coal, iron, copper 0.8–2.6 km from Gridholm, ids 'lode:s<i>'), `regionLode` (cached per canonical region, id 'lode:<rx>:<rz>'; kind by weight × hills for ores), `Lode {k, x, z, y, r, yaw, rich 0.6–1.6, mineral (stone), salt (oil), name '<Kind> at <A> <B>'}`, `lodesNear`, `inLode` (trees, rocks and plants keep off), `lodesWithin` (nearest first), `lodeGives`. `world/lodes.ts`: `updateLodes` (main loop: drawn within 520 m: stone mound and cut blocks, giant trees, an ore ring with veins in `LODES[k].c`, `oilSeep` pools), found within r + 70 → `Char.lodes[id] = [x, z, k, name, 1]` (personal, like the map), toast + log; `lodeHit` (G.obstacle), `lodeName` (placeName), `dropLodes` (closeWorld), `scanLodes` (the orbital scan), `askLode` (the 'rumour' option in a towns world: the nearest unmarked deposit within 9 km of the town, marked heard (0, dashed on the map)). Maps: squares in the deposit's colour (`drawArea`). Fertility (`gen/fertility.ts`): `fertility(world, x, z)` 0.4–1.8 (0 on the sea / ice) from slow noise (2.6 km), low ground, rivers within 180 / 600 m, mountains, the coast (450 m), cold past 16 km, slope; `fertilityWord`, `fertilityColor`; the satellite map's F layer (`fertLayer`, `toggleFertility`, 128 m cells over explored ground, 8 ms a frame) and a footer line. MP: everything from the seed; found deposits and the layer are personal (Not synced); `PROTOCOL` unchanged. Tests: `tests/towns.test.ts`.
- Natural ground (0.181, the owner's rule: nothing levelled or marked round a village until it is built; eased, not table-flat): `gen/buildpads.ts` `buildSites(seed, industry, s)` = only the sites in use (an old village's power plant, built industry site and hall; works / station plots per `plants` / `stations` + the one in `pbuild`; a settlement's power plant once `projectBegun`), `projectBegun(s, k)` (done, a job running or materials given), `SITE_PAD` {flat 2, blend 16, soft 0.3}, `meanGround(world, rect)` (the pad's height = its own mean natural ground, cached), `sitePads(world, v, s)`. `Pad.soft` / `Claim.soft` (gen/terrain.ts, gen/claims.ts) keep that share of the relief inside a pad; fields `FIELD` blend 14, soft 0.3; the warehouse pad (`VEHICLE_HALL`) stays flat. `world/overworld.ts` `villagePads` / `padsOf` (settlement yards only once begun), `syncVillagePads` (main loop, once a second: a village whose pads changed gets them, `rebuildChunksNear` 420 m and `reloadStruct`). No stakes or signs until begun: `drawSettlementSites` skips unbegun plots (natural deposits stay), `drawWorks` / `drawStations` skip empty plots (only the one going up), `drawPower` / `drawIndustry` unbuilt sites draw no stakes. Trees come back where pads are gone (the felled-tree keys there may point at another tree). Next (owner): free placement of every building with a stake. MP: from the shared `towns`, the same on every game; `PROTOCOL` unchanged.
- Electric drive (0.180, stage P4 of the fuel plan): `data/vehicles.ts` `VehicleParts.ev` (kWh in the battery; present = converted, `fuel` unused) / `evPack`, `EV` {scout cap 30 use 0.2, mastodon cap 100 use 0.6 kWh a km at full throttle, `pack` ×2, `price` 3 g a kWh, `idle` 0.0004 kWh/s, `start` 0.25 of the kit's charge}, `isEV`, `evCap`, `evBurn`, `evRangeKm`, `energyOf(m, p)` → [now, full, km, 'L' | 'kWh']; `outOfFuel` reads the battery for an EV. Items `evkit` (Electric Drive Kit, order tech 'evdrive' Electric Drive appended to `TECHS`: motor + 4 batteries + control + 4 cable) and `evpack` (Lithium Battery Pack, order tech 'powercells': 2 powercell + control + 2 cable). `ui/service.ts` slots 'ev' (drop the kit: `electrify`; take it out: diesel again, the pack first) and 'evp' (the pack; taking it out loses its charge); `world/vehicles.ts` `consumeFuel` burns kWh for an EV, `dryWarn` 'Battery flat', `pourCanister` / `refuelVehicle` refuse an EV, the HUD via `energyOf`; the fuel pump skips EVs. Settlement `Project` 'charger' (Charging post: 8 cable, 4 steel, control, 2 batteries, 6 planks; 10 h; after the power plant; in `optionalProjects` once power stands), `RESOURCE_PLOTS.charger` (176, −60), `CHARGER` {kw 40, reach 10, near 2.4, posts}; `world/settlement.ts` draws two pillars with lit screens and coiled cables, a transformer, a cable trench. `world/charging.ts` `updateCharging` (main loop, every second; the import sits last in main.ts, an earlier place broke the module order): your EVs within `reach` of a built post take up to `kw` of the village's spare power (`balance().free`, shared among the vehicles at that post) at `EV.price`; `nearCharger` / `chargerPrompt` (the prompt at a pillar: spare kW or what is charging). Established villages have no post. MP: the owner's game charges the owner's vehicle and gold; the spare power is read, not written (two players at one post each see all of it: not synced); `PROTOCOL` unchanged. Tests: `tests/evdrive.test.ts`.

- Convoy fuel (0.178, stage P2 of the fuel plan): `gen/caravans.ts` `CONVOY_FUEL` {lpkm 2 (two jeeps and the truck), can 20}, `convoyCans(c)` = ⌈road km × 2 / 20⌉, at least 1 (median village ~2.3 canisters a day, a refinery makes 12); `caravanShift` takes each departure's canisters off its home's `fuel` (the market's price signal, every village). Settlements (`progressive`): `TownState.convoy` {t, dry: [caravan id, t0][]} (shared), `settleConvoys(world, v, seed, s, now)` in `gen/hall.ts` (departures since `t` in order, each takes its cans from the stock (hold first) or stays home in `dry`; the first call only starts the count; `dry` kept `CONVOY_KEEP` 3 days), `stayedHome`, `convoyNeed` (cans a day from the timetable); settled from `stockAt` (ui/stock.ts), the hall window, `updateFarms` (after `refineStock`, logs 'A convoy from X stayed home') and `world/convoyfuel.ts` `dryCaravan(c)` (settles the home when the departure is not counted yet), which `world/caravans.ts` and the big map use to leave dry caravans out. Not synced / not done: a dry caravan's cargo still moves the destination's market price (`caravanShift` is pure).
- Vehicle fuel (0.177, stage P1 of the fuel plan: P2 convoys burn fuel from the departing village, P3 a fuel pump in villages, P4 electric drive with chargers and battery packs, P5 electric convoys): `data/vehicles.ts` `fuelUse` Scout 0.5 / Mastodon 1.5 L a km at full throttle, `FUEL` {cruise 0.55 of it without throttle, idle 0.002 L a second at the wheel, can 20 L}, `fuelBurn(m, metres, thr, secs)`, `rangeKm` (at half pedal: ~155 / ~190 km a tank), `cansToFill`, `outOfFuel`; `FUEL_BURN` is gone and `immobile` no longer counts fuel (you can sit in a dry vehicle). `world/vehicles.ts` `consumeFuel` (only the driver's game, seat 0; low-fuel line at 10%), a dry tank zeroes the throttle (`dryWarn`), `pourCanister(v)` (trunk first, then `takeOne`), `refuelVehicle` (R in or beside your own vehicle; on foot by a full tank R reloads), HUD litres and km. `ui/service.ts` pours a clicked or dropped `fuel`. - The metal ladder (0.173, the owner's order of worth, step 1 of 4: scrap and wire, heavy works with the drill rig, the rare-deposit mine, furnaces): ore < scrap < ingots < steel < semi-products < assemblies, each step ~×1.25–1.5. `BasicMat` adds 'scrap' (5) and 'wire' (7) to `BASIC_VALUE`; `Made` = `Good | 'planks' | 'wire'` (`Recipe.out`, `PlantState.out`); the Smelter appends 12 scrap + 1 coal → 2 iron, the Wire Mill appends 1 iron → 12 wire (indexes kept); the blacksmith's 'furnace' order is 1 iron → 8 wire (was 1 iron ore → 4). - The sawmill (0.170, development tree stage 2): settlement projects 'sawmill' (Sawmill: 20 planks, 8 stone, 6 scrap, 4 wire; after power, a `tutorialStep` between power and the warehouse), 'sawmill2' (circular saws, after the sawmill and the warehouse) and 'sawmill3' (band saws: 2 motor, 3 parts, 4 steel, 4 cable), `sawLevel(s)`, `SAW` {perLog [0, 6, 7, 8], kw [0, 15, 25, 40], batch 30, logs 2}, `HAND_PLANKS` 4 (gen/settlement.ts); `optionalProjects(s)` (after the warehouse: the refinery, the sawmill's next saws) is what the elder's panel offers beside the step. `RESOURCE_PLOTS.sawmill` (−80, −80); `JOBS.sawmill` 3 (a yard post after power); `siteKw` + `SAW.kw[level]`; `sawStock` in `gen/hall.ts` (beside `refineStock`, from `stockAt` and the hall window): `SAW.logs` logs a `SAW.batch` minutes × the crew's fill × `sitePower`, taken from the stock (hold first), planks deposited into the hold; `settlement.sawnAt` (set when built in `finishJob`). `world/settlement.ts` draws the saw shed (bench, frame saw, log deck, planks; a circular saw at level 2, the band saw and its motor at 3); `jobsites` puts the saw upgrades on the sawmill's plot. Hand sawing (`world/sawing.ts`): `sawLogs` starts a job, `updateSawing` (main loop) saws a log every `HAND.secs` 15 s while you stay within 1.5 m (stamina 18, kcal 35 each), `sawingHint` in the prompt. The Sawmill works' log recipe gives 12 planks for 2 logs. The elder's panel names the mineral and the salt. MP: pure of the world seed and the shared `towns`; `PROTOCOL` unchanged.

Minds: bandits (`sight` 40; idle: suspicious → face, ≥ 0.6 → state 'search' (walk to `last`, look round, `LOSE.bandit` then 'return'); fight: shares while seen, lost > 1.5 s → 'search'; noise → search the spot (fighting: `heard`); a kill makes group members within 25 m search the body, a hit `alert(b, G.pos)`), robots (patrol: walk over to scan a contact ≥ 0.5; hunting: lost > 1 s → sweep `last`, give up after `LOSE.robot`; silent kill → squad within 30 m `heard` the wreck), creatures (`spots` for ravager / gnawer / investigate / skitter, bramble 11 m × vis, leechwing SIGHT × vis ≥ 0.6; `track` while hunting → 'investigate' the last spot after `LOSE.creature`), drones (`sense` all round, chase the last spot, give up after `LOSE.drone`). - Start and the menu (0.152, `PROTOCOL` 10): `index.html` starts with `#boot` (black, 'loading the world…') and `#menu` hidden; main.ts removes the boot screen when `ui/cinematic.ts` loads and plays the opening film, then `showMenu(false)` (after `backToOwnWorld`'s reload, `sessionStorage` 'gridWorld.noFilm' skips it once). The menu's first row is `#start` (`labels()` in `ui/menu.ts`: 'Single player' / 'Play online' / 'Resume' when `paused`) and `#mpOpen` 'Multiplayer' (`showMpBox` in `ui/mp.ts`; `#mpBox` hidden until opened, always shown while online (`mpShown`), `#mpClose`; `#menu.mp` hides the blurb, keys and seed; `onMpChange` relabels). Single player with a waiting solo save (`soloWaiting`) restores it (`backToOwnWorld`). Deleting servers: `create.key` = this browser's secret (`mpKey`, localStorage 'gridWorld.mpKey'), the server keeps `owner` = its sha256 (`ownerOf`) and `by` (the creator's name, listed); `myRooms` (localStorage 'gridWorld.mpRooms') = rooms this browser created → a `data-del` Delete button (click twice; not for 'main' or the room you are in); `deleteRoom(url, room, key)` in `net/client.ts` sends {t:'delroom', ver, room, key} instead of a hello → server `deleteRoom` (players get `refused` 'deleted by its creator', the room goes, `opts.removed(id)` → server/main.mjs unlinks `world-<id>.json` and saves; also for rooms forgotten after `roomTtl`) → {t:'deleted'} or {t:'refused', why}.
- Document 04 stages D2 and D3 (0.151): settlement `Project` 'relay' (Relay mast: 12 scrap, 10 wire, 4 circuit, 8 log, 2 gears; `projectProblem`: not at a station village, needs the village's comms; built at the receiver's console (`developmentHTML` atComms, `relayOpen`), refused while `satelliteUp` is false), `RELAY` {reach 4000, r 4500}, `hasRelay` in `gen/settlement.ts`; `drawSettlementComms` adds a guyed lattice mast with a dish over the receiver. `scanPlan()` in `ui/worldmap.ts`: a scan centred within `RELAY.reach` of a relay village uses that village's `scanAt` and radius. `planRoute(world, sx, sz, tx, tz)` in `gen/roads.ts` (Dijkstra over `network` edges by straight length, `edgePath` only for the edges taken, a failed edge struck out and retried; the polyline shifted per leg onto the right copy of the planet) → `toggleRoute` (key R on the open map, needs `gpsOn`), `route` drawn dashed in `drawArea`, `routeAhead()` (a point ~120 m on along the route; arriving within 60 m clears it) marked 'Route' on the compass. Runtime only, yours alone. Map tiles: `tile(cx, cz, budget)` works out at most `TILE_BUDGET` 6 new tiles / 6 ms per drawn map, nearest first (the 3×3 round you always), a dim square meanwhile. Not synced: routes.
- Document 04 stage D1 (0.150): `satelliteUp(home)` (a new world whose start village has its link), `ORBIT` {r 3000, every 360}, `scanWait`, `SettlementState.scanAt` (shared, Gridholm's) in `gen/settlement.ts`. `ui/worldmap.ts`: `gpsOn()` (tablet or the link; used by the compass strip's GPS line in `ui/compass.ts` and the right-click waypoint), `orbitalScan()` (key O while the map is open, `ui/input.ts`: discovers every POI and installation within `ORBIT.r` of the waypoint or you, adds fog zones to `char.fogs`, sets `scanAt`; a dashed ring for 2 game hours), the satellite footer (next pass, `weatherAt` now and in 4 h). Not synced: a scan's discoveries go on the scanner's map only. D2 relay masts and D3 road routes are planned in `PLAN_POJAZDY.md`.
- Document 04 stages B and C (0.149): `SettlementState.station` (set for Gridholm by `initializeSettlements` while its comms is not done), `stage`, `car`. `STATION_STAGES` (3, salvage), `isStation`, `stationStage`, `STATION_RANGE` [2000, 6000] (`commsRuin(world, v, station)` picks the nearest ruin in that ring; `linkRuin(world, v, s)`), `projectPlan` gives the current stage's needs, `buildProject` returns `stage` (a stage done) and advances `settlement.stage`; `projectProblem('comms')` for the station wants 2 farms and `car`; `tutorialStep` (station villages): farm 2 → 'Your first vehicle' (`car`, marker at Kuba's yard in `settlementMarker`) → the station → the rest; `farmProblem` lets the second farm wait for nothing in station villages. `car` is set by `updateGarage` once you own any vehicle. `world/settlement.ts` `drawStation` (by stage: fallen mast and rubble, walled bunker and stump, generator shed and cables, mast with dish and lamps; sign), `nearSettlementComms` / `drawSettlementComms` reach 6.4 km for it; `ui/settlement.ts` shows the stages, rewards each (`station-n`), on completion `stationSweep` discovers every place within 5 km. `ui/worldmap.ts` `mapLocked()` (Gridholm a station and comms not done): the big map draws the glove's NO SATELLITE LINK screen; the minimap is unchanged (owner's choice). `townLook` includes `settlement.stage`.
- Document 04 stage A (0.148, `PLAN_POJAZDY.md` from the owner's "Exploration, the mechanic, vehicles and satellites"): Kuba's title is 'Mechanic'; in a new world (`progressive`) his yard opens at `development` ≥ 2 (was 3). `data/garage.ts` `GARAGE` (Scout: 24 scrap, 4 parts, 4 gears, 2 engine, 3 circuit, 8 h; Mastodon: + steel, 14 h; 2 repair kits, 2 h), `GARAGE_MAX` 2; the dealer's option 'garage' → `ui/garage.ts` (`garageHTML` / `garageClick`, `data-garn` order, `data-gar` collect; materials from the village stock via `stockHas` / `stockTake`; `char.garage` {w, v, i, done, n?, told}, personal; a vehicle is parked by `parkNew` / `yardBay` in `world/vehicles.ts`, `buyVehicle` uses them; `updateGarage` in the main loop tells you once). In a new world the vehicle shop lists no vehicles and no engine parts (`renderVehicleShop`). Salvage: `parts` in robots' loot (guardian, sentinel, assault), ruin and ship chests (`rollChest`), gears / parts / engine in abandoned vehicles' trunks (`claim`). `data/uses.ts` and `tools/materials.sim.ts` list the workshop. Tests: `tests/garage.test.ts`.
- Industry document stage 6 (0.147, the space program): market goods `avionics` (6000) and `lifesup` (3400); works 'avionworks' Avionics Works (tech 'avionics': computer + 2 sensor + control → avionics, `DRAW` 70) and 'lifeworks' Life Support Works (tech 'lifesupport': pump + 2 pipes + 2 chems + titanium → lifesup, `DRAW` 60); `BasicMat` adds 'sensor' (500). `INSTALL_MORE.aerospace` = rocket engines (2 ancalloy + 2 precision + ceramics + pump → `rocketeng`, 480 min, bay 4) gated by `InstallWork.tech` 'rocketry' (`setInstallRec(..., known)` refuses it without the plans; the desk shows why). Techs 'avionics', 'lifesupport', 'rocketry' appended. `ANCIENT_GOODS` adds composite, aerocomp, rocketeng. Chariot `STAGES`: hull + 6 aerocomp + 4 composite, engines + 3 rocketeng, avionics + 3 avionics, power + 2 pcm, new 'life' Life Support (4 lifesup, 2 composite, computer) before 'fuel' (7 stages; `CHARIOT_WEIGHTS` 7 entries); `fixShuttle` v3 marks 'life' done when the old six were all done. `drawShuttle` adds two tanks and a lit hose once 'life' is done. Ship chests roll an avionics rack 3%. `world/works.ts` draws both works. Campaign sim: ~262 h alone (186 before), balance left for the paused stage 8.
- Industry document stage 5 (0.146, the complexes): `INSTALLS` append 'aerospace' Old Aerospace Works (20–65 km, r 34; `INSTALL_WORK` 2 titanium + 2 aluminium + composite + advsteel → `aerocomp` Aerospace Components per 360 min, hopper 20, bay 10, draw 220; stage III tech 'aerospace') and 'powerplex' Ancient Power Complex (12–65 km, r 34; no `INSTALL_WORK` / `INSTALL_DRAW`; stage III tech 'powergrid'); both techs appended to `TECHS` and `VILLAGE_TECHS`. The grid (`gen/installs.ts`): `POWERPLEX` (kw 400, reach 16 km, burn 2880 min a crate of `nfuel`, bunker 8), the core's rods are `pw.nfuel` at anchor `t` (`plexLeft`, `plexUntil`, `fuelPlex`), `gridFor(sites, installs, k)` = until when a plant in reach is powered; `hallPick` / `canRun` / `runInstall` / `loadInstall` / `fuelHall` / `hallKw` take that `grid` time (a batch that ends before it runs on the pseudo-set `GRID`, `HallSet.grid`, burning nothing). `ui/install.ts` passes `gridOf(k)` everywhere, the complex's desk (`plex`: rods, hours left, the plants on its lines, `data-ins="rods"`), a grid line over the power hall. `INSTALL_MORE.alloy` appends composite (2 plastic + 2 cloth + titanium → 2 `composite` Composite Sheets, off-market); compVest + 1 composite. `world/installs.ts`: `tubeX`, `drawAerospace` (arched hangar with a fuselage on jigs, wind tunnel on piers with bell mouth and fan house, engine test stand), `pylon` and `drawPowerplex` (domed reactor, two turbine halls with stacks, switchyard, three pylons with lines); `DESKS` / `HALLS` (the complex has no power hall).
- Industry document stage 4 (0.145, electronics): goods appended (proc, market) `silicon` 170, `control` 900, `optics` 520, `hpe` 1250, `computer` 2400, `pcm` 3000. `PLANT_KINDS` append 'siliconworks' Silicon Processing (tech 'semiconductors': 2 glass + chems → 2 silicon, `DRAW` 110) and 'advelec' Advanced Electronics Works (tech 'computing': 2 copperbar + 2 silicon + pipes → hpe; control + microchip + 2 silicon → computer; hpe + control → pcm; `DRAW` 90); both techs appended to `TECHS` and `VILLAGE_TECHS`. Recipes appended: Electronics Shop boards + circuit + cable → control (index 1), Glassworks 2 glass + aluminium → optics (index 1). `BasicMat` adds 'circuit' (salvage) and 'microchip' (`BASIC_VALUE` 60 / 450). Salvage: robots (`loot`: control, optics, hpe), ship chests (`rollChest`), fog lockers (`lockerLoot` keys 19–23). Uses: reactor station + 2 pcm, `IMPROVE.bank` + pcm, `.automation` + 2 control, `.sights` + 2 optics, `PLANT_LEVELS[3]` + 2 control + hpe, `RADAR_UP` + computer, scanner + optics, exoH + pcm. `world/works.ts` draws a furnace hall with crucible towers and acid tanks, and a clean hall with lit windows, roof air units, a transformer and a dish.
- Industry document stages 2 and 3 (0.144, steel stays without coke by the owner's word): `PlantKind` / `PLANT_KINDS` append 'stoneworks' (tier 1: 3 stone → 2 `cutstone`, `DRAW` 15) and 'metallurgy' (tech 'metallurgy' Advanced Metallurgy appended to `TECHS` and `VILLAGE_TECHS`; 2 steel + nickel + chromite → 2 `advsteel`, 2 rutile + chems → `titanium`, 2 pgmore + chems → `pgm`; `DRAW` 140; needs a generator and a pump); `Stuff` adds `BasicMat` 'stone' (`BASIC_VALUE`). `RARES` appends 'pgmore', 'rutile', 'chromite' (Platinum Ore, Rutile Sand, Chromite Ore), rolled apart by `metalOf` (salt 0x3e7a1, a village's second deposit; `depositOf` keeps only the first 6, `OLD_RARES`), `depositsOf` = both (the share panel and the terminal list both, one daily `rareLeft` allowance). Goods `cutstone`, `advsteel`, `titanium`, `pgm` (proc, market). Uses: `FORTIFY[1]` + cutstone 20, coalplant + cutstone 10, `IMPROVE.armour` + titanium 4 + advsteel 6, exoH order + titanium 2 + advsteel 2, scanner + pgm, `RADAR_UP` + pgm 2. `world/works.ts` draws a stone saw shed with blocks and chippings, and an arc furnace hall with three electrode furnaces, retorts and a stack.
- Industry document stage 1 (0.143, `PLAN_PRZEMYSLU.md` from the owner's "Raw materials, industry, components and technologies"): goods appended to `Good` / `GOODS` (proc, market): `motor` (540), `generator` (1080), `pipes` (200), `gears` (175), `pump` (1300). Works 'electrical' Electrical Works (appended to `PlantKind` / `PLANT_KINDS`, tech 'electricity' Electric Machines appended to `TECHS` and `VILLAGE_TECHS`; motor ← copperbar + steel + plastic, generator ← 2 copperbar + steel + parts; `DRAW` 50; drawn in world/works.ts: brick winding hall, a generator on a test bed (glow), a transformer yard). Machine Shop recipes appended (indexes kept): pipes ×2 ← steel + copperbar, gears ← steel, pump ← steel + parts + motor. Salvage: robots' `loot` (gears, motor, pipes, generator, pump by kind), ship chests (`rollChest`). Uses: windfarm + 2 gears, dieselbank + 2 generator, coalplant + generator + pump, `PLANT_LEVELS[2]` + generator, `IMPROVE.automation` + 2 motor, chemworks + 4 pipes + pump, polymer + 4 pipes. `data/itemclass.ts` `classOf(k)` → raw / food / material / industrial / electrical / advanced / aerospace (`CLASS_NAME`; the tooltip's 'class' line; SUROWCE section 6).
- Farming document (0.142, the owner's "Agriculture, food and local economy"): `CROPS` gains 'cotton' (→ `cotton`, a market `Good` appended to `GOODS`, base 16; Textile Mill recipe 2 cotton → 1 cloth) and `also` (a second output: cows 1 `meat` a day, hens 0.5; `meat` = Crate of Meat, an off-market good, food value 1.5; the food shop's `meatC` dish ×8). `farmYield(seed, s, workers?, power)` adds `also`; in a settlement the steel ploughs' extra is × the farms' power (`farmPower`, now cached per hour like `sitePower`). `PROCESSING` (gain 0.35 on grain, potatoes, milk, meat while the 'foodworks' post is manned) in `foodMade`; `settleTarget` / `peopleFed` / `eatenShare` / `villageStats` take `power`. Settlement project 'foodworks' Food processing house (`PROJECTS`, after power and 2 farms, tutorial step after farm 3; `RESOURCE_PLOTS.foodworks` (−80, 140), 8 kW in `siteKw`, `JOBS.foodworks` 3, manned after power; drawn in world/settlement.ts: hall, smokehouse chimney, windmill tower with sails, silo, sacks; carrying workers). `syncFarmVillage` anchors farm goods that have none yet (only farm goods). Timed orders at the forge (ui/orders.ts): ordering takes the materials from the village stock and adds `char.forge` {w, v, i, n, done, told} (personal, not shared), `forgeHours` (1 h basic, 1 + the tech's tier), `FORGE.max` 3, 'In the forge' rows with Collect (`data-forge`, not a stock mutation), `updateForge` (main loop) tells you once when ready. `tools/materials.sim.ts` lists the processing house and the timed orders.
