- Village plan stages A–C (0.141, `PLAN_WIOSEK.md` from the owner's "Villages, population and progression" document; new-world settlements (`progressive`) only, established villages keep their rules): `gen/workforce.ts` (pure, imports only gen/settlement): `JOBS` (farm 4, power 2, quarry 3, lumber 4, mine 4, oil 2, refinery 3, works 3, station 1), `postsOf(s)` in priority order (farms `farm:i`, then the yards `power`/`quarry`/`lumber`/`mine`/`oil`/`refinery` once built, then `works:i` by `TownState.plants`, then `station:i`), `assign(s, workers)` → posts with `got` / `fill` (works and stations need their whole crew: fill 0 or 1), `used`, `free`, `jobs`; `fillOf` (1 for anything not a settlement post). Workers = `workersAt` (60% of `peopleAt`). Effects: `farmYield(seed, s, workers?)` × each farm's fill; `stockOf` (gen/hall.ts) passes the workers, the yards' yields × `yardFill` (instead of `staffing`), `refineStock` batches × the refinery's fill; `baseKw` × the power plant's fill, `balance` stations × fill and a works short of hands is not powered and draws nothing (`crew` / `crewFill` in gen/energy.ts); `shortHands(vid, kind, i)` (ui/villagestats.ts) says so in the works window and prompt. Food (gen/farms.ts): `FOOD` (`eat` 0.26 food crates a person a day beyond the `SETTLEMENT_START` 8 the wilds feed, `margin` 1.1, `short` 0.95, `value` grain / carrots / potatoes 1, eggs / milk 4/3; flax and wool none), `foodMade`, `foodNeed`, `peopleFed`, `settleTarget` (the highest count ≤ the homes that its own workers' farms feed with the margin to spare, stepped down from `housingCapacity`; `farmTarget` returns it for settlements, so `syncFarmVillage` (now for every loaded settlement, farms or not) retargets the population curve to it), `eatenShare` (the people eat their share of the food grown at `peopleAt(now)`; `stockOf` keeps only the rest). `gen/villagestats.ts` `villageStats(seed, home, s, now, stored)` → the six numbers (people, assigned, free, housing, food {made, need, fed, ratio, state short/tight/secure, days in store}, development + `DEV_NAMES`) and `limit` (homes / food / hands); `ui/villagestats.ts` `statsOf`, `statRows`, `postName`, `statusHTML` (the elder's option 'status' "How is the village doing?", allowed for settlement elders from the start) and the terminal's VILLAGE rows for settlements. Yard workers (world/settlement.ts `yardCrew`, `crews`): a figure per post at each built yard (`KIT`: quarry / mine pick, the others hammer), as many shown as `assign` gives it (rechecked every 2 s), striking at their spot. MP: nothing new is saved or relayed; every client works the posts, food and target out of the shared `towns` state the same way; `retarget` writes `TownState.people` on every client in the village as before (same values, last write wins). Tests: `tests/workforce.test.ts`.
- Crews, ladders and gangplanks (0.140, `PROTOCOL` 9): several players on one boat. `world/boats.ts` keeps a pose per `Live` (`x`, `z`, `yaw`); one game drives each boat (`drives`: the player at the helm / oars (seat 0), else not if a `fresh` (`FRESH` 3 s) message names another helm or came from a lower id, else the lowest net id within `NEAR` 400 m; alone always you): it runs `step` (writes the pose and the saved `b`) and `tell`s the room (`relay` {t:'boat', id, x, z, yaw, sp, turn, th, sh, su, fuel, helm, seat2, ro}, real time: every 100 ms while busy, else 1 s); the others `follow` (ease to the heard pose extrapolated by speed and turn rate) and never write `b`. Seats: 0 = helm / oars, 1 = the rowboat's aft oars (`BoatSpec.seat2`, `rowers` 2) or a passenger seat; `board` gives seat 1 when someone else holds the helm (a ship's wheel is refused then: 'Someone else has the wheel'); seat 1 sends {t:'row', id, fwd, turn} every 100 ms, the driver adds it to its own pull with `oarPower(a, b)` (gen/boats.ts: both pulling the same way → up to `ROW_PAIR` 1.5× the speed, against each other they cancel); a seat-1 player whose helm goes quiet is promoted to seat 0. The rowboat has a second pair of oars (`oarPair`). Deck walkers on someone else's ship are carried by `carry` from the followed pose; peers aboard are drawn by `PeerState.boat` [id, u, v, h, seat] (`myBoatSpot` / `boatPoint`, ship-local u along, v across) in `world/peers.ts`. Ladders: rope ladders on both sides of a ship at `LADDER_U` 2 m (`atLadder`: in the water or beside the hull there; E climbs onto the deck; `nearBoat` offers a ship only at its ladder or its wheel). Gangplanks (`Plank`, `planks`, `PLANK` reach 3.2 m, rise 1.6 m, speed 1.6 m/s): while a ship lies still (< 0.4 m/s) each side's plank slides out of the gangway gap to the nearest dock deck, bridge deck or dry ground within reach (rechecked every 0.4 s), tilted to it; walkable (`shipDeckAt` includes them); drawn in when she moves. `launchSpot` also tries a ship alongside the outer end of the dock's head (her gangway by it, the hull out in deeper water) before mooring her beyond the head; the dock's lamp and crate stand at the head's inner end (`LAMP_BACK` 3.9, `CRATE.s` −3.9 in `world/piers.ts`) so a gangway is clear. `relay` types 'boat' and 'row' added to server `RELAY`; `onRelay` keeps a list of listeners. `liveBoats()` (tests).
- Ships and the small boat (0.139, the owner's design: a small boat rowed, refitted with a sail or a motor; a large ship that only sails or steams and whose deck you walk while it goes; different holds; built at a seaside dock): piers are called docks in the game (Dock Kit, DOCK sign; code keeps `pier`). `gen/boats.ts` `BoatKind` row (Rowboat, hold 10) / sail (Sailing Skiff) / motor (Motor Skiff) — `BoatSpec.from` 'row': refits, hold 14, `refitsOf`, `Boat.refit` (`BoatBuild` handed over at the dock: `ui/pier.ts` `refits` for rowboats within 40 m of the head, `data-pier` rnew / rgive; done → `b.k` changes and `syncBoats` redraws) — and the ships ship (Sailing Ship, 16 m, hold 48, `sails`, no oars) / steamer (Motor Ship, 15 m, hold 40, `motor` tank 200); `NEW_BOATS` (built new on the slip), `isShip` (`BoatSpec.deck` 1.25 m), `halfBeam`, `hullPoints`, `launchSpot` (alongside the head or the dock, ships 0.25 m off so you step across, or beyond the head; null if too shallow: the finished hull waits on the slip). `world/boats.ts`: per-boat `Ctl` (speed, throttle, sheet, sailUp) so an unmanned ship sails on as left; `step` runs every live boat each frame; `shipModel` (bulwarks with a gangway gap at |u| < `LAYOUT.gap`, planked deck, deckhouse / wheelhouse, wheel, hatch, two gaff-rigged masts, bowsprit and jib / engine casing, funnel, mast lamp, propeller) and `LAYOUT` (deck furniture `solids`, `wheel`, `stand`); `shipFloor` (G.floor) and `shipHit` (G.obstacle: bulwarks, stem and stern, furniture) for deck walkers; `shipUnder` + `carry` move whoever stands on a deck with the ship (position, height and yaw); `nearBoat` / `boatPrompt` / `board`: climb aboard from beside the hull, take the wheel by `stand`; `stepOff` at a ship's wheel leaves it (the ship holds course); F opens the hold on deck; `boatCamera` 22 m behind a ship. Older notes: boats (the owner's plan: built at the players' piers; they go on the seas, rivers and lakes; stage 1 the rowboat, 2 a sailboat with the weather's wind, 3 a motor boat): `gen/boats.ts` (pure): `BOATS` (`BoatKind` 'row': Rowboat, len 4.2, beam 1.5, draft 0.35, speed 3.6 m/s, back 0.5, turn 0.9 rad/s, hold 10, needs 10 logs + 14 nails + 4 rope, xp 120), `KEEL` 0.15, `floats(k, depth)`; `Boat {id 'boat:<pid>:<time>:<n>', k, x, z, yaw, t}` saved in `char.boats` (forward = (sin yaw, cos yaw); the hold is `char.containers[id]`); `BoatBuild {k, given}` on a pier (`Pier.boat`), `boatRows`, `handOverBoat`. `ui/pier.ts` `slip(p)` on a finished pier: start (`data-pier="bnew"`, `data-bk`), hand over (`bgive`), launched beside the head (gen v = headW/2 + beam/2 + 0.7 at the head's middle, pointing out to sea); `MAX_BOATS` 3. `world/boats.ts`: `rowboat()` (PropBatch hull from stations: gunwale, chine and keel lines, strake, ribs, floorboards just above the water line so no water shows inside, transom, three thwarts; two oar groups pivoting at the oarlocks), `syncBoats` (twice a second, within 400 m, on the player's copy via `nearX`), `updateBoats(dt)` (main loop; returns true while aboard so `updatePlayer` is skipped): W/S/A/D (and the touch stick) row and turn, stamina `STAMINA.swim` × 0.7 by effort (out of breath × 0.35), `G.activity` = swim, the river's `flow` added, `clearAt` (bow, stern, sides over water ≥ draft + keel and no `pierDeck`) or aground (toast), the oars' stroke animation, a bob; the player sits on the thwart (`G.pos` from the boat, eye level + 1.0; `G.yaw` turns with the boat), saved every 8 s. `boatCamera` (seat or 7 m behind, V `toggleBoatView`), `boatHint` in the prompt, `nearBoat` / `board` (E; `G.yaw = yaw + π` looks forward) / `stepOff` (E aboard: a pier or bridge deck, else dry ground or < 0.35 m of water at the sides / bow / stern; none → a second E within 3 s drops you over the side), `openBoatHold` (F, beside or aboard; the transfer window), `boatHit` in `G.obstacle` (hulls you are not in), `clearBoats` in `clearLevel` (steps you off quietly). The armed rule, `attack` and `updateGun` are off aboard. Maps: `drawArea` draws your boats as small hulls. Horizon fixes for low eyes over the sea: `shapeRing` skips ring segments whose both ends are sunk and puts the base at `BASE_SEA` 0.5 when the camera is over the sea (`shapeHorizon` reshapes when that changes); `world/farpeaks.ts` rests the silhouettes at −1.2 (not −30) on the water and rebuilds when wet changes. Sailboat and motor boat (stages 2 and 3): `BOATS.sail` (Sailboat: len 6.6, beam 2.3, draft 0.6, speed 7, turn 0.55, hold 24, `seat` −2.3 at the tiller, `tall` (its mast: `clearAt` refuses bridge decks; the aground toast says so via `bridgeAhead`), `row` 1.6 with the spare oars; 24 logs, 30 nails, 14 rope, 6 hides, 300 xp) and `BOATS.motor` (Motor Boat: len 5.6, speed 9, back 0.35, turn 0.8, hold 18, `motor {tank 40, burn 0.04 L/s at full throttle, can 20}`; 14 logs, 18 nails, 4 rope, 10 scrap, 2 engine parts, 260 xp; launched with `fuel` 10). `BoatSpec.seat` / `eye` place the player. Sailing (pure, `gen/boats.ts`): `windSpeed(w)` 2 + 10·w m/s from `weatherAt(...).wind`, `windAngle(yaw, windDir)` (0 running .. π head to wind; `windDir` = where the wind blows to, (cos, sin)), `polar` (0.5 + 0.5·sin θ up to `CLOSE` 0.75π, falling to 0 at `NO_GO` 0.83π), `bestSheet` (1 running .. 0.05 close-hauled), `trim` (1 − 1.8·|sheet − best|), `sailSpeed` = min(speed, wind·0.75·polar·trim), `pointOfSail` words. `world/boats.ts`: one `model(k)` over a shared `hull(pb, k)` (decked kinds get `foredeck`): the rowboat's oars, the sailboat's mast, stays, shrouds, rudder and tiller and a `boom` group (boom + triangular sail) that swings to leeward by the sheet (`lee` from cos(windDir + yaw)) and folds down (scale y 0.2) with the sail lowered, the motor boat's console, windscreen, gold fuel gauge and a `prop` group (the outboard) turning with the helm. `updateBoats` by kind: motor (throttle eases to W/S, `fuel` burns by |throttle|, empty → no thrust and a toast; steering 0.35 at rest); sail with `sailUp` (W/S move `sheet`, target `sailSpeed`, rudder 0.25 at rest); else oars (the rowboat, or the sailboat with the sail down: `row` speed, stamina). The wind is sampled once a second. `boatHint` per kind (speed, fuel, the wind's point of sail and trim advice). Keys: Space `toggleSail` (aboard a sailboat), R `refuel` (aboard or beside a motor boat: a Fuel Canister from the hold first, then the backpack, +20 L; otherwise R reloads as before). `SUROWCE.md` (section 3.11) lists bridges, piers and boats. Tests: `tests/boats.test.ts` (building, floating, the polar and the trim).
- Mountainous islands (0.206, the owner's rule: the small ocean islands are mountainous; `PROTOCOL` unchanged): `ISLE.top` [38, 95] × (1.1 − 0.3 × the size's share of the range: the smaller the higher), `peakOff` 0.22, `rough` 0.55, `grain` 110 m, `beach` 0.1, `shelf` [20, 42] m; `isleRelief(world, s, dx, dz, d)` in `gen/seas.ts` (the dry part of `isleHeight`): a peak `peakOff` × r off the middle towards `ph` with concave flanks (`q^1.6`), ridged noise (1 − |2n − 1| of a 3-octave `fbm` in island-local coordinates, so both copies of the planet agree), a beach rising over `beach` of r, a level shelf round the middle (the fog site's ground, `toxic.ts` takes `naturalHeight` there). Islands keep their places (`isleOf` rolls unchanged). Tests: the islands test in `tests/toxic.test.ts` (a high middle, a peak near `top`, steep flanks). MP: from the seed.
- Islands and toxic fog (the owner's plan: local toxic fog on some sea islands and on land far from Gridholm, entered with a gas mask; an NBC suit maybe later): islands (`gen/seas.ts`): `ISLE` grid of `cell` 1600 m cells round the planet (`isleOf(world, i, j)`, cached; `chance` 0.35 where `seaMask` ≥ 0.6 at the middle and > 0.35 at 2.5 r in 3 directions), `Isle {i, j, x, z, r 60–220, top 3–11 m, ph}`, `islesNear`, `isleEdge` (lobed), `isleHeight` (a dome dry to r, sloping under the sea to `foot` 1.7 r); `naturalHeight` takes the max with it where the mask > 0.3 (~150 islands a world). Fog zones (`gen/toxic.ts`, pure, from the natural land and the sea mask only, so trees can ask): `regionFog(world, rx, rz)` (cached per canonical region, id 'fog:<rx>:<rz>'): an island whose middle is in the region (`FOG.isleFrom` 6 km out, `isleChance` 0.3, r = island r × 1.25 + 30) or a land zone (`landFrom` 15 km, chance 1.2 → 2.5% per region by 30 km, r 90–170, level ground at the site, not in or near the sea, mountains > 0.3, rivers, camps, roads within r + 20, villages within r + 700; ruins and wrecks may lie in the fog but not within 52 m of the site); ~45 of each a world. `FogZone {id, kind 'isle'|'land', x, z, r, y, yaw, site 'depot'|'lab'|'probe', name}`, `fogsNear`, `fogDensity` (1 in the core `FOG.core` 0.45 r, easing to 0 at a lobed edge), `fogAt`, `inFogSite` (`FOG.site` 22 m bare: `chunkTrees`, `chunkRocks`, `chunkPlants` skip it). `MASK` (filter 300 s at density 1, dmg 9 HP/s, `faint` 0.08), `filterWear`, `fogHarm`, `lockerLoot(world, f, i)` (hashed: power cores, circuits, scrap, maybe a relic, engine parts, filters, medkits, glass / steel by site). `FOG_SITE` names and locker counts. `world/toxic.ts`: `updateToxic` (main loop; zones within 650 m drawn: the site by kind (`drawDepot` bunker, sheds, broken wall; `drawLab` block, three tanks one split; `drawProbe` crater, tilted body, torn solar wing, split spheres; leaking drums; steel lockers with a lime hazard plate, `LOCKERS` per kind) and fog banks (lime sprites, `fog: false`, drifting); found zones saved to `char.fogs` [x, z, r, name] within r + 60; the density `here` eased into `seen`); breathing: with the Gas Mask (`gasmask`, wear slot 'face', new in `WEAR_SLOTS`) and `char.filter` > 0 the filter wears by `filterWear`, spares in the backpack are fitted (`fitFilter`: 'first', 'spent', or '' when used from the backpack via `useItem`); otherwise `fogHarm` HP/s. `tintToxic` after `updateSky` (fog colour, background, fog near/far pulled in, `tintSky` in `world/sky.ts` for the dome, stars, sun and moon); `toxicHere` hides the horizon rings and far peaks in thick fog (main.ts); `toxicName` for `placeName`; `toxicHit` in `G.obstacle`; `nearFogLocker` / `fogLocker` (E in `world/interact.ts`: the transfer window on `char.containers['fog:<rx>:<rz>:<i>']`, rolled once); `dropToxic` in `closeWorld`. `ui/toxic.ts` `showToxic`: the `#toxbar` line (density, filter bar, spares, or NO MASK) and the `#toxhaze` vignette. Items `gasmask`, `filter` (cons); technology 'filters' (Filter Masks, tier 2, last in `TECHS` so the others keep their sites); `ORDERS`: gas mask (2 hide, membrane, 2 scrap), 2 filters (2 logs, scrap). Maps: dashed lime rings for `char.fogs`. Console `fogs [km]`. Tests: `tests/toxic.test.ts`. Later: the NBC suit, fog in dungeons, creatures of the fog.
- Dead cities (0.105, `gen/cities.ts`, pure): `CITY` (`n` 10, radius 520–820 m, the first two 3.8–8 km from Gridholm (`near`), the rest 7–26 km (`band`), `apart` 4.5 km between edges, `danger` +2 faded over `fade` 250 m, `floor` 3.4 m). `citySites(world)` (cached; hashed tries per city, `misfit`: sea, mountain mask, the ice, > 20 m of relief by `naturalHeight`; names from `ruinName` + Metropolis / City / Sprawl / Megacity; `pattern` by hash: 'grid' | 'warped' | 'radial' | 'diagonal'), `cityAt` / `inCity(world, x, z, m)`, `cityDanger` (added in `dangerAt`), `cityToWorld` / `worldToCity` (the city's frame (u, v) turned by `yaw`, like three.js rotation.y). `cityLayout(world, c)` (cached): `streetsOf` per pattern (grid: block sizes, avenues every 3rd / 4th line, 7% of pieces missing; warped: jittered nodes; radial: chord rings round a plaza and spokes, more spokes further out; diagonal: the grid plus two 20 m boulevards), `clipCut` to the disc in ≤ 48 m pieces; buildings (`Bld` {x, z, w, d, a, f floors, st 0 broken crown / 1 gutted / 2 fallen, s seed}) on lots along both sides of every street (`segNearRect`, `rectsOverlap` with a 2 m gap, a spatial index of `CELL` 32 m: `bIdx`, `sIdx`, `cIdx`), then infill; floors by distance from a hashed `core` (`maxF` 18–45, `spread`, landmark towers), low at the edge; `cars` in the streets. `bldsNear`, `carsNear`, `inBld`, `bldTop` (a heap ≤ 4.5 m), `corners`, `segDist`. Everything else keeps off: villages (`candidate`, `CITY.village` 450 m), ruins / camps / wrecks (`CITY.place`), lakes, wells, trees, rocks, plants, abandoned vehicles, land fog, installations ('city'); rivers (`drainage` closes the city + 150 m) and roads (`findWay`: closed within 40 m) go round. `world/cities.ts`: `updateCities` (main loop) keeps the cities within `CITY_IN` 360 m of their edge live (a root group at the city's centre turned by yaw, dropped past 480 or across the seam), tiles of 96 m built within 250 m (nearest first, ~6 ms a frame) and dropped past 330; `drawBld` (faces in bays ~3.3 m with dim floor and bay lines, `ch` corner heights, a ragged crown; a whole roof with a stair head; gutted: walls to 35–65% then a frame of columns and part slabs with hanging beams; fallen: wall stubs, a low heap of rocks and tilted slabs; rubble at the feet), `drawStreet` (kerbs, sidewalk edges, centre dashes or a double line on avenues, crossings, lamp posts, some bent or down), `drawCar`; `cityHit` (buildings below their top, cars; in `G.obstacle`, the vehicles' `blocked`, the foes' `forbidden`), `cityRay` (slab test per building in `G.rayBlock`), `unstick` (an arrival inside a building steps out), `cityName` ('Ruins of <name>' in `placeName`), `dropCities` in `closeWorld`. Maps: the chunk tiles colour buildings and streets (`cityPx` in `tile`), a dashed outline and the name once a part has been explored or you are near (`drawArea`), the dev map shows every city. Console `cities`. Garrisons (0.110): `cityGarrisons(world, c)` in `gen/cities.ts` (pure, cached) = a hashed share of the street pieces (`GARRISON.edge` 30% → `core` 70% towards the core) each with a `Garrison {k, u, v, kind 'machines' | 'nest' | 'gang', size 1–3}` (`mix` 55 / 25 / 20; +1 size near the core); `world/citygarrisons.ts` `updateGarrisons` (main loop, outdoors, twice a second; not while flying): the nearest garrisons within `GARRISON_RT.wake` 105 m wake (at most `awake` 9 at once, outside `mayspawn`'s pacing) — machines `spawnRobotSquad` (`size` × `pickGroup` in `world/robots.ts`), nests `spawnNest` (4 + 3 × size gnawers, `world/creatures.ts`), gangs `spawnBandit` (2 + 2 × size, a leader, bruisers, gunners) at the danger there (≥ 2); past `sleep` 122 m they are removed again; all dead while you are within `wake` → beaten (`beaten`, quiet for `respawn` 45 game minutes; runtime only, cleared for another world). ~50 foes round you in a city's core. Console `cities`. Tests: `tests/cities.test.ts`.
- Megaliths and colossal heads (`gen/megaliths.ts`, `world/megaliths.ts`): `MEGALITH_DESIGNS` = 12 stone sanctuaries then 10 head sites (form 'heads', `layout` valley / council / row / sunken / twin / leaning / assembly / outward / patriarch / vigil; appended so the first 12 keep their sites; `MEGALITH_COUNT` 22; `worldMegaliths` places them in order, each clear of the earlier ones). `headPlan` (own rng `hash(seed, 0x4ead)`) → `megalithHeads(m)` (scaled `MegalithHead {x, z, y (sunk below 0), h, yaw (face along (sin, cos)), lean}` and `MegalithRock`s round them, plus up to 10 great boulders; the valley keeps its middle path clear); `megalithStones` gives each head (`HEAD_FOOT` w .5 h, d .4 h) and boulder an invisible collision volume (`MegalithStone.kind`). `drawHead` lofts `HEAD_RINGS` ([t, half-width, front, back] from shoulders to crown, a hashed `wob` per corner) into triangulated facets (`FACE` green lines), a nose wedge, lips, ears and `GROOVE` visor plates lit with rows of `EYES` dashes (`frontAt` puts them on the face); boulders via `PropBatch.rock`.
- Mountains (`gen/mountains.ts`, pure): `mountainMask(world, x, z)` 0..1 (low-frequency fbm at ~1 km scale, ~24% of the land, none within `MOUNTAIN_CLEAR` 1.4 km of Gridholm), `mountainLift` (ridged noise + peaks, up to ~170 m) added in `Terrain.base` before the polar blend. Villages (`villageOfCell`), ruins, camps, wrecks, lakes, wells and roads (closed above mask 0.3, costly below) keep off them via `onMountain`. `Terrain.forest` thins above ~50 m. Chunks reaching above `SNOW_LINE` (115 m) draw pale lines; the maps shade high ground paler. `world/farpeaks.ts`: a silhouette ring (radius 145, fog-free, travels with the camera) of the real mountains out to 3.2 km, rebuilt after moving 120 m.
- Trails (`gen/trails.ts`, pure): `regionSummit` (the region's highest point after a hill-climb, ≥ 70 m, kept by the region it stands in; `Mount <name>`), `regionTrails` (only peaks with no higher summit within 450 m): from `trailhead` (nearest foot: mask < 0.06, h < 36, no lake) a greedy climber (8 m steps, aims for the top at ~0.7 × `TRAIL.grade`, turn penalty, legs ≥ `LEG_GAP` 14 m apart) up to the summit; the height profile (`Road.h`) is the smoothed ground clamped to `TRAIL.grade` 0.36 both ways; trails needing a cut/fill > 9 m are dropped. A trail is a `Road` (`gate: 'trail'`, `h`, `name`, `half` 2.2 so the 2 m lattice sees the bench), gathered by `featuresIn` from regions within 1.5 km, so trees, rocks, plants and claims avoid it; `Terrain.exact` applies the nearest trail's `trailHeight` with `TRAIL_BLEND` 9 m. Drawn as dashed edges in `buildChunk`; `world/trails.ts` markers (signpost, cairns every 7 points, summit cairn + pennant). HUD place names at the trailhead and summit; maps show them (dashed on the area map).
- Caves (`gen/caves.ts`, pure): `rawCaves` (≤ 1 mouth per mountain region, ~45%: a flank spot with a gentle approach and a steep face behind) and `regionCaves` (linked: two mouths that are each other's nearest within `LINK_DIST` 650 m join one system with `LINK_CHANCE`; `Cave.sys` / `name` are the system's, `mouth` 0/1, `other`). `world/caves.ts` draws the open mouth (arch, dark tunnel, boulders; `caveHit` in `treeHit`); E at it (`atMouth`) → `enterCave` in `world/level.ts`.
- Cave interiors: `gen/cavegen.ts` `generateCave(seed, 1|2)` (pure, seed `hash(world, sys, 0xca11)`): chambers with lobed outlines, a spanning tree + loops + dead-end pockets, winding tunnels (midpoint displacement, varying radius); per-metre `floor` / `ceil` fields (flat middle, floor curling up and vault coming down at the sides; heights blended softly between elements so there are no steps), exits on the west (and east) edge, chests in pockets, crystals, stalagmites/stalactites. `world/cavelevel.ts` `buildCave`: `c.dungeon.cave {name, mouths, from}` (loc stays 'dungeon', `ruinId` = system id, so chest keys work), floor + vault nets over a double-sided fill, `G.ground` = the floor field, a coarse `VoxelGrid` of the rock as `G.space` (walls, vault, shots, minimap via `setMiniOpen`), `G.obstacle` = low headroom + stalagmites, drones on `W.spawnCells`; E at an exit (`caveExitNear`) → `exitCave(i)` in front of mouth i (the far side for a linked system). Test: `tests/cavegen.test.ts` walks every chamber and both exits.
- Terrain LOD: chunks more than 2 chunks away draw grid lines every 4 m instead of 2 m. Distant mountains (`world/sky.ts`) are a fog-free ring that follows the camera.
- Structures are `VoxelGrid.surface(...)` grids: their footprint replaces the terrain (terrain mesh has a hole there, collision uses voxels only).
- Dungeon seeds: `hash(world, ruinId, depth, gx, gz)`; save keys `ruinId:depth:gx:gz`. Save format v3 (`gridWorld.character.v3`; older `gridArena.*` keys are still read), migrations in `save.ts`.
- Vehicles (`data/vehicles.ts` specs incl. price and `enclosed`, `world/vehicles.ts` models/driving): RTV-1 Scout and HTV-6 Mastodon. Bought from Kuba, the dealer at the yard outside Gridholm's north gate (`YARD` in `gen/vehicles.ts`, first free bay), or found abandoned in the wilds (`regionVehicle`, deterministic per region, id `found:rx:rz`; claimed on first use, trunk loot rolled then). Only closed cabs (`enclosed`) shield the driver from drone contact damage (`foeRules.shielded`). Vehicles stay out of the villages; other places block them only where they really stand (`structBlocks` in `world/overworld.ts`, the vehicles' `blocked` hook: a loaded place's voxels at body height, a hole in its floor such as a stairwell, a temple's `blocks`; a place not loaded yet blocks its whole square). They are saved in `char.vehicles` (0.248: the world's, shared).
- Vehicle parts (`VehicleParts` in `data/vehicles.ts`, saved per vehicle): wheel conditions (-1 missing, 0 wrecked), engine, roof cannon. Missing/wrecked wheels or a dead engine stop the vehicle; wear lowers speed. Found vehicles come damaged (rolled from the region seed, visible before claiming). The service window (`ui/service.ts`, E at the vehicle's front) fits Light/Heavy Wheels, Engine Parts and the Vehicle Cannon. Kuba sells parts (`PART_PRICE`) and buys them back at `PART_BUYBACK` (20%); vehicles sell back for half price scaled by condition (`resaleValue`), trunk must be empty.
- People in vehicles: `SEATS[model]` in `data/vehicles.ts` (3 each: driver 0, passenger, gunner standing at the roof cannon (`gun`); `y` = hip, or feet for the gunner). `Vehicle.riders` (`Rider {who, fig}`), `seatRider` / `unseat` / `freeSeat` / `aboard` / `seatPoint` in `world/vehicles.ts` draw a figure per occupant (sitting with legs forward, the driver's hands on the steering wheel, the gunner turning with the turret in `pose`); getting in seats you ('you', hidden in cockpit view), the HUD shows `n/3 aboard`. Raiders seat a driver and gunner (+ a passenger in a Mastodon), caravans draw a crew (`convoyModel(..., crew)`). Hits: `HULL_BOXES[model]` (the solid parts: the Mastodon cab is built round open windows and a roof hatch) + wheels vs `riderBoxes`; `rayVehicle(v, o, d, max)` returns the nearer of the body (seat -1) or an occupant. `shoot()` / `fireCannon` use `rayRaider` → `hurtCrew` (raider `crewHp` per seat: a dead gunner silences the cannon, a dead driver makes the crew bail out (`bailOut`) and leaves the vehicle whole and claimable, `abandonRaider`) or `hurtRaider` (the vehicle). Bandit bolts aim at `seatPoint(driving.v, 0)` and `updateBolts` tests `rayVehicle` per step: seat 0 hurts you, the body damages the vehicle.
- Hull and fuel: `VehicleParts.hull` (hull points, max = spec `hull`) and `fuel` (litres, max = spec `tank`). Hull damage goes through `damageVehicle` (bandit/raider bolts, rams, crashes, and contact damage a closed cab absorbs via `foeRules.shieldHit`); at 0 the vehicle is wrecked (immobile, smoulders, driver thrown out) until Hull Plating is fitted in the service window. Fuel is tracked and shown but not burnt yet (`FUEL_BURN = 0` in `data/vehicles.ts`). Old saves get a full hull and tank (`upgradeParts`).
- Hero name: `char.name` (default '', set in the main menu's `#heroName` field, `cleanName` in `ui/menu.ts`; Play refuses without one; New character clears it). Dialogue passes texts through `here()` in `ui/dialog.ts`, which swaps `{name}` for it (and 'Gridholm' for the town): use `{name}` in `NPC_INFO` hellos and `VILLAGER_LINES`.
- Game clock (`core/time.ts`, pure): `char.time` in game minutes, 1 real second = 1 game minute (a day = 24 real minutes), runs whenever the game is not paused. Day/night in `world/sky.ts` (`updateSky`): sun and moon on opposite sides of one orbit, stars fade by day; a sky dome (`dome`, a ShaderMaterial drawn at the far plane, no depth: zenith → horizon gradient plus a glow round the sun) goes black at night → a bright hazy green by day (`DAY_Z` / `DAY_H`) → amber at dawn and dusk (`DUSK_*`, by `twilight`); the fog and `scene.background` take the horizon colour and lie further off by day; the sun's disc brightens, the moon's globe takes the zenith colour. Underground stays black. `daylight(t, tilt)` drives solar output too (gen/energy.ts). The notice board posts every 6 game hours (`boardPeriod`, `char.board.stamp`): the two oldest untaken offers per posting are replaced.
- Weather (`gen/weather.ts`, pure): `weatherAt(world, x, z, t)` → `{kind, cloud, rain, fog, storm, wind, windDir}`; every `WEATHER.cell` 3 km cell rolls a kind per `WEATHER.slot` 4 h (`KINDS` weights: clear 40, overcast 20, rain 18, fog 12 (×2.2 in the early morning), storm 10), numbers blended over the 4 nearest cell centres and faded from the last slot over `WEATHER.fade` 45 min; no thunder over the ice. Looks only for now; `weatherAt` is the hook for later effects (lightning strikes, storms tearing roofs, rain on roads, clouds on solar output). `world/weather.ts` (runtime, surface only): `seen` eases towards the forecast; rain streaks in a box round the camera (`N` 1600, slanted by the wind), faceted cloud banks drifting at 60–80 m (veiled by fog), forked lightning bolts 60–170 m off in a storm (`strike`, a flash that lights the sky, a tremor of the camera; no sound yet); `forceWeather` (console `weather clear|overcast|rain|fog|storm|auto`). `world/sky.ts` reads `seen`: clouds grey the dome and hide the stars, sun and moon; fog pales the horizon and pulls the fog in; the flash brightens it. Fog hides the horizon rings and far peaks (main.ts). The HUD clock shows the weather ("· Rain").
- Creatures (`data/creatures.ts`, `world/creatures.ts`), open world only, unsaved like drones: Ravager packs (flank, dash, bite, retreat; plains), Bramble (territorial ~11 m, head-down charge with knockback, 60% less damage from the front; valleys h < 9), Leechwing (circles ruins/high ground, stalks, dives; flies over terrain), Gnawer (rat-like, long incisors, spined lashing tail; nests of 3–8 turn up anywhere, ~30% of spawns: scurry and sniff, the whole nest swarms in zig-zagging, bites and skitters off, the last one or two flee). Models are PropBatch bodies with origin at the body centre so weapons (`t.g.position`, `t.r`) and EMP (`t.p`) work unchanged; `damageFoe` routes them to `hurtCreature`; at 0 hp `fallCreature` takes them out of `W.creatures` and `updateDead` animates the body (runners slide and roll onto a side kicking, a Bramble folds its legs, a Gnawer flips over, a Leechwing falls tumbling and lands with its wings spread; colour drains, `LIE_C` s, sinks, `removeCreature`). Skitterwing (PF-06, `skitter`: flocks of 3–5, now and then (12% of spawn attempts) at danger 0.6–2.2; flutter 5 m up round `home`, on sight within 28 m the flock follows you and one or two at a time `dive`, peck, `climb`; 1 hp) and the sea (`SWIMMERS`, spawned where the spawn point is 'sea' ≥ 2.5 m deep via `SpawnEnv.water` = `Terrain.water`, `spawnSea`): Sea Lurker (PF-07, `lurker`, from danger 1: roams with the dorsal fin out (`LURK_DEPTH`), `swim` keeps to sea ≥ 1.6 m and turns along the shallows; a player in the sea (`playerInSea`: below the level + 0.3, not aboard) within 45 m is circled (`stalk`), lunged at (`dash`) and bitten; boats are only circled) and Silverfin (PF-08, `silverfin`, shoals of 5–9 following the first, flee within 12 m, leap now and then; harmless, `CREATURE_COST` 0, not counted by `fighting`). `FLYERS` / `SWIMMERS` in `data/creatures.ts`; dead swimmers roll belly up at the surface then sink, their drops float (`dropPickup` at level + 0.47); item `fishR` Raw Fish (250 kcal). Console: `spawn ravager|bramble|leechwing|gnawer|skitter|lurker|silverfin` (the sea kinds in the nearest deep sea within 80 m).
- Danger and the threat budget: `gen/danger.ts` (pure) `dangerAt(world, x, z, nearRuin)` grows in rings round Gridholm: `ringDanger` of the distance from Gridholm's walls (`DANGER.rings`: 0 to 60 m, 1 from 600 m to 1.6 km, 2 at 4 km, 3 at 8, 4 at 13, 5 at 19, 6 at 26, 7 at 34, 8 at 44 km), the distance warped by `wobble` (angular harmonics, ±16%) so the rings are blobs; every village is a refuge (0 within `DANGER.safe` 60 m of its walls, `villageFar`, the ring's danger returning over `DANGER.refuge` 500 m); +0.6 near ruins, capped at 8. Higher danger = more and bigger encounters and a larger share of heavy robots (`pickGroup` weights grow with lv past each kind's `min`); `overworld.danger()` wraps it and the HUD place name shows it ("Wilds · danger 3") only in god mode since 0.246. Every spawner (creatures, robots, bandit patrols, raiders, ambushes) asks `mayspawn(cost, lv, heat)` in `world/threat.ts`, which paces encounters: one at a time, then the next waits `gap(lv)` (~45-75 s at danger 0, ~20-30 s at 8; gunfire `heat` shortens it), nothing new while anything hostile is within `FIGHT_R` 45 m, and the threat within 160 m plus the newcomer must fit `budget(lv)` = 1 + 1.1·lv; nothing spawns for `GRACE` (20 s) after leaving a village, or while flying. A granted `mayspawn` starts the next gap (the caller spawns right away). Spawns come ahead or to a side of the player (60-95 m). Creature bites and robot blows are scaled by `FOE_HIT` 1.35. Gates: gnawers and brambles anywhere, ravagers from 1.2, leechwings 2.5, robots per `ROBOTS[k].min`, bandit patrols 2, ambushes 2.5, raiders 3; bandit camps stay ≥ 480 m from villages (`regionInfo`).
- Robots (`data/robots.ts`, `world/robots.ts`, the RD sheet; open world, unsaved; replaced the old field drones, which now only come from repair drones): RD-03 Scout Automaton (danger 1: fast blade packs, flank and slash), RD-01 Guardian Drone (1.8: spider legs, energy cannon, keeps 12–22 m, groups; more near ruins), RD-05 Repair Drone (3: hovers back, heals hurt robots with a beam, deploys up to 2 defence drones), RD-02 Sentinel (4.5: heavy biped, 3-bolt bursts and a crushing blow), RD-04 Artillery Walker (5: shells your position from 16–110 m, a red ring marks the blast; helpless up close), RD-06 Assault Construct (6: charges, hammer smash with knockback). Heavy ones take `armour` 0.55 of damage. Kills drop crystals and salvage (`loot`): Scrap Metal, Electronic Components (`circuit`), Power Core (`pcore`, rare); they count as 'drone' kills for bounties. Destroyed robots (`wreckRobot` → `updateScraps` in `world/robots.ts`, runtime only): shudder with sparks and flickering lights (`SHUDDER`), then give way (bipeds topple about their feet away from the hit, walkers' legs splay and the body drops, the repair drone falls out of the air), crash with a blast that tears an arm off heavy ones (`scene.attach`, thrown with gravity), smoke, go dark, lie `LIE_R` s, sink, `removeRobot`. Robots use `kind: 'robot'` in `foes()` / `damageFoe` → `hurtRobot`. Console: `spawn scout|guardian|repair|sentinel|artillery|assault`.
- Survival (`data/survival.ts` numbers, `world/survival.ts` runtime, HUD bars under HP): stamina (not saved; spent by sprinting, swimming, jumping and blade swings; regenerates after a short pause; at 0 you are exhausted until it is back to 30), calories and water. `char.kcal` is the body's energy store (max `KCAL.max` 3000, start 2400): it burns `KCAL.perDay` (2000) per game day at rest, times the activity (`G.activity`, set by the player's movement: walk 1.6, sprint/swim 4) and the carried weight (`BURN.perKg`), plus one-off costs (jump, blade swing, `burn()`). Below `KCAL.low` you are hungry (slower stamina regen), at 0 you starve (HP drain). Eating adds a food's `NOURISH[k].kcal` and puts its weight (`BULK`) into the stomach (`char.stomach`, `STOMACH.cap` 2 kg, digests 0.3 kg per game hour); `cantEat` refuses food when the stomach is full or the store is near max, so bulky low-calorie food (caps, pods) fills you before it feeds you. Water `char.water` 0..100 as before (drinks do not fill the stomach). Old saves: the 0..100 food bar becomes the same share of `KCAL.max`. The blade hits hard (`BLADE.dmg` 5 vs the Blaster's 1 per shot) but each swing costs stamina; exhausted, swings are slow (`tiredRate`, ignoring any speed bonus) and half as strong. Console: `eat`.
- Weight and bulk (`BULK` in `data/items.ts`: kg and litres per item; `PACK`): the backpack holds `PACK.vol` 40 L (a hard limit: `roomFor`, and the optional `cap` of `putItems` / `putSlot` / `moveStack` / `dropStack` in `inventory.ts`; every way into `char.inv` passes it, e.g. `addItem`, `packRoom`); weight is soft: above `PACK.comfy` (20 kg) you walk slower and sprint costs more, above `PACK.max` (35 kg) you are overloaded (slow, no sprint or jump). Equipped relics and fitted attachments weigh nothing. The backpack and container windows show `loadText()`, item details show kg, L and kcal.
- Dropped items lie on the ground as what they are (`world/pickmodels.ts` `lyingModel`: logs with cut ends and rings, planks, stones and ore lumps with veins, scrap (bent plate, cog, pipe), circuit boards, power cores, hides, fangs, incisors, plates, membranes, meat on the bone, caps, pods, crystals, bread, bowls, flasks, fire kits, tools, wire/rope coils, nails; trade goods as crates): `Pickup.rest`, no hover or spin, `restY` puts them on the terrain or the voxel floor. Other items keep the floating tokens (relics, keys, boxes).
- Food from the wilds: kills drop items besides crystals (`DROPS` in `data/creatures.ts`): only the big herbivore Bramble gives Raw Meat (+ Bramble Plate); Ravager (Hide, Fang), Leechwing (Membrane) and Gnawer (Incisor) give crafting materials (`type: 'mat'`, crafting later; Oskar buys them). Raw meat can be eaten (`RAW_SICK` chance to hurt) or roasted into Roasted Meat at any fire (E at a bandit camp's campfire or at a fire lit from a Fire Kit, `world/cooking.ts`, unsaved, `FIRE_LIFE`) or by Jan for `COOK_PRICE`. Jan and Oskar buy goods (`BUYS` in `data/npcs.ts`, the 'sell' option). Food pickups are lime (`FOOD_COLOR`), materials bone-white.
- Edible plants (RF-01 sheet): F-01 Nutrient Mushroom Cluster (shady forest) and F-02 Alien Fruit Pod Tree (forest edges, low ground) from `gen/flora.ts` (`chunkPlants`, at most one per chunk, cached; plants clear the trees around them via `plantsNear` in `gen/trees.ts`), F-03 Nutrient Crystals in dungeon room corners (`DungeonMap.crystals`, own RNG stream). `world/flora.ts` draws them (the picked part is a separate lime object) and handles E to pick; picked state is `char.harvest[key]` = game time, removed once regrown (`FORAGE` in `data/survival.ts`: caps 12 h, pods 24 h, crystals 48 h). Keys: `shroom|pod:<cx>:<cz>` (canonical chunk), `crys:<dungeonKey>:<i>`.
- Crafting (`data/crafting.ts` pure: `RECIPES`, `craft`, `GATHER`; `ui/craft.ts` window inside the dialogue panel): materials are Log, Stone, Scrap Metal (drones 35%, guardians 3, bandits 30% / leaders always, raider wreck trunks) plus the creature materials. Tools (`type: 'tool'`, carried in the backpack): Hatchet → hold E at a tree (`world/gather.ts`: `strike` starts the work, `updateGather` swings a blow every `GATHER.swing` 1.05 s while E is held, the tool drawn on the camera, weapons away via `working()` in the armed rule; 6 blows, big trees 14; blows kept while you stay; logs drop as pickups), Pickaxe → hold E at a rock with r ≥ `ROCK_MIN_R` (5 / 9 blows, stones). Ore veins: `Rock.ore` 'iron' | 'copper' (`oreOf` in `gen/trees.ts`, own hash stream per rock index; `ORE`: 5% of workable rocks in the lowlands, up to ~45% in the mountains, copper 35–50%), drawn as zigzag streaks and nuggets (`PropBatch.vein`, `ORE_COLOR` in `world/overworld.ts`), ×1.6 blows, drop Iron Ore / Copper Ore lumps (`ironO`, `copperO`) besides the stones, regrow in `GATHER.ore.regrow` 4 days (the harvest time is set ahead). Ore recipes: forge smelts iron ore + log → scrap, iron ore → wire, copper ore + scrap → electronic components; a bench packs 8 lumps + 2 planks into a market crate (`ore` / `copper` goods); Oskar buys the lumps. Crafting takes time (`Recipe.time`, `craftTime`, `CRAFT_TIME` bench 3 s / forge 6 s; planks 5 s): `ui/craft.ts` runs a job with a progress bar (Craft / ×5, Stop; materials used only when a batch completes, leaving or closing the window stops it). E ignores key repeat (`input.ts`). Felled trees / broken rocks are `char.harvest` keys `tree|rock:<cx>:<cz>:<i>` (index in `chunkTrees` / `chunkRocks`, canonical cx; `gatherKey` maps loaded objects to keys), a stump is drawn, the chunk is rebuilt (`rebuildChunkAt`), they regrow after 3 / 2 game days. Stations: every village blacksmith's workbench is a 'forge' (dialogue option 'craft', all recipes); a Workbench Kit sets up a 'bench' in the wilds (`world/benches.ts`, `char.benches`, max 3, E to use, can be packed up again) for the plain recipes (fire kit, tools, flask, medkit, workbench kit). Oskar sells Hatchet/Pickaxe and buys logs, stone, scrap.
- Noise (`world/noise.ts`): `makeNoise(at, radius)` for loud things (Blaster shot = gun stat `noise`, 55 m; Suppressor barrel attachment cuts it to ~12 m; vehicle cannon 95 m; EMP 35 m), `onNoise` listeners react: ground creatures (not Brambles) within the radius go to `investigate` the spot (and hunt you if they see you there), Leechwings hear from 1.8× as far, bandits (not ambushers) within 0.8× are alerted, drones within 0.5× give chase. Noise also builds `heat` in world/creatures.ts: more and faster spawns, placed around the noise and sent to investigate it; heat fades over ~25 s. Leechwing flight is steered (turn-rate-limited arcs, banking): soaring loops of 40–55 m radius at ~24 m, spotting prey up to 90 m (with line of sight), stalking in tightening, descending circles, diving, then a long climbing pull-out; with no player about they hunt Gnawers.
- Figures and arms (`world/rig.ts`): every human figure (`makeFigure(color, kit, shield, big)` in `world/npc.ts`) has two-segment arms solved by a two-bone IK each frame (`poseRig(rig, {swing, aim, strike, recoil})`) so the hands sit on the weapon; kits: 'none' (arms swing with the walk), 'rifle' (both hands, low ready → shouldered with `aim`, recoil kick; the boss's is longer with a scope), 'pistol' (one hand, raised to aim), 'sword' (guard, then a stroke by `strike` 0..1: wind-up over the shoulder, slash, recover; optional shield on the left arm), 'spear' (guards: upright, shield), 'drive' (raider crews: hands on the wheel / cannon). `muzzleLocal(rig)` gives the muzzle for bolts. Bandits pick a kit in `kitOf` (gunners rifle or pistol 35%, bruisers sword + shield 50%, the boss a scoped rifle); a bruiser's blow lands mid-slash (`Bandit.blow`), a shield halves damage from the front (`hurtBandit`). Death (`fallBandit(b, from)` in `world/bandits.ts`, also from caravan guns): out of `W.bandits` at once; a `Corpse` (runtime only) animates: weapon and shield `scene.attach`ed and thrown with gravity and spin (one bounce, then rest on `groundUnder`), knees buckle, the figure topples about its feet away from the blow (`lie` angle from the ground slope at the head), bounces, colour drains, lies `LIE` s, sinks over `SINK` s, then `removeBandit`.
- Bandits (`world/bandits.ts`, human enemies built from the NPC figure, amber; the Bandit Boss is red): Gunners keep 12–20 m, strafe and fire bursts of visible, dodgeable bolts; Bruisers rush with blades; each camp has one Boss. They need line of sight, alert their whole group, sometimes flee when hurt. Bolts that hit a closed-cab vehicle damage its engine/wheels instead of the driver. Camps are a POI type (`camp`, `gen/camps.ts`: crates/barricades as voxels for cover, A-frame tents, campfire, stash container `camp:<id>`), placed per region away from the village and from ruins; a cleared camp stays empty for 30 min (`char.camps`). Small patrols roam further out. Console: `spawn bandits`.
- Raiders and ambushes (`world/raiders.ts`, open world, danger ≥ 1 / 0.8): raider vehicles are AI-driven vehicles (`spawnAIVehicle`, `steerVehicle` with obstacle feelers; tinted amber, not claimable while `ai`) with a driver and a roof gunner; they chase from behind, circle and fire bursts, ram players on foot. Shot to pieces they become claimable wrecks (damaged parts, 50% cannon kept, some trunk loot) and the crew bails out as normal bandits. Ambushes spawn on roads ahead of the player: a roadblock of 3 barricades (`placeRoadblock`, `Barricade` with `BARRICADE_HP` 14; `ambushHit` obstacles for players and vehicles; `rayBarrier` / `hurtBarrier` in `shoot()` and `fireCannon()`, so shots wear them down; they stay after the bandits die and go when all are shot apart or you are 230 m away; `barriersNear`) and bandits lying low (`sight` 18) on both sides. Console: `spawn raider [mastodon]`, `ambush`.
- Quests (`gen/quests.ts` pure generator, `world/quests.ts` progress, `ui/board.ts` window): every village has a notice board on its plaza (`VillageMap.board`, `boardDeco`); each shows 4 notices. Gridholm's board is `char.board` (seq numbering and ids `q<n>` as ever), the others `char.boards[villageId]` (`boardOf`, made when first read; `refreshBoard` takes the old offers down on all of them and they refill when read). `generateQuest(t, n, taken, town, camps)` takes a `QuestTown` (`questTown(poi)`: position, `pay` = `boardPay(ringDanger(...))`, resident names from the loaded NPCs; `GRIDHOLM_TOWN` by default): other boards use their own RNG stream (`hash(world, id, n)`), ids `q<id>.<n>`, measure hunts/ruins/wrecks/camps from the village, drop the dealer's errand, and stamp `town`, `townName`, `giverName` on the quest. Claims only at the posting board (`claim(id, poi)`, `townOf`, `boardName`), errands only with that village's resident (`questOptions(role, town)` / `questTalk(id, town)`, `giverOf`); `questTarget` points back to the village once done. A camp is not posted twice on one board. Up to 3 accepted (`char.quests`). Kinds: bounty (kill N of a kind anywhere, incl. bandits), camp (clear a real bandit camp), hunt (a group + leader — Pack Alpha / Old Bull / Matriarch — at a real spot; `describeSpot` derives the wording and the creature from the terrain there), fetch (a resident errand: talk to the giver, then an item in a real ruin's dungeon — first guardian's room of depth 1 sector 0,0 — or by a real abandoned vehicle's parking spot). Board quests are claimed at the board, errands at the giver. Below the notices a Deliveries section (`deliveries()` in `ui/board.ts`): your contracts with Drop (`dropContract`) and `boardContracts(poi)` from `ui/contracts.ts` (the village's own offers plus the 4 nearest supply orders of villages within `CONTRACT.far`), taken with `acceptOffer` (the same path as the store's 'contracts' option; a haul still has to be loaded at its village). HUD tracker `#qtrack`, gold markers on the maps.
- Chest models (`world/chestmodel.ts`): `CHEST_KINDS` wood (the old treasure chest) / locker (ammunition footlocker) / canister (sealed tech canister) / vault (armoured strongbox with a keypad), each `base` + `lid` in the shared frame (body within x ±.45, z ±.3, rim at y .5; lid relative to the rear hinge y .5, z −.3); `chestKind(...seed)` picks by hash (wood 1 in 14); `makeChest` in `world/loot.ts` uses it, `drawClosedChest(pb, x, y, z, kind)` (camp stash 'locker', the home chest 'wood').
- Containers: chests roll their contents once on first open and keep what the player leaves or puts in (`char.containers`, key `chest:<dungeonKey>:<index>`). The backpack window (`ui/backpack.ts`, `#pack .packrow`) has a side panel (`#packSide`, `PackSide` {prefix, render, drop, click, act / doAct, close}; `openPack(side?)`, `packSide()`, `refreshPack()`): by default the ground (`ui/ground.ts`, imported last in main.ts: `groundNear()` = `W.pickups` within `GROUND.reach` 3 m as slots `f:i`, a click or a drag into the kit takes it (`takeDrop` for shared drops, `grabPickup` in `world/loot.ts` for loot), a drag from p/k/h onto it = `dropAtFeet`, Pick up all, refreshed every 0.3 s when the signature changes); chests, stashes, trunks, lockers and holds open there too (`ui/transfer.ts` `openTransfer` keeps the online lock, the container side `b:i` with gold and Take all, the backpack's selected item gets Store; closing the backpack unlocks it; E or Esc closes). No hover handlers that change layout: the item tooltip (`#itip`) is the only hover effect.
- Item slots (`ui/slots.ts`): every inventory window renders slots with `slotHTML` and gets drag and drop (pointer events: mouse and touch) from `bindSlots`; a press without movement is a click (quick action). Stack moves are pure functions in `inventory.ts` (`dropStack` merges or swaps, `putSlot`). `Slot.c` is the condition of a worn part (tires keep their wear off the vehicle); such items never stack.
- Item icons and tooltips: `ui/icons.ts` (`itemIcon(k)`: a 24×24 line SVG per item from path helpers `R`/`C`/`E`/`L`/`Z`/`gear` and shared shapes (SACK, CRATE, BARREL, INGOTS...), stroked in `currentColor`; `itemName(k)` = icon + gold name with `data-k` for lists: shops, market, hall, share, stations and the build rows); `slotHTML` shows the icon and `shortName` (prefixes like 'Crate of ' stripped; hidden in the small `#gear` slots) and carries `data-k` / `data-n` / `data-c` instead of a `title`. `ui/itemtip.ts`: `itemTip(k, n, c)` (type `TYPE_NAME`, description, numbers: Blaster / blade stats, `WEAR` slot and armour, the mask and filter, `NOURISH`, `HEAL`, attachment effects worked out by applying them to `BLASTER.base`, hands-only; kg / L per piece and stack, stack size, shop price or the good's base market price, uses), `initItemTips` (main.ts; one `#itip` panel following the mouse over any `[data-k]`, hidden under pointer lock, while dragging, on press or key). `data/uses.ts` `usesOf(k)` (pure, built once from ORDERS, PLANTS, INSTALL_WORK, MENU, STATIONS, FORTIFY, WORKS, farms, plant levels, installation stages, Chariot STAGES, repairs, bridges, piers, boats) grouped by `USE_GROUPS`. New items need an entry in `P` (tests/icons.test.ts checks every item has one).
- Guns (`data/weapons.ts`, pure): `GunSpec` base stats (damage, interval, range, magazine, reload, zoom) and attachment slots (optic, barrel, magazine); `gunStats` applies the fitted attachments (`char.gunMods`, fitted in the backpack). `world/weapons.ts`: magazine + reload (R / RLD, automatic when empty; from the rounds in the backpack, see Ammunition), aiming zoom (right mouse / AIM), scope overlay at 3×+, attachments shown on the held gun. Oskar the blacksmith sells attachments (`ATTACH_PRICE`).
- Ammunition and arms (0.99): `data/weapons.ts` `GUNS` (blaster 'ammoE' with attachments, pistol / smg 'ammo9', shotgun 'ammoS' with `pellets` 7 and `spread`, rifle 'ammoR' with a 3× scope; `gunOf`, `look` for the viewmodel) and `MELEE` (blade, machete, spear (a thrust, narrow arc), sledge: dmg, rate, reach, stamina factor; `meleeOf`); `AMMO` = the round items (type 'ammo'). Every gun keeps its magazine in `char.loaded[gun]` (`G.ammo` mirrors the held one; switching saves and loads it); R (or an empty magazine) reloads from the rounds in the backpack (`reserve`, `takeRounds` in `world/weapons.ts`), none left = no reload. `calcStats` uses the held gun and melee weapon (attachments only on the Blaster). Rounds are found (`rollAmmo` in `world/loot.ts`: chests, ship chests, bandit stashes, bandits, robots, raider trunks, the crash ship's locker 40 cells, fog lockers `R(14)` / `R(16)`) and, with the plans, ordered from the blacksmith (tech 'gunsmith' appended to `TECHS`: rounds from leadbar + chems + scrap/steel, the four guns; 'batteries': 40 cells; 'forging': machete, spear, sledge). `Pickup.n` drops a stack. Old saves (no `loaded`) get `OLD_SAVE_CELLS` 120 cells (overflow into Gridholm's hold). `addItem` splits stacks, all or nothing.
- GPS Tablet (`tablet`, tool, order tech 'radio'): shows the compass strip and `#gps` under it (x, z, altitude, heading, waypoint distance and direction); a right click on the big map (`contextmenu` in `ui/worldmap.ts`) sets `char.waypoint` [x, z] (on it: clears it); drawn on the maps and the compass (`WAYPOINT_C`). Vehicle Repair Kit (`repairkit`, cons, Kuba `PART_PRICE` 90, order tech 'engines'): `useItem` → `useRepairKit` in `world/loot.ts` (the driven vehicle or your nearest within 7 m) → `repairWithKit` in `data/vehicles.ts` (`KIT`: hull +40% of full, engine +30, fitted wheels +25; not a wreck or a missing wheel).
- Armour, packs and exoskeletons (0.100): `WEAR_SLOTS` adds 'pack' and 'frame' (the backpack window's `#gear` grid has 8 wear columns). Three armour sets, each head/body/gloves/legs/feet in `WEAR`: hide (`hideCap`…, basic orders from hides), woven (`wovenHood`…, tech 'weaving': cloth + wire), composite (`compHelm`… `compVest` 30%, tech 'composites': plastic + alloy + cloth, the vest also ceramics). `PACK_VOL` (rucksack 55 basic, framepack 70 'weaving', cargopack 90 'composites') and `EXO` (exoL Salvage Exoframe, tech 'exoframe': +15 / +20 kg, speed ×1.06, stamina ×0.8; exoH Powered Exoskeleton, tech 'automation': +30 / +40 kg, ×1.12, ×0.6); `gearOf(wear)` (pure) → {vol, comfy, max, speed, stamina}; `gear()` / `packVol()` in `character.ts` replace `PACK.vol` / `comfy` / `max` at every runtime check (`PACK` stays the base; `save.ts` migrations use it); `calcStats` multiplies `G.S.speed` and sets `G.S.stamina`, which `spendStamina` / `drainStamina` apply. The backpack refuses taking a pack off while the contents exceed the smaller volume (`move` in `ui/backpack.ts`). Techs 'weaving', 'composites', 'exoframe' appended after 'gunsmith'.
- Tools in the hands (0.101): `HAND_TOOLS` in `data/items.ts` (hatchet, pickaxe, hammer, saw, screwdriver, pliers, welder, torch, shovel) work only from `char.hands` (`inHands(k)` in `character.ts`); instruments (compass, scanner, tablet) still work carried. `gatherTarget` finds trees / rocks for a tool carried anywhere and sets `Target.held`; without it held, E (`strike`) calls `takeInHands(k)` (from the backpack; the held item goes to the back or the backpack via `stowHeld`) and `gatherPrompt` says so; `restPose` in `world/gather.ts` draws any held hand tool in front of the view (solid models from `world/toolmodels.ts` `toolModel(k, wood, steel)`: grip at the origin, handle along −z, fill + edges; `lyingTool` lays them on the ground for `world/pickmodels.ts`) (`setToolRule` from `world/level.ts`: not driving, boating, swimming or climbing; `carryVM` skips them). The backpack offers Take in hands for hand tools (a held tool swaps out). Building (`lacks` / `dismantle` in `world/building.ts`): a part's first tool and the `cut` tool must be in the hands, the rest carried. Recipe `tools` at a bench still count from the backpack (crafting is frozen).
- Vehicle service (`ui/service.ts`): slots for each wheel's tire, the engine (drop Engine Parts to repair), 2 engine upgrade slots (`ENGINE_UPGRADES`: Turbocharger, Engine Guard; `engineBoost`, `hurtEngine`), the roof mount (cannon) and the hull (drop Hull Plating), plus the backpack. Kuba buys used parts back scaled by their condition.
- Compass and map boards: the Compass (`compass`, a 'tool'; Zofia's store sells it, bench recipe scrap + circuit) shows `ui/compass.ts` (canvas strip at the top, only on the surface while carried): heading, the nearest village more than 150 m away and quest markers (`bearingTo`, `point8`, `fmtDist`). Every village has a map board (`VillageMap.mapBoard`, drawn by `mapBoardDeco` in `world/overworld.ts`); E opens `ui/areamap.ts` `openAreaMap`: 900 m around the village (terrain, lakes, roads, POIs, the 14 nearest named), edge arrows to the 3 nearest other villages, a list with distance/direction; reading it `discover`s the POI chunks.
- **Building outside the villages is closed for now** (`BASES_OPEN = false` in `data/building.ts`): no new flag (`useItem` flagpole), no build list (B), no Workbench Kit set up in the wilds (its recipe is hidden), `stockFor` leaves out `BASE_ITEMS` (flagpole, turret kit, code lock); existing claims, parts, doors, locks and turrets keep working. The direction instead: the hero helps fortify villages (better walls etc.) and has a house in town; other players can live in the same town or pick another. The claim/building code below stays for reuse.
- Land claims (the first step towards player bases): a Flagpole (`flagpole`, Zofia sells it; later a workbench recipe) is used from the backpack → placing mode in `world/claims.ts` (`startPlacing`, `updatePlacing` from the main loop instead of `attack`; click/E confirms, right mouse/Esc cancels): a hologram (depthTest off) with the pole, the levelled floor grid, cut/fill posts, the blend spokes and the dashed claim border; red with the reason from `claimProblem` (pure, `gen/claims.ts`: villages 150 m, places, roads, water, steep ground `MAX_CUT`, the ice, other claims). `char.claims` `{x, z, y, t}` (y = `padHeight`, mean ground to 0.5 m). The terrain takes them via `Terrain.setClaims` (clears its caches; `Features.claims`, `claimFlatten` after pads, before lakes): flat `CLAIM.flat` 12 m, blend 12 m (`CLEAR_R`), claim radius `CLAIM.r` 30 m. `buildChunk` leaves out trees, rocks and plants within `CLEAR_R` (`Terrain.claimAt`); `rebuildChunksNear` redraws the ground. E at the flag (`nearFlag`) takes it down. HUD "Your claim", compass and map markers. Not a safe zone. Next: building parts on the claim, owner-only doors/dismantling, raiding with explosives, turrets.
- Hands, back and wear (`char.hands` 1 slot, `char.back` 2 slots, `char.wear` per `WearSlot`): weapons are items (`blaster`, `blade`, type 'weapon', `WEAPON_KIND` → `G.weapon` 0/1, -1 when the hands are empty or full); `world/weapons.ts` `syncHeld` (hooked via `onHandsChanged`; call `handsChanged()` after touching `char.hands`), `drawBack(i)` (keys 1/2), `swapWeapon` (Q/wheel), `holster` (X). `HANDS_ONLY` items (wheels, cannon, flagpole, workbench kit) never enter the backpack (`roomFor` gives 0 under a cap); `addItem` puts them in the hands (the held weapon is stowed by `stowHeld`: back, else backpack), `takeOne`/`hasItem` also look in the hands, crafting delivers them to the hands (`craft(..., hands)`). Slot ids in windows: `h:0` hands, `k:i` back, `g:i` wear (`WEAR_SLOTS`), `listOf(w)` in character.ts; the backpack, transfer and service windows show them. Armour: `WEAR[k].def`, combined in `G.S.def`, applied by `armoured(dmg)` at every combat hit on the player. `carriedKg()` = backpack + hands + back + wear (the load). Oskar sells weapons and gear (`GEAR_PRICE`) and buys them back. Old saves get the Blaster in hand and the Blade on the back (`newChar` defaults).
- Building (on a claim): `data/building.ts` `PIECES` (wallW/doorW/roofW, wallM/doorM/roofM: `needs` materials used up, `tools` that stay in the backpack, `cut` = the tool to take it down) and `BUILD` (2 m cells, walls 2.8 m, doors 1.1 x 2.2 m). `gen/base.ts` (pure, claim-local u/v metres round the flag): `Part {k, gx, gz, d, open?}` (walls/doors on cell edges, d 0 = along u, 1 = along v; roofs on cells), `snap`, `partProblem` (within `CLAIM.flat`, not through the flagpole, one part per slot via `slotKey`), `solidSegs` / `baseHitLocal` (walls, door jambs, shut door leaves), `refund` (half). Saved as `char.claims[i].parts`. `world/building.ts`: PropBatch models per claim (planks/seams lines), `baseHit` in `G.obstacle` and in the creature/robot/bandit `forbidden` hook, doors (`nearDoor`, `toggleDoor`, E), build mode (`startBuilding` from the B list `ui/build.ts`, `updateBuilding` in the main loop like flag placing: hologram, click = `placePart`, F = `dismantle`, right mouse / Esc = `stopBuilding`). Tools: hammer, saw, screwdriver, pliers, welder, torch, shovel (Oskar, `TOOL_PRICE`); supplies nails, rope, wire, code locks (Zofia, `SUPPLY_PRICE`); planks from logs with a saw (`Recipe.tools`).
- Storeys and stairs: `Part.lv` (storey; old roofs default to 1, `lvOf`), `BUILD.storey` 3 m (2.8 m wall + 0.2 m slab), `floorH(lv)`; 'roof' = floor slab at the top of storey lv-1; stairs (`d` 0..3 = +u,+v,-u,-v, two cells, `cellsOf`, `stairDir` from the yaw) climb one storey. Support rules in `partProblem`; `dismantle` refuses parts something above rests on. Physics hooks set in `openWorld`: `G.floor` = `baseFloor` (slab tops and stair ramps within `STEP_UP`; `moveAxis` skips the slope limit for them, `settleOnGround` lands on floors you were above), `G.rayBlock` = `baseRay` (in `rayWorld`: every shot and every enemy sight line; bolts also check their step so they cannot skip thin walls), `G.solid` = `baseSolid` (in `emptyAt`). Slab boxes only stop you from below (`Box.slab`). Boxes cached per claim, invalidated by `redraw` (`rev`).
- Code locks (`codelock` item, `Part.lock {code, on, auth[]}`, `char.pid`): L at a door (`lockMenu`) fits one (keypad `ui/keypad.ts`, 4 digits), changes the code (auth reset), locks/unlocks, takes it off; E at a locked door you are not authorised for asks for the code (`toggleDoor`). Locked doors cannot be taken down without the code. Not yet: explosives/raiding, floors on the ground storey, windows.
- Auto turrets: part `turret` (shape 'turret', one cell, shares the cell slot with stairs; on the pad or on a slab of its storey; needs `turretkit` (Oskar, `TOOL_PRICE`) + 2 wire, screwdriver + pliers; `PieceSpec.refund` gives the kit back; solid plinth box). `world/turrets.ts` (runtime, unsaved): `syncTurrets` keeps a turning head per turret near the player, `updateTurrets` (main loop, while playing) picks the nearest hostile `foes()` entry (not calm Brambles) within `TURRET.range` with `rayWorld` line of sight, turns at `TURRET.turn`, fires hitscan via `damageFoe` every `TURRET.rate` s with `makeNoise`. `Part.off` = switched off (E, `nearTurret` / `toggleTurret`). Not yet: turret health, ammo or power, targeting settings.
- Multiplayer stage 1 (0.103): `server/mp.mjs` (plain JS, `ws`): a relay, `createMp(log)` → `{attach(server, path), close, players, state}`; `MP` (path '/mp', port 7777, max 8, rate 100 ms), `PROTOCOL` 1 (must match `net/client.ts`). The first player to say hello hosts: their world seed and clock are everyone's (`welcome`); the host's `state` carries `time`; `snap` (everyone but you) every 100 ms with 2+ players; `chat` cleaned; the host leaving hands over to the longest connected. `vite.config.ts` plugin `multiplayer()` attaches it to the dev and preview http servers at /mp (so `start-gry.bat`, now with `--host` and the LAN addresses printed, hosts it); `npm run server` runs it alone. `src/net/client.ts` (no three.js / DOM): `net` {ws, id, host, peers, address}, `connect` / `disconnect` / `sendState` / `sendChat`, `serverUrl`, `NetHooks`. `world/peers.ts`: `updatePeers(dt, moving)` (main loop: sends `PeerState` every `SEND_EVERY`, draws a `makeFigure` per peer in the same `myLoc()` ('o' or 'd:'+dungeonKey) eased between snapshots, kit from the held item, name label, `peerColor`), `clearPeers`. `ui/mp.ts`: the menu's `#mpBox` (Host a game / Join / Leave / Back to my own world), a joiner in another world gets their save copied to `SAVE_KEY + '.solo'` and `switchWorld` (main.ts) to the host's; `#chatlog`, `#chatIn` (T, `G.chatOpen` in `uiOpen`), `#mpBadge`; `mpLocked` stops the menu changing the world while online. Not shared yet: enemies, loot, chests, villages, vehicles, boats (each player simulates their own). Dedicated server (0.104): `server/main.mjs` (`npm run serve`, after `npm run build`) serves `dist/` (static, no path escape, unknown paths → index.html, hashed assets cached) and the relay at /mp on one port (`PORT` 8517, `HOST`, `WORLD_SEED` read on the first start (`FORCE_SEED=1` to change), `DATA_DIR` server/data with `server.json` {world, time} saved every 30 s and on SIGTERM/SIGINT, `SERVER_NAME`); `GET /mp/info` → {dedicated, name, world, time, online, max, players, version}. `createMp(log, {world, time})` = dedicated: the world fixed, `hostId` 0, the clock `time0` + real seconds (always running), the hello's world / time and the state's time ignored, snaps (with the clock) from 1 player; `welcome.dedicated`. Client: `net.dedicated`, `serverInfo()` (fetch 'mp/info' next to the page); `ui/mp.ts` on a dedicated page hides Host and the address, shows the server's name / who is online (polled every 10 s in the menu) and 'Join the server'; `VITE_MP_SERVER` (build time) prefills the address. Behind a portal at a sub-path: `serverUrl('', location)` resolves 'mp' next to the page, the relay takes upgrades on any path ending in /mp, /mp/info likewise, and a static path that is not found is retried without its first part (a proxy that does not strip /gridworld). Deploy: `deploy/install.sh` (Ubuntu / Debian as root, beside other apps: its own Node 22 in `<DIR>-node`, clone to /opt/gridworld, build, user and systemd unit `gridworld`, refuses a taken `PORT` (8517) or a foreign `DIR`, changes no web server, proxy or firewall, prints an nginx `location` example; rerun = update), `Dockerfile` (+ `.dockerignore`, volume /app/server/data), the owner's Polish guide `SERWER.md`. `ws` is a runtime dependency. Server list (0.106, `PROTOCOL` 2): `createMp(log, {rooms})` holds several game servers (rooms `{id, name, world, time0, run, since, players, hostId, created, last}`; 'main' first, named `SERVER_NAME`); each room has its own world, players, snaps and clock (`clock(room)` = time0 + the run time; `occupied` starts it with the first player and stops it with the last: an empty room is paused). hello `{room?, create?: {name, world}}` (`pickRoom`: a name is required and unique, at most `MP.rooms` 12, an unknown room or another protocol is refused with a reason); `welcome.room`; `list()` → `[{id, name, world, time, online, max, running, players, created, last}]`, served in `/mp/info` `rooms` and saved by main.mjs as `server.json` `{rooms}` (the older `{world, time}` becomes 'main'); empty rooms are forgotten after `MP.roomTtl` 14 days (never 'main'). Hosted mode (the dev server) is one room 'lan' as before. Client: `connect(url, {..., room, create})`, `net.room`; `ui/mp.ts` `roomRows()` lists `#mpRooms` (name, ● n/8 playing or ○ paused, who, world, Join / Move here; the current one `.here`, refreshed every 5 s while the menu is up) and `#mpNew` (name, 'in my own world' `#mpMine`, Create a server). Away: `PeerState.away` (= `!G.playing`; `updatePeers` runs in the menu too), so a player in the menu stays in the world, labelled '(in menu)', and the room keeps running. Shared vehicles (0.106.1): `PeerState.cars` (`PeerCar` [model 0/1, x, y, z, heading, pitch, roll, cannon, driven], from `myCars()` in `world/vehicles.ts`: your own vehicles, not AI ones; the server keeps at most `MP.cars` 8); `world/peers.ts` draws each as a `convoyModel` ghost (`syncCars`, key peer:index, the driver a `seatFigure` in the peer's colour while driven; the peer's standing figure is hidden then, only its label stays), `peerCarHit` (in `G.obstacle` and the vehicles' `blocked` in `world/overworld.ts`). Smoothing: `Peer.hist` (the last 8 snapshots with arrival times), `peerAt(p, t)` samples it at `now − PEER_DELAY` 220 ms (holding the end ones outside the buffer). Not shared yet: boats, trunks, damage. Dropped items (0.107): the backpack's Drop (`ui/backpack.ts`) lays the stack at your feet via `dropAtFeet` in `world/drops.ts` instead of deleting it; such pickups have `Pickup.drop` (id), `c` (condition), `by` and are taken with E only (`nearDrop` / `dropPrompt` / `takeDrop` in `world/interact.ts`; `updateLoot` skips them). Online the server owns them: client {t:'drop', k, n, c, p, loc} → the room's `drops` Map, broadcast {t:'drop', d} to all (the dropper too); {t:'take', id} → {t:'got', d} to the first asker (then `give` into the kit; no room → dropped again), {t:'gone', id} to the rest; `welcome.drops`; `MP.drops` 300 a room (the oldest goes), `MP.dropTtl` 6 h (swept every minute), a hosted room's drops clear when it empties; `mp.save()` (rooms with their drops) is what server/main.mjs writes to server.json. Client: `NetDrop`, `net.drops`, `sendDrop` / `sendTake` / `onGot`. Alone they live in a runtime map (`local`, cleared by `switchWorld`). `syncDrops` (main loop, 4 times a second) draws the drops of `myLoc()` (`dropPickup` now returns the pickup) and removes the gone ones. Riding together (0.108): `driving.seat` (0 driver, `SEAT_NAMES`, `gunSeat(m)`); `switchSeat(i)` in `world/vehicles.ts` (keys 1 / 2 / 3 in `ui/input.ts` while in a vehicle, else they draw back weapons); away from the wheel `updateDriving` takes no input and brakes; `myCannon(v)` (the gunner's seat, or the wheel while `Vehicle.gunYaw` is unset) gates `fireCannon` and `aimTurret`; `shootCannon(turret)` is the shot itself; bandits aim at and hurt `driving.seat`. `PeerCar` [8] = the owner's seat + 1 (0 = not in it; older clients 1 = driving), [9] the cannon's yaw. `PeerState.ride` [owner id, index in their `myCarList()`, seat] and `gun` (the cannon's yaw while at it); the server passes both on. `world/peers.ts`: `ride` {on, gun, cockpit} (your own ride), `Ghost` (owner, idx, `turret`, `occ` 'p<id>' / 'me', `bumped`, seat figures per seat in the riders' colours, the gunner turning with the cannon), `seatsFor` (the owner first, then riders by id; the wheel is never a rider's; losers are `bumped`), `ghostOf` / `allGhosts`; `syncMyRiders` seats the players riding in your own vehicles (`seatRider` 'peer:<id>') and sets `gunYaw` from the one at your cannon; a riding peer's standing figure is hidden. `world/ride.ts`: `rideSpot` (on foot at a ghost's door, the first free seat: passenger, then gunner if it has a cannon) / `ridePrompt` / `board` (E in `world/interact.ts`), `updateRide` (instead of `updatePlayer` in main.ts: G.pos at the vehicle, a bumped rider moves on or `getOff`, the gunner aims `ride.gun` with the view and fires `shootCannon` with its own cooldown, the HUD line), `rideCamera` (chase or the seat's view, V `toggleRideView`), `rideSeat` (1 / 2 / 3; not the wheel), `getOff` (E, beside the door; also on loading another place). The armed and tool rules, `attack` and `updateGun` are off while riding. Not yet: driving another player's vehicle, riding in boats. The shared world (0.109): `world/share.ts` `SHARED` = the save's world fields (towns, market, installs, bridges as maps; bridgeSites, piers, boats as lists by `id`; shuttle as one value '_'; containers but 'home:*'; opened, unlocked, killed, harvest, camps, caravans); `worldDoc` ({field: {key: value}}), `syncWorld` (main loop, once a second while online: JSON of each key vs `last`, changes and deletions → `sendWorld` [[field, key, value|null]]), the others' changes (`onWorld` hooks from net/client.ts) go in place (`put` / `patchInto`, so references held elsewhere stay valid) and are shown by `show` (a village's look (`townLook`: wall, works, farms, crops, plants, stations, levels, improvements) changed → `reloadStruct`; the Chariot → the hangar; a felled tree or broken rock → `rebuildChunkAt`; the rest redraws from the save itself). Joining: the welcome carries `wdoc` / `wseeded`; `joinWorld()` (ui/mp.ts, after the solo backup, before `switchWorld` or `reloadWorld` from main.ts) seeds the room with your world (`seedWorld`) if nobody has and you already play its world, else adopts the room's (`adopt`). Server: `room.doc` / `seeded` / `dirty`; {t:'wset', ch} applied and broadcast to the others, {t:'wseed'} accepted once (later ones get {t:'wdoc'}), `MP.payload` 4 MB; `dirtyDocs()` → server/main.mjs writes `DATA_DIR/world-<room>.json` (loaded back at start); a hosted room's doc clears when it empties. Last write per key wins. Shared foes (0.111): every creature, robot and bandit is run by one game, its owner; `world/remote.ts` (no loops): `markRemote` / `remoteOf` / `unmarkRemote` (a WeakMap of copies: owner, nid, the owner's position, heading, hp), `stepRemote` (the update loops of creatures, robots and bandits ease a copy to its owner's word and skip its mind and the distance cull), `withTarget(x, z, fn)` (the owner's foe's turn runs against the nearest player: `G.pos` is set to that peer for the turn, `proxied()` is true (then `armoured` and `foeRules.shielded` ignore yours), and the HP it took is sent to them, reckoned with their armour), `spawnAuthority` (alone, or no non-away player with a lower id in your `myLoc()` within `FOESYNC.lead` 220 m: `mayspawn`, `updateGarrisons`, `spawnCamp`, the wreck's `spawnGuards`, the live village raid start are refused otherwise), `otherPlayers` / `hurtOther` (bandit bolts hit the others' bodies, artillery blasts reach them), `boltOut` / `hitOwner`. `world/foesync.ts` (`syncFoes` in main.ts every frame): `FOESYNC.every` 0.1 s while anyone else is in your place, `{t:'foes', loc, list}` with `[nid, type 0 creature / 1 robot / 2 bandit, kind/model/role, x, y, z, heading, hp, maxHp, level, state]` and dead ones as `[nid, -1]` for 1 s; copies made by `spawnRemoteCreature` / `spawnRemoteRobot` (ignores `forbidden`) / `spawnBandit`, a dead entry plays the death (`fallCreature` / `wreckRobot` / `fallBandit`), missing = removed, an owner silent `quiet` 2.5 s, gone or elsewhere = all theirs dropped. `damageFoe` on a copy flashes it and sends `fhit` to the owner, who runs `damageFoe` and answers `kill` if it died; the shooter then unmarks its copy and kills it as their own (loot, xp, bounties; the owner's game drops its own loot too). `hurt` (their foe hit you: armour, a closed cab and the safe zones apply here), `bolt` (drawn by `ghostBolt`, harmless). Relay: client `relay(m, to?)` → server `{t:'cast', m}` (the rest of the room) / `{t:'to', to, m}` (one player) → `{...m, from}`, `m.t` only from `RELAY` ('foes', 'bolt', 'fhit', 'kill', 'hurt'); `onRelay`. Everything shared (0.134, `PROTOCOL` 8): `world/foesync.ts` types 0 creature, 1 robot, 2 bandit, 3 drone, 4 boss, 5 raider (`spawnRemoteRaider`, `placeAI` in `world/vehicles.ts` eases the copy, `RemoteFoe.aux` = cannon yaw; a remote kill: the owner wrecks it under `quietLoot`, the shooter gets `remoteRaiderKilled`), 6 barricade (`barricades`, `placeBarricadeCopy`, `removeBarricade`; `hurtBarrier` on a copy goes to its owner); a dungeon's drones and bosses are only the authority's (`tell` skips them otherwise; a non-authority drops its own drones, marks its local bosses as copies by `idx`); `stepRemoteP` for position-only copies; `fhit` runs `damageFoe(..., false)` under `quietLoot` (`world/loot.ts`: no loot, crystals or rewards: `lootQuiet` in `killBoss`, drones, `wreckRaider`) and answers `kill`; owners leaving, going silent or elsewhere hand their foes over (`dropOwner(id, true)`: unmarked and kept, `adopted` re-links them if the owner speaks again); culls use `nearestPlayer` (`world/remote.ts`). Mounted turrets run through `withTarget` (only `spawnAuthority` fires; shots `boltOut`), hits relay as `thit` (`setTurretHook`, `remoteTurretHit`). Ground loot: online, `dropPickup` (`setGroundHook`, hooked on the first `syncDrops`) sends a server drop with `auto` (quest items stay local); `drawPickup` draws; `Pickup.auto` drops are taken like any other (E or the ground panel, since 0.156); server `MP.lootTtl` 2 h, `MP.drops` 800. `world/share.ts` also shares `claims` and `benches` (lists keyed by position, `Field.key`), `board` (one, `EMPTY.board`) and `boards` (map); `claimsChanged` in `world/claims.ts` (terrain + `redraw` from `world/building.ts`); server `FIELDS`. Not synced: villagers' walks, a caravan's halt in a raid, a raider wreck's claim (the owner's game).
- `data/` — items, NPC texts, vehicles. `save.ts` — persistence and version migrations.
